---

### PART IX: COMMON ALCHEMICAL REAGENTS TABLE

The following table covers substances alchemists commonly use in processing operations — not as structural materials, but as agents applied to other materials during synthesis, decomposition, and displacement operations.

| Reagent | Chemical Tags | Primary Use | Hazard Profile | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Sulfuric acid | Corrosive, Caustic | Decomposition (dissolution), refining | Contact burn, fume inhalation | Universal industrial acid; dissolves most metals |
| Hydrochloric acid | Corrosive, Caustic, Volatile | Decomposition, cleaning, etching | Contact burn, toxic fumes | Reacts violently with bases |
| Nitric acid | Corrosive, Caustic, Volatile | Decomposition, testing metals, etching | Contact burn, toxic fumes | Distinguishes gold from base metals |
| Aqua regia | Corrosive, Caustic, Volatile | Decomposition of noble metals (gold, platinum) | Extreme contact hazard, toxic fumes | One of few reagents that dissolves Inert metals |
| Lye (sodium hydroxide) | Caustic, Reactive | Processing leather, soapmaking, decomposition of organic matter | Severe tissue damage | Strong base; used in tanning and cleaning |
| Salt (NaCl) | Inert (solid), Reactive (in solution) | Preservation, displacement reactions, curing | Minimal | Universal preservative; Synod-adjacent process |
| Charcoal (carbon) | Reactive | Synthesis (steel production), reduction of ores, filtration | Minimal (combustible) | Essential for smelting; the carbon in steel |
| Quicklime (CaO) | Caustic, Reactive | Processing stone, morite production, tanning | Contact burn, heat generation when wet | Exothermic reaction with water |
| Alcohol (ethanol) | Volatile, Reactive | Solvent, tincture base, preservation, fuel | Flammable, mild toxicity | Universal alchemical solvent |
| Mercury | Volatile, Toxic | Historical amalgamation, displacement of metals | Severe cumulative toxicity | Useful but fundamentally dangerous |
| Sulfur | Volatile, Reactive | Fumigation, gunpowder component, vulcanization | Toxic fumes when burned | Borderlands Fire affinity |
| Saltpeter (KNO₃) | Reactive, Volatile (in compounds) | Gunpowder component, preservation, fertilizer | Low hazard alone, explosive in mixture | Oxidizer — provides oxygen to combustion reactions |
| Phosphorus | Volatile, Reactive, Toxic | Ignition sources, reagent in synthesis | Spontaneous combustion (white), toxic | White phosphorus is extremely dangerous |
| Flux (borax, limestone) | Reactive | Smelting — removes impurities as slag | Minimal | Essential for refining operations |
| Tannins (plant-derived) | Reactive | Leather processing — converts hide to leather | Minimal | The Arrested heritage state of leather is created by tanning |
| Oils (mineral and plant) | Reactive (some), Volatile (some) | Lubrication, fuel, preservation, solvent | Flammable (most) | Range of properties depending on source |

---

*End of Section II. Section III: Item Architecture — The Five Layers follows.*

# THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX — System Reference Document
## Material Quality Table — Formation Stage as Quality & Capacity

*Alpha SRD Draft — Working Document*

---

### THE QUALITY PRINCIPLE

Formation Stage for a base material measures the quality of the sample — the purity, structural coherence, and physical perfection of the substance. A higher Formation Stage means a better specimen: fewer impurities, more consistent molecular or crystalline structure, sharper natural geometry, and greater capacity to support planar expressions.

Formation Stage directly caps the Effective Mastery Level (EML) that the material can support. A Stage 3 material can hold mutations and effects up to EML 3. A mutation that would naturally express at EML 6 in an appropriate medium is bottlenecked to EML 3 in a Stage 3 substrate — the medium cannot sustain the full intensity. Upgrading the material's quality through purification, refining, or reprocessing raises the Formation Stage and therefore raises the cap, potentially unlocking expression that was previously bottlenecked.

For planar mutations occurring in the material, the mutation's own intensity is assessed independently but is expressed through the substrate at whatever level the substrate can support. The mutation "wants" to express at its full intensity. The material determines how much of that intensity actually manifests.

---

### THE QUALITY TABLE

| Formation Stage | Quality Descriptor | Physical Character | EML Cap | Crafting Band | Acquisition |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Trace** | Heavily contaminated or degraded. Significant impurities, structural flaws, visible defects. Barely functional for its intended purpose. A rusted iron scrap. Obsidian with heavy inclusions and fracture lines. Bone riddled with microfractures. | EML 1 | Novice | Common — found everywhere, often as waste or byproduct |
| 2 | **Poor** | Functional but clearly flawed. Noticeable impurities or structural inconsistencies. Works for basic purposes but underperforms relative to the material's potential. A rough iron ingot with slag inclusions. Obsidian with cloudy patches. Green wood not yet seasoned. | EML 2 | Novice | Common — standard low-grade commercial stock |
| 3 | **Common** | Standard working quality. Minor impurities present but not functionally significant. The baseline for most crafted goods. What you get from a competent supplier without special requests. Standard iron bar. Clear obsidian with minor inclusions. Properly seasoned heartwood. | EML 3 | Novice | Common — standard commercial quality |
| 4 | **Clean** | Good quality. Impurities minimal and evenly distributed. Reliable performance across the material's expected applications. The upper end of what an apprentice works with. Well-smelted steel. Clean obsidian with consistent fracture. Dense, straight-grained wood. | EML 4 | Novice (upper) | Available — may require sourcing from a quality supplier |
| 5 | **Fine** | High purity. Consistent structure throughout the sample. Professional-grade material that performs at the upper range of its type. The threshold where real expertise is needed to work the material without wasting it. Pattern-welded steel. Flawless obsidian. Old-growth heartwood with tight grain. | EML 5 | Journeyman | Uncommon — requires specific sourcing, higher cost |
| 6 | **Superior** | Excellent quality. Negligible impurities. The material expresses its natural properties at near-optimal levels. A meaningful step above what most practitioners encounter. Crucible steel with controlled carbon distribution. Museum-quality obsidian. Century heartwood. | EML 6 | Journeyman | Uncommon to Rare — may require questing or specialist contacts |
| 7 | **Exceptional** | Near-perfect. The material's properties are expressed at levels that surprise experienced practitioners. Specimens at this quality are noteworthy — people remember handling them. Damascus steel with visible crystalline patterning. Obsidian with natural iridescence from perfect cooling. Ironwood-grade heartwood. | EML 7 | Journeyman (advanced) | Rare — specific sources, significant effort to obtain |
| 8 | **Pristine** | Effectively flawless for all practical purposes. The material performs at the theoretical maximum for its type under normal conditions. Working it requires mastery because any error wastes an irreplaceable sample. Wootz steel. Obsidian that fractures to a monomolecular edge. Petrified wood with complete mineral replacement. | EML 8 | Master | Rare to Very Rare — legendary sources, quest-worthy |
| 9 | **Perfect** | Theoretical perfection achieved in physical form. No measurable impurities. Structure is ideal at every scale. Materials at this quality are historical — they get names. The specific billet of steel a legendary blade was forged from. The single piece of obsidian that a civilization's sacred knife was knapped from. | EML 9 | Master | Very Rare — generational finds, divine providence, planar sources |
| 10 | **Absolute** | The conceptual ceiling. The material exists at the maximum possible expression of what that substance can be while remaining a physical material. A Stage 10 sample is what that material looks like in the mind of the god who conceived of it. One may not exist in the current age. | EML 10 | Master (peak) | Unique — may require planar travel, divine intervention, or discovery of pre-Calamity materials |

---

### QUALITY EXAMPLES BY MATERIAL

The same Formation Stage means different things for different materials. Quality is relative to what the substance is capable of.

**Iron / Steel**

| Stage | What It Looks Like |
|---|---|
| 1 | Bog iron. Spongy, full of slag and carbon pockets. Barely holds an edge. |
| 3 | Standard smithy iron. Serviceable. Makes nails, horseshoes, common tools. |
| 5 | Well-smelted steel. Clean carbon distribution. Makes reliable weapons and armor. |
| 7 | Pattern-welded or crucible steel. Visible quality in the grain. Named swords are made from this. |
| 9 | Wootz/Damascus at its historical peak. Self-organizing carbon nanostructures. Edges that cut silk in the air. |

**Obsidian**

| Stage | What It Looks Like |
|---|---|
| 1 | Fractured chunks with heavy mineral inclusions. Dull, opaque, fractures unevenly. |
| 3 | Standard knapping-grade obsidian. Clean enough for tools and arrowheads. |
| 5 | Jewelry-grade. Translucent, even coloring, predictable conchoidal fracture. |
| 7 | Display-grade with natural iridescence (rainbow obsidian) or perfect transparency (Obsidian of Jalisco). |
| 9 | Monomolecular fracture edge. Surgical-grade. Used in real-world eye surgery because it's sharper than steel scalpels. |

**Bone**

| Stage | What It Looks Like |
|---|---|
| 1 | Weathered fragments. Porous, brittle, partially mineralized. |
| 3 | Fresh clean bone from a healthy animal. Standard material for handles, tools, divination implements. |
| 5 | Dense cortical bone from a large, healthy specimen. Consistent structure. Good material for carved items. |
| 7 | Fossil bone with complete mineral replacement maintaining original structure. Ancient, dense, resonant. |
| 9 | Dragonbone, or equivalent — bone from a creature whose biological processes produced a material beyond normal biological limits. |

**Heartwood**

| Stage | What It Looks Like |
|---|---|
| 1 | Punky, partially rotted. Loose grain. Would not survive carving. |
| 3 | Standard lumber. Straight grain, properly seasoned. Makes furniture, handles, building material. |
| 5 | Old-growth heartwood. Tight rings, dense, resonant. Staff and instrument grade. |
| 7 | Ironwood equivalent. Density approaching stone. Sinks in water. Holds an edge like soft metal. |
| 9 | Petrified wood with complete mineral replacement maintaining wood grain structure — or living wood from a Verdant Font bleed zone that has grown for centuries without disease or damage. |

---

### BOTTLENECKING AND UPGRADING

**Bottlenecking:** When a mutation's natural intensity exceeds the substrate's Formation Stage, the mutation is bottlenecked — it expresses only at the level the material can support. A V-13 Cancer mutation with inherent intensity equivalent to EML 6, embedded in a Stage 3 obsidian substrate, expresses at EML 3. The mutation is "there" at full potential, but the medium limits the output.

A bottlenecked mutation may exhibit signs of strain — the expression pushing against the substrate's limits. In the Cancer blade example, a bottlenecked Cancer might produce erratic growth patterns, unpredictable shedding, or localized stress fractures where the mutation is trying to express more intensely than the glass can sustain. This strain is a signal to the Analyzer that the mutation is being suppressed by substrate quality.

**Upgrading:** Purification, refining, and reprocessing operations raise the Formation Stage by improving the material's quality. Each stage of improvement is a crafting operation with its own Artifice check against a TN determined by the current quality and the material's chemical behavior.

As Formation Stage rises, previously bottlenecked mutations unlock additional intensity. Upgrading the Cancer blade's obsidian from Stage 3 to Stage 5 would release the Cancer expression from EML 3 to EML 5 — and the blade's behavior would change immediately as the mutation expresses more fully. The growth becomes more aggressive, the shedding more frequent, the seeding on contact more likely.

This means material upgrading is a form of indirect power growth — the practitioner isn't adding new capabilities, they're removing limitations on capabilities that were always latently present. The mutation was always Stage 6 Cancer. The obsidian just couldn't handle it.

**Limits of Upgrading:** A material can only be refined to the upper limit of what that substance can be. You cannot refine bog iron past what iron is capable of. Stage 10 iron may be theoretically perfect iron, but it's still iron — it has iron's physical ceiling. To exceed that ceiling, you need a different material (steel, mithril, a planar metal) or mutations that alter the physical properties beyond the base substance's natural limits.

---

### VOLATILITY AS A COMBINED STATE PROPERTY

Volatility is assessed on the combined state of the material, not on its individual constituents in isolation. The same element can be extremely volatile in pure form and completely stable as part of a compound.

**Example:** Pure potassium is violently reactive — it combusts on contact with water. Potassium ions distributed through a banana's complex organic matrix are completely stable. The potassium hasn't changed. The composition has changed, and the composition determines the volatility.

This principle applies in both directions during crafting:

- **Concentrating** a reactive element (extracting potassium from plant ash, purifying elemental sulfur from ore, distilling alcohol from fermented liquid) **increases** volatility because you're isolating a reactive substance from the matrix that was stabilizing it.
- **Distributing** a reactive element into a stable matrix (alloying a volatile metal into a stable compound, dissolving a dangerous reagent into an inert solvent, incorporating a reactive mineral into a ceramic glaze) **decreases** volatility because the combined state is more stable than the pure component.

The alchemist managing volatility is doing real chemistry — understanding which compositions tame dangerous elements and which ones concentrate them. Volatility management is a core competency, not an edge case.

**Mechanical Implication:** Volatility modifiers on the materials table and reagents table represent the material AS ENCOUNTERED — in the combined state the practitioner will actually be working with. If a processing operation changes the combined state (distillation separates a compound into fractions; alloying combines two metals), the volatility of the resulting products must be reassessed. The GM and practitioner should evaluate what the new combined state's volatility is based on what they've produced.

---

*This table is referenced by Section III (Base Materials & Mutations) and Section V (Item Creation).*

# THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX — System Reference Document
## The Spell Sentence System — The Grammar of Magic

*Alpha SRD — Second Draft*

---

## PREAMBLE

Magic is a living language. The "spells" taught in academies are merely common phrases — recipes proven to be effective and stable. The true master composes. They understand the fundamental grammar of reality and shape it to their will.

The Spell Sentence System is the universal framework governing all magical expressions. It applies equally to practitioners composing rotes, items expressing mutations, alchemists building compounds, and the analysis of ambient planar phenomena. The same grammar operates at every scale.

---

## PART I: THE PRIMA ÆTHER

The source of all magical power in Tessera is the Prima Æther — a pervasive force resulting from the interplay between two fundamentally different magical systems forcibly merged during the Ancient Magical Calamity.

**The Primal Current (Anima's Law):** The world's native magic. Raw, chaotic, intuitive. Akin to alternating current. Practitioners of Innate Magic (Sorcerers, Psions, Sōmaturges) connect to this current.

**The Structured Current (Kanon's Law):** An imposed lattice of magical law. Orderly, systematic, predictable. Akin to direct current. Practitioners of Studied Magic (Wizards, Clerics) tap into this grid.

---

## PART II: THE SPELL SENTENCE

All magical expressions deconstruct into a foundational sentence:

| Component | Role | Function |
|---|---|---|
| **Effect** | Verb | The core action — what the expression fundamentally does |
| **Isotope** | Adjective | The planar character — what nature or quality it carries |
| **Target** | Noun | Who or what is affected — spatial geometry |
| **Range** | Preposition | How far the expression reaches |
| **Duration** | Adverb | How long the expression persists |
| **Additives** | Augmentations | Enhancements making the expression more complex or powerful |
| **Subtractives** | Anchors | Limitations making it easier to control or less costly |

### The Rote

A rote is a complete spell sentence frozen in a specific configuration — a stable, repeatable formula. Different rotes can share the same Effect and Isotope but differ in geometry, duration, and additives. A mutation in an item is a rote that the material expresses as a consequence of its planar nature — the system measures what the expression actually does using the same framework regardless of source.

### Secondary Subtypes (Thematic Descriptors)

Divine values and philosophical alignments (Sovereignty, Predation, Nurture, Expression, Duty, etc.) describe HOW an expression manifests aesthetically. Same damage, same geometry, same isotope — different narrative texture. Important for setting and characterization but do not alter mechanical calculations.

---

## PART III: THE THREE TIERS OF PROFICIENCY

A practitioner's ability is understood through three independent tiers, each rated 1-10 for mortal beings.

**A. Overall Mastery** — Total potential. Governs the absolute ceiling. Determines maximum ÆP expenditure per rote.

**B. Effect Tier** — Deep understanding of a particular Effect (Damage, Affliction, Create, etc.). Rated 1-10 per Effect. Determines raw power (Base Effect Rank) and contributes to pool size.

**C. Isotope Tier** — Fine control over a specific isotope (B-01 Inferno, V-13 Cancer, S-09 Wraith, etc.). Rated 1-10 per learned isotope. Determines accuracy and contributes to pool size.

### The Interlocking Cap

**Effective Spell Level (ESL) = min(Effect Tier, Isotope Tier)**

A practitioner cannot apply more precision than the power they are channeling. Damage 7 + Inferno 5 = ESL 5 maximum.

**Accuracy Cap:** Attack bonus for a targeted expression equals Isotope Tier, capped by ESL.

---

## PART IV: THE TWO POOLS

### The Unified Pool Formula

**Pool Size = (½ Effect Tier) + (½ Isotope Tier) + Overall Mastery + Moiety**

Both pools use this formula. Same size, different spending.

**A. Construction Pool** — Buys geometry: Target shape, Range band, Duration step. Points spent from the Construction Menu.

**B. Complexity Pool** — Buys Additives. Total Additive cost cannot exceed this budget. Subtractives grant bonus points to this pool.

**Architect Bonus:** Studied casters receive a bonus to Complexity Pool equal to ⌊Dominion ÷ 2⌋.

---

## PART V: THE INVERTED FORMULA

For casters, the formula works inside out — stats combine into pools that compose rotes. For items and environmental expressions, it works outside in — an observable effect is decomposed into constituent variables.

### Decomposition

An Analyzer examining an expression extracts:
- **Effect and its level** — what it does and how strongly
- **Isotope and its level** — what planar character and how intensely
- **Substrate contribution** — how the material's physical properties modify the expression (hardness feeding duration, tensile feeding damage, density feeding range, etc.)
- **Interaction effects** — modifications from other expressions in the same combined state

### Item Pool Calculation

**Item Pool = (½ Effect Level) + (½ Isotope Level) + EML + Moiety equivalent**

Effect Level and Isotope Level are intrinsic properties of the expression. EML is the item's current Effective Mastery Level. The pools determine what geometry and complexity the expression can support.

### Extraction and Transplantation

Extracted expressions carry their Effect Level and Isotope Level as intrinsic properties — these belong to the expression, not the substrate. When introduced to a new substrate, the interaction rules (cooperative/competing/cancellation/emergent) apply to the new combined state.

---

## PART VI: THE EFFECTS

Effects are the core verbs of any expression. Effect Tier is rated 1-10 per Effect.

### Offensive Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Damage** | 1 | Direct harm — physical, mental, or metaphysical. Nature defined by Isotope. |
| **Affliction** | 2 | Debilitating conditions. Does not necessarily cause direct harm but leaves targets stunned, paralyzed, confused, restrained, or otherwise impaired. Also covers sensory impairment (blinding, deafening) when imposed as a condition on the target. |
| **Nullify** | 3 | Counter-magic. Counters or suppresses magical effects. Unravels existing expressions. |
| **Weaken** | 3 | Drains core attributes. Saps strength, dulls intellect, diminishes capacity. |

### Defensive Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Protection** | 2 | Field or reinforcement increasing resistance to harm. |
| **Concealment** | 2 or 3 | Sensory denial. Cost 2: single sense. Cost 3: entire sense category. Also covers environmental information degradation — when the SPACE becomes harder to perceive rather than a condition imposed on a target. |
| **Deflect** | 3 | Active interception and diversion of incoming attacks aimed at allies. |
| **Immunity** | 4 | Complete immunity to a specific Isotope or concept. Absolute negation. |
| **Immortality** | 5 | Ensures return from death. Does not prevent death but guarantees resurrection. |

### Movement Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Leaping** | 1 | Prodigious jumps beyond normal capability. |
| **Speed** | 1 | Enhanced ground movement speed. |
| **Swimming** | 1 | Enhanced aquatic movement. Fauna-dominant Font isotopes. |
| **Burrowing** | 1 | Movement through solid ground. Earth-dominant Borderlands and underground-adapted Font expressions. |
| **Wall-Crawling** | 1 | Movement on vertical or inverted surfaces. Adhesion-capable Font isotopes (V-02 Carnivorous). |
| **Flight** | 2 | Aerial movement and hovering. Borderlands Gas (B-13 Void Sky) at moderate+ Formation Stage. |
| **Teleport** | 3 | Instantaneous translocation. Palimpsest Error (P-13) seam-walking. |
| **Dimensional Travel** | 4 | Travel between planes of existence. |
| **Time Travel** | 5 | Temporal manipulation at translocation scale. Palimpsest domain. |

### Healing & Recovery Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Healing** | 2 | Mending wounds, reversing damage, curing conditions. At high Construction Pool investment with appropriate Additives, can address multiple conditions simultaneously for broad-spectrum effect. |
| **Regeneration** | 3 | Automatic recovery over time without repeated application. |

### Sensory Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Senses** | 2 | Enhancing or granting perception modes — darkvision, danger sense, enhanced hearing. |
| **Communication** | 2 | Information transmission over distance — telepathy, magical missives, network signaling. |
| **Comprehend** | 2 | Bypassing barriers of language, species communication, or system logic. Forge domain. |
| **Mind Reading** | 3 | Direct access to surface thoughts and deep memories. |
| **Remote Sensing** | 3 | Displacing senses to a distant location — scrying, clairvoyance. |

### Support & Utility Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Feature** | 0 or 1 | Cost 0: minor cosmetic. Cost 1: minor but mechanically useful utility. |
| **Quickness** | 1 | Temporal compression of routine tasks. Palimpsest domain — the experienced duration is condensed rather than physical speed increasing. |
| **Create** | 2 | Forming solid, non-living objects from Prima Æther. Includes Summon (Construct) — autonomous created entities requiring Forge infrastructure for independent function. |
| **Move Object** | 2 | Telekinesis — moving, lifting, manipulating at distance. |
| **Illusion** | 2 or 3 | False sensory impressions. Cost 2: single sense. Cost 3: multi-sensory. |
| **Enhanced Trait** | 2, 3, or 4 | Attribute enhancement. Cost 2: single. Cost 3: partial group. Cost 4: full suite. |
| **Environment** | 2, 3, or 4 | Area condition alteration. Cost 2: single change. Cost 3: two simultaneous. Cost 4: massive area-wide. Also covers environmental information degradation when the space's perceivability is altered rather than a condition imposed on targets. |
| **Summon (Contract)** | 3 | Calling a specific existing entity through Forge Channeled infrastructure + Communication + Dimensional Travel. The entity is real, independent, and bound by terms. |
| **Transform** | 3 | Altering fundamental properties of an inanimate object — shape or composition. |
| **Luck Control** | 3 | Direct manipulation of probability currents. Subject to Aíon's cosmic arbitration. |
| **Variable** | Rank × 2 | A pool of unrealized potential that can manifest as other Effects temporarily. Multiple isotope homes with different control mechanisms — Casino (random), Dream (narrative), Fey Logic (conviction), Chimera (reactive), Cancer (uncontrolled+dangerous), Borderlands phase transitions (environmental), Mutagen+Forge (directed). Cannot produce Effects the source isotope excludes. |

### Form-Altering Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Elongation** | 1 | Stretching body and limbs. Carnivorous (V-02) tendrils, Chimera (V-08) adaptive reach, Mire (B-07) cohesive extension. |
| **Extra Limbs** | 1 | Growing additional manipulative limbs. Chimera and Mutagen. |
| **Growth / Shrinking** | 2 | Altering physical size in either direction. Growth = greater strength and resilience. Shrinking = smaller and harder to hit. Bidirectional — the isotope determines direction. |
| **Morph** | 2 | Altering physical appearance — features, voice, build. |
| **Insubstantial** | 3 | Assuming less solid form. Dual-homed: Borderlands Gas (physical diffusion into gaseous state — still matter, just dispersed) and Synod Spectral (identity projected without physical body — not matter at all). Similar mechanical output, fundamentally different mechanism. |

---

## PART VII: ISOTOPES AS SUBTYPES

Primary subtypes are individual isotopes learned as talents ranked 1-10. Each isotope's profile specifies which Effects it naturally produces and which it excludes, constraining what rotes can be composed with that isotope.

**Learning Progression:** Pure elements are prerequisites for compounds. Catalyzed diagonals require Forge knowledge. Emulsions require both antithetical planes plus the Forge. Each isotope is a talent costing 6 XP.

**Specialization:** Because each isotope is a separate investment, practitioners naturally specialize. Two Borderlands practitioners with different isotope investments have fundamentally different capability profiles.

---

## PART VIII: CASTING PHILOSOPHIES

### The Architect (Studied Magic)

**Recipe Optimization:** Final Rank reduction by ESL band (−4 / −3 / −2).
**Complexity Mastery:** Bonus to Complexity Pool equal to ⌊Dominion ÷ 2⌋.
**Intellect-Based Metamagic Efficiency:** Each Metamagic surcharge reduced by ⌊Intellect ÷ 2⌋, minimum +1 ÆP per Metamagic.
**Weakness:** Tactical rigidity. Rotes are fixed once designed.

### The Improviser (Innate Magic)

**On-the-Fly Modification:** Freely swap Additives or primary Isotope within a known rote at cast time, within Complexity limits.
**Anima-Based ÆP Reduction:** Per-rote Base ÆP reduced by (Anima − 3), minimum 0. Floors at 0 — casting never generates ÆP.
**Weakness:** No Final Rank reduction. Less efficient foundations.

### The Polymath

A practitioner can learn both philosophies, maintaining separate repertoires. Most versatile, requires investment in both paths.

---

## PART IX: THE CONSTRUCTION MENU

### Base State (Universal Grammar)

Every expression starts at 0 CP: Individual / Touch or Close / Instantaneous. All practitioners inherently know all 0 CP components.

### Targets (Shapes)

| CP | Shape | Description | Access |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | Individual | Single target | Common |
| 0 | Ray | Line of effect attack | Common |
| 0 | Emanation | Aura centered on caster/target | Common |
| 0 | Burst (Small) | Scaling explosion: 10 ft + (5 ft / 2 ML) | Common |
| 1 | Cone | Spray/breath weapon | Common |
| 2 | Spread | Cloud/mist that turns corners. 10-20 ft radius | Common |
| 2 | Line (Area) | Beam hitting all in path | Common |
| 3 | Wall (Standard) | Barrier. 2 squares/ML. Thin | Common |
| 6 | Wall (Fortified) | Barrier. 4 squares/ML. 1 inch/ML thick | Dialect |

### Ranges

| CP | Range | Description | Access |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | Touch / Self | Contact | Common |
| 0 | Close | 25 ft + (5 ft / 2 ML) | Common |
| 1 | Medium | 100 ft + (10 ft / ML) | Common |
| 1 | Line of Sight | Bypasses barriers, not cover | Common |
| 2 | Long | 400 ft + (40 ft / ML) | Common |
| 3 | Visual Sight | Anything you can see | Common |
| 7 | Unlimited | Same plane | Dialect |
| 9 | Extraplanar | Another plane | Treasure |
| 10 | Vast | 20 miles per ML | Treasure |

### Durations

| CP | Duration | Description | Access |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | Instantaneous | Happens and ends | Common |
| 0 | Concentration | Lasts while focused (standard action) | Common |
| 1 | Minutes | 1 min per ML | Common |
| 2 | Hours | 1 hour per ML | Common |
| 3 | Day | 24 hours | Common |
| 4 | Week | Days to 1 week | Common |
| 5 | Weeks | Extended | Dialect |
| 9 | Years | Master-level binding | Treasure |
| 10 | Permanent | True permanence | Treasure |

### Access Tiers

**Common:** Known by default (0 CP) or purchasable at creation using Grammar Points (GP = Intellect + Dominion or Anima).

**Dialect:** Must be learned through play.

**Treasure:** Must be acquired through exceptional circumstances.

---

## PART X: ADDITIVES

Total Additive cost cannot exceed Complexity Pool.

### Application & Targeting

| Additive | Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Affects Others** | +2 | Personal-target expression affects other willing creatures by touch |
| **Affects Objects** | +2 | Creature-only expression affects inanimate objects |
| **Chain** | +3 | Arcs to secondary targets |
| **Dimensional** | +2 | Crosses immediate planar boundary |
| **Indirect** | +1 | Originates from point other than caster |
| **Linked** | +1 | Fuses two+ Effects into single simultaneous rote |
| **Multiattack** | +3 | Multiple attacks at full power |
| **Ricochet** | +1 | Bounces off surfaces |
| **Split** | +1 | Divides between multiple targets |
| **Selective** | +1 | Exclude targets within area effect |

### Potency & Duration

| Additive | Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Persistent** | +3 | Two defense checks, take worse result |
| **Penetrating** | +2/+4/+6 | Tier 1: bypasses Impervious. Tier 2: downgrades Immunity. Tier 3: ignores Immunity |
| **Secondary Effect** | +3 | Affects target again next round |
| **Tenacious** | +2 | Harder to dispel, extended duration |
| **Tethered** | +2 | Duration becomes Continuous, bound to caster. Consumes Mastery Capacity Pool |
| **Released** | +4 | Continuous and independent — permanent self-sustaining entity |
| **Suspended** | +3 | Pause effect and duration, hold in ready state |
| **Toggleable** | +1 | Sustained/Tethered expression switched on/off as free action |

### Tactical & Utility

| Additive | Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Alternate Effect** | +2 per | Multiple Effects, switchable |
| **Alternate Resistance** | +2 | Targets different defense pillar |
| **Coaxing** | +2 | Affects magic-immune or magic-averse creatures |
| **Conduit** | +1 | Deliver touch expression through willing ally |
| **Homing** | +2 | Missed projectile retries on subsequent rounds |
| **Imbued** | +2 | Summon linked to auto-applied beneficial effect |
| **Incurable** | +2 | Cannot be healed by normal means |
| **Insidious** | +2 | Target unaware they've been affected |
| **Lethal** | +3 | Massive margin overcomes = instant death |
| **Lingering Area** | +1 | Instantaneous area persists one additional round |
| **Reversible** | +1 | Dismiss effects on target at will |
| **Subtle** | +1/+2 | +1: difficult to perceive. +2: imperceptible without magical senses |
| **Transforming** | +3 | Affliction terminal condition permanently transforms target |
| **Triggered** | +2 | Pre-cast, activates on specific condition |
| **Vampiric** | +2 | Damage heals caster proportionally |
| **Variable Descriptor** | +2 | Primary Isotope changeable between uses |

---

## PART XI: SUBTRACTIVES

Limitations granting bonus points to Construction or Complexity Pool.

### Activation & Action

| Subtractive | Saving | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Activation** | −1 | Requires preparatory action |
| **Concentration** | −2 | Requires active concentration each round |
| **Increased Action** | −1 to −4 | Casting time increased: full-round (−1), one minute (−2), ten minutes (−3), one hour+ (−4) |

### Limitation & Restriction

| Subtractive | Saving | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Close Range Only** | −2 | Permanently Touch range |
| **Limited** | −1 to −4 | Broad category (−1), specific sub-category (−2), rare condition (−3), near-unique (−4) |
| **Removable** | −2 or −4 | Housed in item: hard to remove (−2), easily disarmed (−4) |
| **Requires Vessel** | −1 | Requires physical medium |
| **Ritual Components** | −1 or −2 | Verbal/somatic (−1), both (−2) |
| **Sense-Dependent** | −1 | Target must perceive with specific sense |
| **Uncentered Area** | −1 | Must include own position in area |

### Risk & Consequence

| Subtractive | Saving | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Check Required** | −2 | Skill check needed — failure wastes action and ÆP |
| **Contingent Control** | −2 | Control in external object/condition |
| **Delayed Activation** | −1 | Cast as trap or timer only |
| **Feedback** | −1 | Manifestation destroyed = caster backlash |
| **Overwhelming** | −4 | All-or-nothing — worst outcome on defense failure, no effect on success |
| **Sacrifice** | −2 or −4 | Personal price. Minor (−2), major persistent (−4) |
| **Side Effect** | −1 to −4 | On failure: inconvenient (−1) to catastrophic (−4) |
| **Uncontrolled Parameter** | −2 | GM may alter one parameter |
| **Unstable** | −2 | Chance of mishap per use |

---

## PART XII: ÆP — THE MAGICAL RESOURCE

**Total ÆP Pool:** ÆP_max = (Dominion + Tₘ) × Mastery
*(Tₘ = total magical/supernatural talents purchased)*

**Base ÆP Cost:** (ESL × 2) − 1

**Architect Efficiency:** Each Metamagic surcharge reduced by ⌊Intellect ÷ 2⌋, minimum +1.

**Improviser Reduction:** Base ÆP reduced by max(0, Anima − 3), floored at 0. Metamagic surcharges added at full cost after reduction. Casting never generates ÆP.

---

## PART XIII: THE BOOST SYSTEM

| Cap | Overall Mastery | Effect/Isotope Tier |
|---|---|---|
| Permanent | +2 max | +3 max |
| Temporary | +2 max | +3 max |
| Combined | +4 max | +6 max |

Temporary boosts cannot break tiers (mortal cap at 10). Permanent boosts CAN — this is how ascension happens. The Prodigy Aptitude (+1 for specific focus) stacks on top of all other boosts.

---

## PART XIV: THE DRIP RECHARGE SYSTEM

All recharge rates expressed in terms of the 10-minute interval.

| Unit | Duration | Intervals |
|---|---|---|
| Round | 6 seconds | — |
| Interval | 10 minutes | 1 |
| Hour | 60 minutes | 6 |
| Day | 24 hours | 144 |
| Week | 8 days | 1,152 |

**Calculation:** Period ÷ Frequency = Interval between charges. When interval resolves to 6 seconds or less, flip to per-round.

**Planar Influence:** Recharge rates affected by ambient planar radiation, carrier's planar profile, and active planar pressure. Items with affinity to the dominant expression are less affected; items with antithetical participation are most vulnerable.

**Mutation Self-Generation:** Mutations generate charges for their own effects at rates determined by Formation Stage and interaction profile. Projecting (Gas-derived): faster, wider-dispersing. Static (Solid-derived): slower, more stable. Absorbing (Plasma-derived): generates by consuming fuel. Cycling (Liquid-derived): generates through internal conversion.

---

## PART XV: MASTERY CAPACITY POOL

Measures ability to sustain Tethered continuous effects simultaneously.

**Base Pool:** Overall Mastery × 4
**Maximum Pool:** Overall Mastery × 34
**Expanded Consciousness Talent:** Each purchase adds (Overall Mastery × 3). Purchase limit: current Overall Mastery. Dynamic scaling.

---

## PART XVI: METAMAGIC TALENTS

| Talent | ÆP Cost | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| **Empower** | +2 | Variable numeric effects +50% |
| **Maximize** | +4 | All variable effects at maximum |
| **Quicken** | ESL × 2 | Standard action → swift/minor action |
| **Enlarge** | +1 | Double range |
| **Widen** | +2 | Double area |
| **Extend** | +1 | Double duration |
| **Unbalanced Magic** | — | Overwhelm (−attack, +effect) or Pinpoint (−effect, +attack) |

---

## PART XVII: FOCUS TALENTS

- **Focus (Effect):** Chosen Effect harder to resist (+1 challenge TN). Repeatable.
- **Focus (Isotope):** Chosen Isotope harder to resist.
- **Spell Penetration:** Bonus to overcome magical resistance.
- **Warden Breaker:** Significant bonus vs static magical defenses.
- **Immunity Breach (Mastery Talent):** Temporarily suppress Immunity to specific Isotope.

---

## PART XVIII: CHARACTER CREATION INTEGRATION

**Universal Grammar (Free):** All 0 CP components.

**Grammar Points:** GP = Intellect + Dominion (or Anima). Spent to unlock Common components at creation.

**Restricted Components:** 5+ CP (Dialect/Treasure) cannot be selected at creation.

**Isotope Learning:** Each isotope is a talent (6 XP). Compounds require constituent pures. Diagonals require Forge. Emulsions require both antithetical planes + Forge.

**Effect Learning:** Each Effect ranked independently, 1-10.

---

*End of Spell Sentence System. Next section: Planar Strain & The Mutation Economy.*

# Ætherium Codex — Spell Sentence System Revision
## Session 2: Effect Consolidation, Scaling Tables, & Architectural Decisions

**Date:** April 9, 2026  
**Status:** Working draft — all decisions locked unless explicitly reopened  
**Predecessor:** Session 1 transcript (AC_Effect_Scaling_Tables)

---

# PART I: THE REVISED EFFECTS LIST (19 + Variable)

Down from 35+ effects to 19 core effects plus Variable. No mechanical capability lost. Every collapsed effect exists as a specific application of its parent effect.

## A. Offensive Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Damage** | 1 | Direct harm across any pillar. Isotope determines damage type and die size band. |
| **Affliction** | 1–5 (by subcategory) | Impose discrete conditions or cause directed condition track movement. Subcategorized by severity — each condition IS its own BER. Recovery conditions are Additives. |
| **Nullify** | 3 | Counter-magic via neutralization reaction. Introduce incompatible magical expression to destabilize target spell's concordance structure. Energy release scales with target's ESL. |
| **Weaken** | 2 / 3 / 4 | Reduce attributes. Single attribute (2), Pillar (3), Full Suite/negative levels (4). Mirrors Enhanced Trait. |

## B. Defensive Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Protection** | 2 | Numeric defense increases — Defense Bonus, DR, Energy Resistance, Threshold Bonus, Barriers. Never reaches absolute. Stays in its own lane; Immunity handles absolutes. |
| **Immunity** | 4 | Absolute negation of a specific isotope expression, condition category, or concept. Breadth scales with ESL. Narrow by design — each purchase protects against ONE thing absolutely. |
| **Immortality** | 5 | Permanent soul-body binding preventing death from completing. Not resurrection (that's high-rank Panacea). Dot progression determines binding strength and distribution. |

## C. Movement Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Movement** | 1 | Grant or enhance ANY movement mode — land speed, swim, burrow, climb, leap, flight. Isotope determines which mode. Flight is not separate; the BER 1 premium is identical for all media. Hover (air) unlocks at mid ESL the same way treading water (swim) does. |
| **Teleport** | 3 | Same-plane translocation by skimming the Palimpsest surface. |
| **Dimensional Travel** | 4 | Interplanar transit through the Palimpsest. Dot 1 = controlled aperture + Translocation Geometry shortcuts. Dot 2 = full interplanar transit via deep Palimpsest navigation. |
| **Time Travel** | 5 | Temporal displacement through the Palimpsest via Synod navigation. Three modes: Loop (round trip, both endpoints fixed), Rethread (one-way, adding to the tapestry), Loose Thread (serial travel, origin must be anchored). Temporal wall at the Calamity — cannot navigate pre-Calamity. |

## D. Recovery Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Healing** | 2 | HP restoration across all four pillars. Strictly HP. Duration from Construction Pool creates HoT effects. Regeneration merged here — HoTs are just Healing + duration. |
| **Panacea** | 3 | Condition reversal. Inverse of Affliction. Whatever Affliction can impose at a severity, Panacea can reverse. Limb regrowth = Panacea or Transform restoring original configuration. Resurrection = high-rank Panacea (reversing the death condition). |

## E. Sensory Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Senses** | 2 | Unified information management — acquisition, processing, AND dissemination. Absorbs Communication, Comprehend, and Remote Sensing. Covers enhanced perception, analytical overlay (Hypercognition), remote sensing (scrying), object/location reading (Synod archive), temporal perception (precognition at high ESL), and information transmission (telepathy, languages, missives). The isotope determines the information channel. Construction Pool targeting determines input vs output vs bidirectional. |
| **Mind Link** | 3 | Bidirectional deep mental access. Low rank: read surface thoughts. Mid rank: implant suggestions, charm (Unity Academy signature). High rank: dominate, memory rewrite. Stays separate from Senses because the invasion of another mind is categorically different from information gathering. |

## F. Support & Utility Effects

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Feature** | 0 | The universal magical tell. Cosmetic only. Everyone in Tessera has one. The prima æther acknowledging you exist. Diagnostic — trained observers read Features to map magical aptitude. Base cost 1 eliminated; all mechanically useful "features" map to existing effects at ESL 1–2. |
| **Chronos** | 2 | Temporal flow manipulation — local time dilation/compression. Haste, slow, aging, preservation. Both perceptual AND actual temporal acceleration can be simultaneously true (Sandevistan model). Isotope determines the flavor. Chronosynclastic Institute's signature effect. Buff and debuff in one effect. |
| **Create** | 2 | Form solid non-living objects or extradimensional spaces from Prima Æther. Spaces are Palimpsest folds — pinches in the fabric that create accessible pockets. ESL determines volume. Familiar Pocket (1 cu ft) → Bag of Holding (30 cu ft) → Rope Trick (1000 cu ft) → Demiplane (millions cu ft). Demiplanes = construction projects using multiple linked rotes over multiple sessions. |
| **Move Object** | 2 | Telekinesis — move, lift, manipulate objects at distance. ESL determines weight capacity. |
| **Illusion** | 2 / 3 | Palimpsest superposition collapse. Single sense (2), multi-sensory (3). ESL determines collapse percentage. Low ESL = ghostly, barely present. Mid ESL = quasi-real (Shadow Conjuration). High ESL = fully collapsed, indistinguishable from reality. >100% = hyper-real, MORE anchored than baseline reality. Succeeding on save against >100% causes Metaphysical track damage. Chiaroscuro Institute's signature effect. Absorbs Concealment (invisibility = illusion of empty space). |
| **Enhanced Trait** | 2 / 3 / 4 | Attribute enhancement. Single attribute (2), Pillar (3), Full Suite (4). Mirrors Weaken. |
| **Summon** | 3 | Call forth a creature to aid the practitioner. ESL caps the Creature Mastery Level. Summoned creatures use the same character engine as players. Companion talent trees enhance summoned creatures. Feature Point system (when built) will decompose into spell sentence expressions. |
| **Transform** | 1 / 2 | Tiered. Alter fundamental properties of any target — object OR living tissue. Minor physical modification (1): elongation, extra limbs, cosmetic changes. Significant restructuring (2): growth, shrinking, full biological rewrite, hostile transformation, phase state changes (gaseous form, ethereal). Absorbs Insubstantial (self-targeted phase transformation), Morph, Elongation, Extra Limbs, Growth, Shrinking. Petrification = Transform (flesh→stone). Disintegration = extreme Transform. Gaseous Form = Transform (flesh→gas). Three-leg reversion for significant transformations: Believing (Integrity/self-concept) + Knowing (Metaphysics/theory) + Doing (Praxis/execution). |

## G. Metapowers

| Effect | Base Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **Variable** | Rank × 2 | Bounded replicator at mortal tier. Output identical to replicated effect at that ESL. Constraint: can only replicate effects caster has ≥1 dot in. Variable bypasses isotope requirement. Praxis Collegium's signature (overt Scholar's Gambit = flexibility; covert Fulcrum = learns effects to deconstruct them). |

# PART II: EFFECTS COLLAPSED (with reasoning)

| Original Effect | Collapsed Into | Reasoning |
|---|---|---|
| Leaping | Movement | Same verb, different medium. Isotope determines mode. |
| Speed | Movement | Same verb, different medium. |
| Swimming | Movement | Same verb, different medium. |
| Burrowing | Movement | Same verb, different medium. |
| Wall-Crawling | Movement | Same verb, different medium. |
| Flight | Movement | Same verb. BER difference was arbitrary. Hover unlocks at mid ESL. |
| Concealment | Illusion | Invisibility = illusion of empty space. Same mechanism. |
| Deflect | Protection (Additive) | Reactive casting via Triggered (+2) Additive. |
| Regeneration | Healing | HoTs = Healing + duration from Construction Pool. |
| Communication | Senses | Output side of information management. Same channel, different direction. |
| Comprehend | Senses | Processing layer on existing input. Pure Senses. |
| Remote Sensing | Senses | Senses with range from Construction Pool. |
| Elongation | Transform (minor, tier 1) | Minor physical modification. |
| Extra Limbs | Transform (minor, tier 1) | Minor physical modification. |
| Growth | Transform (significant, tier 2) | Significant restructuring. |
| Shrinking | Transform (significant, tier 2) | Significant restructuring. |
| Morph | Transform | Subsumed into tiered Transform. |
| Insubstantial | Transform | Self-targeted phase state change. Ghosts aren't Insubstantial — they're souls without bodies. |
| Environment | Systemic Catalyst (Talent) | Not an effect — a force multiplier. Change one parameter, let physics amplify. |
| Quickness | Chronos | Temporal dilation covers haste, slow, aging, preservation. |
| Luck Control | Fulcrum Point System | Meta-currency handles rerolls/narrative intervention. Numerical bonuses = Enhanced Trait/Weaken. |

# PART IV: ARCHITECTURAL DECISIONS

## BER Model (Model A Confirmed)

- BER is STATIC. Base cost IS the BER, always.
- BER never scales with dots. Dots invested = Effect Tier = ESL cap.
- Base cost = pool footprint tax determining room for Additives.
- Higher base cost = less room for customization, not harder to cast.

## Core Formula

- **Final Rank** = BER + Additives − Subtractives = ESL
- **ESL** capped by min(Effect Tier, Isotope Tier) AND cannot exceed Mastery
- **Pool** = (½ Effect Tier) + (½ Isotope Tier) + Mastery + Moiety
- **ÆP Cost** = (ESL × 2) − 1

## Linked Rotes (Composition Rule, NOT Additive)

Linked (+1) REMOVED from Additive list. Becomes composition rule:

"Two or more independently constructed rotes may be composed as a Linked pair. Linked rotes activate simultaneously as one action. Each rote maintains its own BER, ESL, Construction Pool, Additives, and Subtractives. The caster pays the combined ÆP cost of all Linked rotes."

Each rote in a Linked pair carries its own Subtractives independently. This explains how Stinking Cloud works: fog rote (Environment/Illusion) with minutes duration + Affliction rote with Overwhelming (−4) anchor at shorter duration. Different ESLs, different parameters, one activation.

Linked rotes are implanted into items as single units. Insets modify the implanted rote externally (like talents). A wand of Stinking Cloud contains the linked pair as one function.

## Three-Layer Modifier Architecture

1. **Internal (part of the rote):** Additives and Subtractives. Fixed at composition. Change the rote itself.
2. **External-permanent (talents/insets):** Metamagic talents, Focus talents, item insets. Applied from outside. Don't change the rote's internal formula.
3. **External-temporary (casting decisions):** Metamagic ÆP surcharges, Improviser swaps. Per-casting modifications.

## Condition Track System (SWSE-style, three layers)

- **Layer 1:** HP Pools (four pillars) — Damage reduces, Healing restores
- **Layer 2:** Condition Tracks (four pillars, 5 steps each) — Threshold-exceeding hits OR Affliction causes track movement
- **Layer 3:** Discrete Conditions (binary on/off) — independent of tracks

Track movement = just track movement. Recovery conditions belong to the EFFECT, not the track step.

## Unified Architecture (Spell = Item = Creature)

The spell sentence is the universal grammar for ALL expressions:

- A **spell** is a temporary expression produced by a caster
- An **item mutation** is a permanent expression bound to a material substrate
- A **creature mutation** is a permanent expression bound to a biological substrate
- An **enchantment** is an external expression applied to an existing substrate
- An **alchemical compound** is an expression applied through chemical process
- A **planar bleed** is an environmental expression produced by cosmological pressure
- A **permanent spell (Released)** is a free-floating expression without a substrate
- A **companion's abilities** ARE spell sentence expressions baked into its character sheet as permanent mutations

Same grammar. Same decomposition. Same inverted formula to analyze any of them.

1. **Tenacious naming collision:** Existing Tenacious (+2, harder to dispel) vs new Tenacious Recovery (+1, requires check). Different mechanics, same name.
2. **Incurable (+2) vs Rooted Recovery (+2):** Describe nearly the same limitation from different angles. May be redundant.
3. **Transforming (+3):** May be redundant with hostile Transform at high rank. Needs reconciliation.

## NEW — Systemic Catalyst (replaces Environment effect)

**Systemic Catalyst (Talent):** When targeting a natural system's leverage point rather than a direct target, the effect's reach multiplies based on the system's scale.

| System Scale | Reach Multiplier | Example Leverage Point |
|---|---|---|
| Local (room/pond) | ×2 | Heating element in closed room |
| Site (building/grove) | ×5 | Structural keystone, root system hub |
| Block (street/stream) | ×10 | Water main, gas line, herd alpha |
| District (neighborhood/creek) | ×25 | Tributary junction, power grid node |
| Regional (valley/river) | ×100 | Pressure gradient, aquifer source |
| Territorial (weather front/ecosystem) | ×500 | Jet stream boundary, watershed divide |

Multiplier applies to AREA of consequence, not intensity. Precision lost proportionally. ×2 = mostly controllable. ×500 = act of god you pointed in a direction. Output becomes non-magical after triggering — natural processes take over, Nullify can't reverse it.

**Prerequisites:** Minimum ranks in Discovery or Survival, knowledge of relevant isotope, GM confirms valid leverage point exists.

# PART VIII: KEY SETTING REVELATIONS

## Nullify as Neutralization Reaction

Nullify operates via chemical neutralization model. Incompatible expression introduced to target spell's concordance structure. Reaction is exothermic — released energy scales with target ESL. Deepguard wards are pressurized containers; breaking one releases stored energy as uncontrolled burst. Antimagic Field runs suppression PLUS containment simultaneously (why it's high ESL).

Against Structured Current (Architect) spells: attack concordance lattice, introduce turbulence.
Against Primal Current (Improviser) spells: impose unwanted structure on chaotic flow, crystallize until it shatters.
Against natural phenomena: significantly harder — no caster maintaining it.

## Palimpsest Ecology and Extradimensional Spaces

The Palimpsest is NOT featureless void (unlike PF1e's Ethereal). It has terrain, ecology, Phase Hounds, Silverbark trees. Extradimensional spaces are folds in the Palimpsest's occupied territory. A Bag of Holding's interior IS a pinch of Palimpsest space. Every fold has a LOCATION accessible from the Palimpsest side. Palimpsest-native creatures can access fold contents (dimensional mice in your backpack). Items don't stop working inside each other — the Palimpsest has structural integrity to hold nested folds trivially.

The Palimpsest's fabric is "effectively infinite" — mortal-scale folding is scooping a cup from the ocean. The Calamity was draining a sea. Aíon prevents the fabric from ever breaking entirely.

Planar incursions = Palimpsest stretching thin between two points. Same mechanism as Create but in reverse — Create PINCHES (adds local thickness), incursions STRETCH (reduce buffer). The Palimpsest never breaks; even at thinnest, the membrane exists.

Every extradimensional space ever created is a room in the Palimpsest. Ancient abandoned folds persist. The Palimpsest IS the mega-dungeon. This is the Narrative Engine's dungeon generator.

## Illusion as Palimpsest Superposition Collapse

Illusions pull potential states from the Palimpsest and partially collapse them into material reality. The "percentage real" = how far the superposition has collapsed. Will saves = metaphysical resistance to having local reality edited. >100% collapse = hyper-real, more anchored than baseline reality. Succeeding on save against >100% causes Metaphysical track damage because the observer sees reality has a fidelity setting that can be exceeded.

The Maestro (Chiaroscuro Headmaster) is a permanently >100% collapsed superposition state — a meta-narrative Easter egg about fictional characters existing through collective belief. Lyra (Unity) wants to reverse-engineer the Maestro's creation mechanism for her ascension bid.

## Wildpath Wardens: The Immunity vs Transform Philosophy

Novice = picks Immunity (defensive, narrow). Master = uses Transform (adaptive, comprehensive). "Why resist lava when you can become something that lives in lava?" The immortality engine teaches through death. Forest "monsters" = failed students whose psyche collapsed. Stormpeak's monster hunters are doing triage on Wildpath failures.

## The Two Intelligence Agencies

Chronosynclastic Institute = SIGINT (signals intelligence, NSA model, Hitman). Sit in volcano, collect data, model behavior, predict outcomes. Senses as analytical framework.

Praxis Collegium = HUMINT (human intelligence, 007/MIB model). Field operations, boots on ground, gadget toolkit (Variable). Senses as operational field awareness.

Mutually Assured Disclosure prevents direct conflict. The gun is both a threat and for show because that gun is an information nuke. Neither can exist without the other. Both play kal-toh, not chess.

## Feature as Universal Magical Tell

Everyone in Tessera has a Feature (base cost 0). The candle flame a fire-aspected child can't stop flickering. The static charge following an electricity-aspected person. Diagnostic — trained observers read Features to map aptitude. The magical equivalent of fidgeting. The easiest way to determine magical aptitude (Avatar-style).

## Ghost Sidebar Integration

Ghosts are NOT using Insubstantial. Ghosts are souls without bodies — their incorporeality is the natural condition of a disembodied consciousness, not a spell effect. A living creature "going incorporeal" is Transform (self-targeted phase change). Different phenomena, different mechanics.

# AMENDMENT 2: ADDITIVE LIST CHANGES

## 2A. Additives REMOVED

| Additive | Old Cost | Reasoning |
|---|---|---|
| Linked | +1 | Becomes composition rule for independently constructed rotes (see Amendment 5) |
| Incurable | +2 | Redundant with Rooted Recovery (+2); identical mechanical outcome |
| Transforming | +3 | Redundant with hostile Transform + Linked rotes with Triggered Additive |

## 2B. Additives RENAMED

| Old Name | New Name | Cost | Reasoning |
|---|---|---|---|
| Tenacious | Anchored | +2 | Resolves naming collision with Tenacious Recovery (+1). Anchored = harder to dispel, extended duration. |

## 2C. Additives ADDED

| Additive | Cost | Description |
|---|---|---|
| Tenacious Recovery | +1 | Affliction rote modifier. Condition requires skill check or treatment to remove. |
| Rooted Recovery | +2 | Affliction rote modifier. Condition requires magical intervention (Panacea/Nullify) to remove. |
| Bound Recovery | +3 | Affliction rote modifier. Condition requires specific narrative condition defined at casting to remove. |

## 2D. Additive Design Principle CONFIRMED

Additives are NOT hard-locked to specific effects. Unusual combinations create interesting narrative results (Vampiric Protection = shield absorbing attacks and converting to HP). The system does not restrict — grammar naturally produces coherent results. Ignite is an isotope expression, not an Additive.

# AMENDMENT 4: TALENT LIST CHANGES

## 4A. Talents RENAMED

| Old Name | New Name | Reasoning |

## 4B. Talents ADDED

| Talent | Description |
|---|---|
| **Systemic Catalyst** | When targeting a natural system's leverage point rather than a direct target, the effect's reach multiplies based on the system's scale. Replaces Environment effect. Output becomes non-magical after triggering — natural processes take over, Nullify can't reverse it. |

### Systemic Catalyst Reach Multiplier Table

| System Scale | Multiplier | Example Leverage Point |
|---|---|---|
| Local (room/pond) | ×2 | Heating element in closed room |
| Site (building/grove) | ×5 | Structural keystone, root system hub |
| Block (street/stream) | ×10 | Water main, gas line, herd alpha |
| District (neighborhood/creek) | ×25 | Tributary junction, power grid node |
| Regional (valley/river) | ×100 | Pressure gradient, aquifer source |
| Territorial (weather front/ecosystem) | ×500 | Jet stream boundary, watershed divide |

**Multiplier applies to AREA of consequence, not intensity.** Precision lost proportionally. ×2 = mostly controllable. ×500 = act of god pointed in a direction.

**Prerequisites:** Minimum ranks in Discovery or Survival, knowledge of relevant isotope, GM confirms valid leverage point exists.

# AMENDMENT 5: LINKED ROTES (Composition Rule)

**Linked (+1) REMOVED from Additive list.** Replaced by composition rule:

"Two or more independently constructed rotes may be composed as a Linked pair. Linked rotes activate simultaneously as one action. Each rote maintains its own BER, ESL, Construction Pool, Additives, and Subtractives. The caster pays the combined ÆP cost of all Linked rotes. No surcharge."

**Key properties:**
- Each rote priced independently with full ÆP
- Each rote carries its own Subtractives independently
- Different durations, different ESLs, different saves all permitted within one Linked pair
- Linked rotes implanted into items as single units
- Insets modify the implanted rote externally (like talents)
- Demiplanes = construction projects using multiple Linked rotes, can be built over multiple casting sessions

**Test case — Saltray (2nd level equivalent):**
- Rote A: Damage (BER 1), ray, close, instantaneous. ESL 3. ÆP = 5.
- Rote B: Affliction Stunned (BER 4), Overwhelming (−4). FR = minimum 1. ESL 1. ÆP = 1.
- Combined ÆP: 6. Damage always hits. Stun is all-or-nothing.

**Test case — Stinking Cloud (3rd level equivalent):**
- Rote A: Illusion visual denial (BER 2), spread (2 CP), medium range (1 CP), minutes (1 CP). ESL 2. ÆP = 3. Creates fog.
- Rote B: Affliction Nauseated (BER 3), Overwhelming (−4). FR = minimum 1. ESL 1. ÆP = 1. Separate save each round.
- Combined ÆP: 4.

**Test case — Magic Mouth (2nd level equivalent):**
- Effect: Senses output (BER 2)
- Additive: Triggered (+2)
- Subtractive: Ritual Components (−2)
- FR: 2 + 2 − 2 = 2. ESL 2. ÆP = 3.

**Test case — Rope Trick (2nd level equivalent):**
- Effect: Create (BER 2), Palimpsest/spatial isotope
- Construction: Touch (0 CP), Hours (2 CP)
- Subtractive: Requires Vessel (−1, the rope)
- FR: 2 − 1 = 1. ESL 1 naked = ~1 cu ft. Higher Effect Tier pushes to room scale.

# AMENDMENT 8: ARCHITECTURAL DECISIONS

## 8A. BER Model (Model A) — CONFIRMED

BER is STATIC. Base cost IS the BER, always. BER never scales with dots. Dots invested = Effect Tier = ESL cap. Base cost = pool footprint tax determining room for Additives.

## 8B. Core Formulas — CONFIRMED (Book of Shadows is canonical)

- Final Rank = BER + Additives − Subtractives = ESL
- ESL capped by min(Effect Tier, Isotope Tier, Mastery)
- Pool = (½ Effect Tier) + (½ Isotope Tier) + Mastery + Integrity
- ÆP Cost = (ESL × 2) − 1
- Character sheet formula is OUTDATED and needs updating to match

## 8C. Three-Layer Modifier Architecture — CONFIRMED

1. Internal (part of the rote): Additives and Subtractives
2. External-permanent (talents/insets): Metamagic, Focus, item insets
3. External-temporary (casting decisions): Metamagic ÆP surcharges, Improviser swaps

## 8D. Condition Track System — CONFIRMED (SWSE-style, three layers)

- Layer 1: HP Pools (four pillars)
- Layer 2: Condition Tracks (four pillars, 5 steps each)
- Layer 3: Discrete Conditions (binary on/off)

## 8E. Unified Architecture — CONFIRMED

Spell = Item = Creature. Same grammar, same decomposition, same inverted formula. The difference is substrate and duration.

## 8F. Nullify as Neutralization Reaction — NEW

Nullify operates via acid-base chemistry model. Incompatible expression introduced to target's concordance structure. Reaction is exothermic — energy release scales with target ESL. Deepguard wards are pressurized containers. Against Structured Current: introduce turbulence. Against Primal Current: impose unwanted structure. Against natural phenomena: significantly harder.

## 8G. Permanency Analysis — NEW

Two mechanisms cover permanent effects:
- **Tethered (+2):** Always-on personal enhancements consuming Mastery Capacity Pool (Darkvision, Tongues, Resist Energy)
- **Released (+4):** Independent infrastructure (Magic Mouth, Teleportation Circle, Symbol wards, Walls)

Design principle: passive states and infrastructure = appropriate for permanent expression. Events = not appropriate. Living spells = events that became entities through Released.

---

# AMENDMENT 9: SETTING REVELATIONS

## 9A. Palimpsest Ecology and Extradimensional Spaces

The Palimpsest has terrain, ecology, and native inhabitants. Extradimensional spaces are Palimpsest folds with LOCATIONS accessible from the Palimpsest side. Nesting works — the fabric is effectively infinite (ocean metaphor). Ancient abandoned folds persist as explorable spaces. The Palimpsest IS the mega-dungeon and dungeon generation engine. Items do NOT stop working inside each other. Planar incursions = Palimpsest stretching thin (inverse of Create's pinching). The Palimpsest never breaks — Aíon prevents it.

## 9B. Illusion as Palimpsest Superposition Collapse

Illusions pull potential states from the Palimpsest and partially collapse them. Will saves = metaphysical resistance to local reality editing. >100% collapse = hyper-real. Succeeding on save against >100% causes Metaphysical track damage. The Maestro = permanently >100% collapsed superposition state = meta-narrative Easter egg. Chiaroscuro masters who push past 100% demonstrate the Great Lie is a technique, not truth = ontological threat monitored by the Generalist College.

## 9C. Wildpath Wardens Philosophy

Novice = picks Immunity (defensive, narrow). Master = uses Transform (adaptive, comprehensive). "Why resist lava when you can become something that lives in lava?" Immortality engine teaches through death. Forest monsters = failed students. Stormpeak monster hunters = triage on Wildpath failures.

## 9D. Two Intelligence Agencies

Chronosynclastic = SIGINT (NSA, Hitman). Praxis = HUMINT (007, MIB). Mutually Assured Disclosure prevents direct conflict. Both play kal-toh. Information security is razor sharp.

## 9E. Feature as Universal Magical Tell

Everyone has one. Diagnostic for trained observers. The magical equivalent of fidgeting. Avatar-style aptitude expression.

## 9F. Ghost Sidebar Integration

Ghosts are souls without bodies — natural condition of disembodied consciousness, not a spell effect. Living creatures "going incorporeal" = Transform (phase change). Different phenomena entirely.

## 9G. Biological Adaptation Spectrum

| Stage | Description | Example |
|---|---|---|
| Pure Beast | No magical adaptation | Horse in low-saturation zone |
| Adapted Beast | Minor isotope absorption, cosmetic | Cave salamander with faint bioluminescence |
| Saturated Beast | Significant isotope integration, functional | Lava flea with fire resistance |
| Emulsion Creature | Multiple isotopes merged into stable biology | Phase Hound with dimensional awareness |
| Magical Creature | Isotope expression IS the biology | Elemental — made OF the expression |

Natural immunity through adaptation = same mechanism as Immunity spell, expressed through evolution instead of spellcraft. Wildpath Wardens compress this gradient through Transform.

## 9H. Summon as Universal Shaping Verb

Summon condenses prima æther into ANY shaped magical expression — not limited to creatures. Covers: creature summons, soul weapons, soul armor, totem aspects, ætheric tools, synthesist fusion, construct shells. All use the same build menu (Feature Points decomposing into spell sentence effects). The distinction from Create (physical objects that persist) and Transform (altering existing targets requiring reversion): Summon = active magic that dissolves without will/ÆP.

## 9I. Portal Railgun (Thought Experiment)

Two stacked Teleport apertures with conservation of energy producing unlimited gravitational acceleration. Stormpeak weapons program territory. Low ESL, high consequence.

# Ætherium Codex — Effect & Isotope Integration Reference
## Mapping the 19 Effects to Their Planar Homes

**Date:** April 9, 2026
**Status:** Working draft — plane-level mapping with individual isotope highlights
**Prerequisite:** AC_SpellSentence_Revision_Session2.md

---

# HOW TO READ THIS DOCUMENT

Effects are organized pedagogically from simplest to most complex, grouped under umbrella categories that reflect how a student would learn them. Within each effect entry, the five planes are presented in consistent order:

1. **Borderlands** — The Physical Register (inorganic chemistry)
2. **Verdant Font** — The Biological Register (organic chemistry)
3. **Synod of Endings** — The Entropic Register (thermodynamics)
4. **Hidden Forge** — The Technological Register (catalysis/process chemistry)
5. **Palimpsest** — The Narrative Register (statistical mechanics/equilibrium)

Within each plane, isotopes follow phase order: **Solid → Liquid → Gas → Plasma**. The phase profiles are:

| Phase | Borderlands | Verdant Font | Synod | Hidden Forge | Palimpsest |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Solid** | Earth | Flora | Osseous | Crystal | Echo |
| **Liquid** | Water | Fauna | Archival | Chem | Veil |
| **Gas** | Air | Myco | Spectral | Pneuma | Probability |
| **Plasma** | Fire | Viral | Void | Volt | Glitch |

**Key terminology reminders:**
- Soak (not DR). Same principle, setting-accurate name.
- TN (Target Number, not DC).
- Synod of Endings is NOT "the plane of death" — it is the bureaucracy of entropy, the archive of everything that has ended, and the waypoint for souls in transit.
- Verdant Font is NOT "the plane of life" — it is the engine of biological process: growth, decay, adaptation, and mutation. Cancer is as much Verdant Font as healing.
- Palimpsest is NOT "the ethereal plane" — it is the connective tissue between all planes, with its own terrain, ecology, and native inhabitants. It holds superposition states, probability currents, and every Palimpsest fold ever created.
- Prima Æther is the ambient magical field — the ongoing byproduct of the Calamity's cosmic neutralization reaction between two incompatible magical systems (Primal Current / Structured Current).

---

# I. FOUNDATIONS — The First Expressions

These are the effects every practitioner encounters first. They represent the most basic interactions between will and prima æther.

---

## Feature (Base Cost 0)

**The Universal Magical Tell**

Everyone in Tessera has a Feature. It is the prima æther acknowledging that you exist and you acknowledging it back. Cosmetic only. No mechanical impact. The magical equivalent of fidgeting — the candle a fire-aspected child can't stop flickering, the static charge trailing an electricity-aspected person, the way water moves toward someone with a water affinity when they're nervous. Trained observers read Features diagnostically to map magical aptitude.

Feature is not learned. It is expressed. A character's Feature reflects their deepest isotope affinity.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Elemental tells. Persistent warmth (Fire). Damp skin (Water). Pebbles tremble nearby (Earth). Hair moves without wind (Air). |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological tells. Flowers lean toward you (Flora). Animals calm in your presence (Fauna). Mushrooms sprout near your footprints (Myco). Cuts heal slightly faster than normal (Viral). |
| **Synod** | Entropic tells. Candles gutter near you (Osseous). You remember things you never learned (Archival). Your reflection moves a half-beat late (Spectral). Small objects near you age subtly (Void). |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological tells. Machines respond to your touch without activation (Crystal). Chemical reactions accelerate near you (Chem). Doors open slightly before you reach them (Pneuma). Lights flicker in your presence (Volt). |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative tells. Déjà vu follows you (Echo). Dreams bleed into waking description (Veil). Coincidences cluster around you (Probability). Minor errors in reality — a book you never put down, a door you never opened (Glitch). |

---

## Damage (Base Cost 1)

**Direct Harm Across Any Pillar**

The most immediate magical expression. Channel prima æther through an isotope to produce destructive output. The isotope determines the damage type and die size band. The ESL determines the dice count.

Damage targets the PHYSICAL pillar by default. Targeting other pillars (Mental, Social, Metaphysical) requires specific isotope selection — Mind-aspected damage targets Mental HP, Emotion-aspected targets Social HP, raw ætheric or Void-aspected targets Metaphysical HP (Pattern).

### Borderlands — Physical Destruction

The primary home for physical damage. Every Borderlands isotope can produce Damage as a natural expression. The phase determines the delivery:

- **Solid (Earth):** Bludgeoning, crushing, burial. B-09 Bedrock = raw mass impact. B-10 Magma = earth + fire adhesive weight. B-11 Acid = earth + water corrosive dissolution. B-12 Dust = earth + air abrasive shredding.
- **Liquid (Water):** Drowning, pressure, cold-wet. B-05 The Abyss = crushing pressure and hypothermia. B-06 Vapor = scalding penetrating burns. B-07 Mire = entangling suffocation. B-08 Hail = blunt cold projectiles.
- **Gas (Air):** Slashing (wind), concussive (sonic), suffocation. B-13 Void Sky = vacuum and depressurization. B-14 Current = electrical discharge through atmosphere. B-15 Frost = flash-freezing cold snap. B-16 Erosion = abrasive particulate stripping.
- **Plasma (Fire):** Thermal combustion, radiant burns. B-01 Inferno = consuming fire. B-02 Steam = scalding pressure penetration. B-03 Slag = adhesive molten mass. B-04 Scorch = ranged radiant heat without combustion.

### Verdant Font — Biological Harm

Biological damage works through the target's own systems. Poison, disease, parasitic invasion, allergic cascade. Often slower than Borderlands damage but harder to treat.

- **Solid (Flora):** Piercing (thorns), bludgeoning (roots, vines), strangling constriction. V-01 World Tree = crushing mass of ancient wood. V-02 Carnivorous = predatory plant attack. V-03 Spore = respiratory invasion. V-04 Overgrowth = destructive uncontrolled growth through the target.
- **Liquid (Fauna):** Bite, claw, venom injection. V-05 The Hunt = predatory physical damage. V-06 Sylvan = nature-red-in-tooth-and-claw. V-07 Cordyceps = parasitic invasion through biological vector. V-08 Chimera = hybrid biological attack.
- **Gas (Myco):** Spore clouds, mycotoxin exposure, respiratory damage. V-09 Mycelium = network-based decomposition. V-10 Rot-Garden = fungal decay of living tissue. V-11 The Hive = swarm damage through distributed organisms. V-12 Plague = weaponized pathogen.
- **Plasma (Viral):** Cellular corruption, hyper-mutation, immune cascade failure. V-13 The Cancer = uncontrolled cellular reproduction destroying tissue. V-14 Bioluminescence = radiant biological energy. V-15 Titan = hyper-growth damage through biological overload. V-16 Mutagen = forced mutation as a damage vector.

### Synod of Endings — Entropic Damage

The Synod does not deal "death damage" — it deals entropy. Things touched by Synod damage don't die; they END. The difference matters: death is a transition in the soul cycle. Ending is the cessation of a process. Synod damage degrades the target's structural coherence.

- **Solid (Osseous):** Brittle destruction, dessication, calcification. S-01 The Grave = structural collapse into final rest. S-02 Ossuary = archived remains weaponized. S-03 Husk = hollowing out, leaving empty shell. S-04 Necropolis = mass structural termination.
- **Liquid (Archival):** Memory damage, identity erosion, Pattern (Metaphysical HP) targeting. S-05 The Record = overwriting existing patterns with archived ones. S-06 Whispers = information as corrosive agent. S-07 Reliquary = preserved destruction replayed. S-08 Censor = selective deletion of target's properties.
- **Gas (Spectral):** Soul-touching damage, bypasses physical defenses. S-09 The Wraith = spectral contact draining vitality. S-10 Shadow = damage through darkness contact. S-11 Echo = damage from temporal afterimage, hit by something that already happened. S-12 Possession = damage from internal spiritual invasion.
- **Plasma (Void):** Pure entropic negation. S-13 Oblivion = damage through existence-denial. S-14 Decay = accelerated decomposition. S-15 Hollow = consuming internal substance. S-16 Amnesia = destroying the target's connection to their own pattern.

### Hidden Forge — Technological Weaponry

Engineered damage. Precise, efficient, often requiring specific infrastructure. The Forge doesn't produce raw elemental destruction — it produces designed weapon effects.

- **Solid (Crystal):** Resonant shattering, focused beams, lattice disruption. F-13 The Spire = crystalline cutting edges. F-14 Lens = focused energy amplification. F-15 Data = information overload as damage vector. F-16 Forge Mutagen = chemical-crystal hybrid weaponization.
- **Liquid (Chem):** Alchemical weapons, corrosives, volatile compounds. F-09 The Vat = chemical burns. F-10 Hydraulic = pressure-based destruction. F-11 Battery = stored energy discharge. F-12 Mana-Punk = magical-chemical explosive reaction.
- **Gas (Pneuma):** Concussive force, pressure weapons, vacuum effects. F-01 The Engine = mechanical force projection. F-02 Steampunk = pressure-based weaponry. F-03 Diesel = combustion-engine driven impact. F-04 Æther-Tech = magical-mechanical hybrid weapons.
- **Plasma (Volt):** Electrical damage, electromagnetic disruption. F-05 The Grid = network-routed electrical damage. F-06 Clockwork = precision-timed energy delivery. F-07 Galvanism = electrically animated projectiles. F-08 Cyberpunk = integrated weapon systems.

### Palimpsest — Probability Damage

The Palimpsest doesn't produce physical energy. It produces improbable outcomes. Damage through the Palimpsest means the target suffers harm through events that SHOULDN'T have happened but did — the floor gives way, the sword finds the gap in the armor it couldn't see, the target's own spell backfires. At high ESL, this becomes reality editing — the target is damaged because reality was briefly rewritten to include their injury.

- **Solid (Echo):** Damage from temporal echoes — hit by attacks that already happened, wounds from previous timelines. P-01 The Loop = trapped in a damage cycle. P-02 Haunt = damage from persistent echoes of past violence. P-03 Nostalgia = longing made physically harmful. P-04 Corrupt = echo degradation damaging pattern integrity.
- **Liquid (Veil):** Dream damage, perception-as-weapon, damage from things that aren't "real" but hurt anyway. P-05 Dream = nightmare made manifest. P-06 Gambit = calculated risk weaponized. P-07 Fey Logic = damage from internally consistent illogic. P-08 Horror = fear made physically destructive.
- **Gas (Probability):** Bad luck weaponized. The target trips, their weapon breaks, they walk into an attack. P-09 Casino = pure probability manipulation against the target. P-10 Prophecy = foreknowledge weaponized. P-11 Karma = accumulated consequence delivered. P-12 Chaos = random destructive events clustered on the target.
- **Plasma (Glitch):** Reality errors as damage. The target's armor doesn't exist for a moment. Their HP was always lower than they thought. Physics glitches in their vicinity. P-13 Error = fundamental reality malfunction. P-14 Paradox = self-contradicting damage that can't be logically resolved. P-15 Hazard = accumulated small errors cascading. P-16 Simulation = damage from the revelation that local reality is less stable than assumed.

---

## Movement (Base Cost 1)

**Grant or Enhance Any Movement Mode**

One effect, all media. The isotope determines the movement type (land, swim, burrow, climb, flight). The ESL determines the speed. Magic is the engine — the isotope determines the medium and flavor. Low ESL air movement requires forward momentum (ekranoplan effect). Mid ESL unlocks hover. High ESL = full three-dimensional authority at any speed including zero.

### Borderlands — Physical Movement (PRIMARY HOME)

Every physical movement mode originates here. The phase determines the medium:

- **Solid (Earth):** Burrow speed. Move through earth and stone. B-09 Bedrock = clean tunneling through rock. B-12 Dust = movement through loose particulate. B-10 Magma = movement through molten earth.
