- **Liquid (Water):** Swim speed. B-05 The Abyss = deep-pressure swimming. B-07 Mire = movement through viscous liquids. B-08 Hail = surface-running on water (freezing each step).
- **Gas (Air):** Flight and enhanced land speed through wind assistance. B-13 Void Sky = pure aerial movement. B-15 Frost = ice-surfing, skating. B-14 Current = riding electrical/thermal updrafts. B-16 Erosion = wind-assisted ground speed.
- **Plasma (Fire):** Rocket propulsion, thermal-lift flight, explosive acceleration. B-01 Inferno = thrust through combustion. B-04 Scorch = radiant propulsion.

### Verdant Font — Biological Movement

Enhanced biological locomotion. Not magical propulsion — adapted biology optimized for specific movement.

- **Solid (Flora):** Vine-swing, root-network traversal, arboreal movement. V-01 World Tree = climbing, brachiating.
- **Liquid (Fauna):** Animal-speed running, predator pursuit, pack movement. V-05 The Hunt = enhanced pursuit speed. V-06 Sylvan = forest-adapted movement.
- **Gas (Myco):** Spore-drift, network-hop (mycelial translocation through connected organisms). V-09 Mycelium = distributed movement through fungal networks.
- **Plasma (Viral):** Hyper-accelerated cellular response, burst speed. V-13 Cancer = uncontrolled growth as movement.

### Synod of Endings — Spectral Movement

Movement through the Synod's domain. Phase determines the mechanism:

- **Solid (Osseous):** Grave-walk — movement through burial sites and ossuaries as if connected. S-01 The Grave = passage through interred ground.
- **Liquid (Archival):** Movement by following the Record — tracing paths that were historically traveled. S-05 Record = retracing archived routes.
- **Gas (Spectral):** Ghostly passage. Phase through solid matter by becoming partially spectral. S-09 Wraith = incorporeal movement. S-10 Shadow = shadow-step, moving through connected darkness.
- **Plasma (Void):** Movement through negation — the space between you and the destination is temporarily voided. S-13 Oblivion = distance-negation.

### Hidden Forge — Technological Movement

Engineered propulsion systems. Kan-tech vehicles, magnetic levitation, mechanical locomotion.

- **Solid (Crystal):** Rail systems, crystalline resonance paths, structured transit. F-13 Spire = lattice-guided movement.
- **Liquid (Chem):** Chemical propulsion, hydraulic systems. F-09 Vat = chemical-powered locomotion. F-10 Hydraulic = pressure-driven movement.
- **Gas (Pneuma):** Pneumatic propulsion, jet engines, compressed-air systems. F-01 Engine = mechanical propulsion. F-02 Steampunk = steam-powered movement. F-03 Diesel = combustion-engine vehicles.
- **Plasma (Volt):** Electromagnetic propulsion, maglev, lightning-rail. F-05 Grid = network-routed transit. F-08 Cyberpunk = integrated augmented locomotion.

### Palimpsest — Narrative Movement

You don't travel through physical space. You change your relationship with where you are relative to where you need to be. At low ESL this looks like uncanny luck with shortcuts. At high ESL this is the beginning of Teleport territory — the Palimpsest IS the plane of translocation.

- **Solid (Echo):** Walk paths that used to exist. Follow archived routes through spaces that have changed.
- **Liquid (Veil):** Dream-walk. Move through the subjective experience of space rather than space itself.
- **Gas (Probability):** Be where you're statistically most likely to be useful. "I was already heading this way."
- **Plasma (Glitch):** Spatial errors. You're suddenly somewhere else because local geometry briefly malfunctioned.

---

## Transform (Base Cost 1 Minor / 2 Significant)

**Alter Fundamental Properties of Any Target**

The unified alteration effect. Minor (1): cosmetic changes, elongation, extra appendages, minor structural modification. Significant (2): size change, full biological rewrite, phase state change, hostile forced transformation, species alteration.

Absorbs: Morph, Elongation, Extra Limbs, Growth, Shrinking, Insubstantial.

Three-leg reversion for significant transformations: Believing (Integrity/self-concept), Knowing (Metaphysics/theory), Doing (Praxis/execution). Wildpath Wardens train all three. Forest "monsters" = failed students whose psyche collapsed — immortality engine keeps them alive in broken form.

### Borderlands — Physical Transformation

Phase state changes in physical matter. Stone to mud. Water to ice. Metal to rust. The Borderlands is the most literal expression of Transform.

- **Solid (Earth):** Stone Shape, Transmute Rock to Mud, crystal restructuring. B-09 Bedrock = reshape stone. B-11 Acid = dissolve and reform. B-12 Dust = particulate restructuring.
- **Liquid (Water):** Freeze/thaw, purify, corrupt. B-05 Abyss = pressure-transformation. B-07 Mire = viscosity manipulation.
- **Gas (Air):** Atmospheric composition change. B-13 Void Sky = create/remove atmosphere. B-16 Erosion = gradual surface transformation through sustained exposure.
- **Plasma (Fire):** Smelting, calcination, combustion-based processing. B-01 Inferno = fire as transformation agent. B-03 Slag = molten-state material processing.

### Verdant Font — Biological Transformation (PRIMARY for living targets)

The Wildpath Wardens' domain. Every polymorph, beast shape, and biological adaptation lives here.

- **Solid (Flora):** Plant-based body modification. Bark skin, wooden limbs, root-feet. V-01 World Tree = structural biological scaffolding. V-02 Carnivorous = predatory plant hybrid forms.
- **Liquid (Fauna):** Animal forms. Beast shape, predator adaptation, chimeric hybridization. V-05 The Hunt = predator form assumption. V-06 Sylvan = forest-creature forms. V-08 Chimera = multi-species hybrid transformation.
- **Gas (Myco):** Fungal transformation. Spore-body, distributed consciousness across a mycelial network. V-09 Mycelium = becoming a distributed organism. V-07 Cordyceps = parasitic takeover of another body.
- **Plasma (Viral):** Rapid forced mutation. Uncontrolled cellular replication. V-13 Cancer = destructive transformation through biological overload. V-15 Titan = beneficial hyper-growth. V-16 Mutagen = controlled mutagenic transformation.

### Synod of Endings — Entropic Transformation

Transformation as process termination. The Synod doesn't change what something IS — it changes what something WAS. Petrification as "ending mobility." Aging as "accelerating temporal transformation."

- **Solid (Osseous):** Petrification (flesh → stone as "ending" biological motion). Calcification. S-01 Grave = transforming living tissue into burial material.
- **Liquid (Archival):** Identity transformation — the target's pattern is overwritten with an archived one. S-05 Record = "you become what the Record says you were."
- **Gas (Spectral):** Phase transformation toward spectral state. Becoming ghost-like. S-09 Wraith = self-targeted gaseous/incorporeal form.
- **Plasma (Void):** Disintegration. Transformation into nothing. S-13 Oblivion = reducing something to its component absence. S-14 Decay = accelerated decomposition.

### Hidden Forge — Technological Transformation

Material processing, refining, alloy creation. Kan-tech modification. The Forge transforms raw material into finished product through engineered process.

- **Solid (Crystal):** Crystalline restructuring, material purification, gem cutting. F-13 Spire = lattice optimization. F-16 Forge Mutagen = chemical-crystal hybrid processing.
- **Liquid (Chem):** Chemical transformation, alchemical transmutation, solution processing. F-09 Vat = reaction-vessel transformation. F-12 Mana-Punk = magical-chemical conversion.
- **Gas (Pneuma):** Pressure transformation, gas-state processing. F-01 Engine = mechanical conversion of energy states.
- **Plasma (Volt):** Electromagnetic restructuring, plasma-state material processing. F-05 Grid = energy-state transformation.

### Palimpsest — Narrative Transformation

You become what the story needs. Reality edits applied to form. The Palimpsest doesn't change biology or chemistry — it changes what you ARE in the narrative.

- **Solid (Echo):** Become what you were. Revert to a previous form. P-01 Loop = body returns to a fixed state.
- **Liquid (Veil):** Dream transformation. Become what you imagine yourself to be. P-05 Dream = fluid identity.
- **Gas (Probability):** Become what you're most likely to be. Statistical averaging of your potential forms. P-09 Casino = form determined by probability.
- **Plasma (Glitch):** Involuntary transformation through reality error. You WERE always this way — the records just caught up. P-13 Error = retroactive form change.

---

## Affliction (Base Cost 1–5 by Subcategory)

**Impose Conditions or Cause Condition Track Movement**

Subcategorized by severity. Each condition IS its own BER — the system self-prices. The spell sentence names the specific condition being imposed. Recovery conditions are Additives on the Affliction rote.

*Severity tiers: Trivial (BER 1) → Minor (BER 2) → Moderate (BER 3) → Severe (BER 4) → Critical (BER 5)*

All five planes can produce Afflictions. The isotope determines the FLAVOR of the condition and often carries inherent recovery conditions (Synod Afflictions are inherently Tenacious or Rooted — entropy doesn't un-do itself easily).

| Plane | Typical Affliction Expressions | Inherent Recovery |
|---|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Burning (fire), Frozen (cold), Blinded (light/darkness), Deafened (sonic), Entangled (earth/water) | Default — duration expires or rest |
| **Verdant Font** | Poisoned, Diseased, Nauseated, Fatigued (biological exhaustion), Parasitized | Often Tenacious (biological processes continue) |
| **Synod** | Exhausted (soul-fatigue), Aged, Frightened (confronting mortality), Weakened (entropic drain) | Inherently Tenacious or Rooted — entropy resists reversal |
| **Hidden Forge** | Stunned (system overload), Dazed (electrical shock), Deafened (sonic), Blinded (flash) | Default or Tenacious (depending on damage to systems) |
| **Palimpsest** | Confused, Frightened (existential), Charmed, Fascinated, Cognitive Dissonance | Variable — some self-resolve, some require specific narrative resolution |

---

# II. INTERMEDIATE ARTS — The Practitioner's Toolkit

These effects require more understanding of how prima æther interacts with the world. A novice might stumble into them. A practitioner wields them with intent.

---

## Healing Umbrella

Effects that restore what has been lost or damaged. Grouped together because they share the restoration principle — the difference is WHAT is being restored.

### Healing (Base Cost 2)

**HP Restoration Across All Four Pillars**

Strictly HP — not conditions, not transformation reversal. Duration from Construction Pool creates healing-over-time effects.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Minimal. Water isotopes (B-05 Abyss, B-06 Vapor) can purify and cleanse — the only Borderlands healing. |
| **Verdant Font** | PRIMARY HOME. Cellular repair, tissue regeneration, immune boost, biological knitting. Every Verdant Font isotope can express Healing in some form. Flora isotopes heal through herbal/botanical medicine. Fauna isotopes heal through biological repair signals. |
| **Synod** | Anti-healing is the Synod's DEFAULT. Healing with a Synod isotope means REVERSING entropy — fighting the plane's fundamental nature. Incredibly difficult and expensive but thematically profound. The chirurgeon who heals using death magic is healing by telling entropy "not yet." |
| **Hidden Forge** | Repair, not healing in the biological sense. Restoring Kan-tech systems, mending constructs, structural restoration. F-16 Forge Mutagen = chemical healing compounds. |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative healing. "The story needs you whole." Probability-adjusted recovery — wounds that should have been fatal weren't because the dice landed right. |

### Panacea (Base Cost 3)

**Condition Reversal — Inverse of Affliction**

Whatever Affliction can impose at a severity, Panacea can reverse. Disease cure. Poison neutralization. Limb regrowth (reversing the Crippled condition). Resurrection = high-rank Panacea (reversing the death condition).

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical condition clearing — unfreeze (reverse Frozen), extinguish (reverse Burning), restore hearing (reverse Deafened from sonic). |
| **Verdant Font** | PRIMARY HOME. Biological condition reversal. Cure disease, neutralize poison, purge parasites, restore biological function. The Verdant Font produces the problem AND the solution. |
| **Synod** | Reversing entropy-based conditions. Extremely difficult but represents the most profound Panacea — undoing what the Synod's nature says should be permanent. Reversing aging, restoring what has been "ended." |
| **Hidden Forge** | System reset. Clear malfunction conditions. Reboot crashed Kan-tech. Debug corrupted programming. |
| **Palimpsest** | Retroactive condition reversal. "That never happened." The condition is removed because the event that caused it is edited out of the local probability. |

---

## Defense Umbrella

Effects that prevent or reduce harm. Grouped from most accessible (numeric reduction) to most exclusive (absolute negation).

### Protection (Base Cost 2)

**Numeric Defense Increases**

Five currencies: Defense Bonus (Dodge/Deflect), Soak, Energy Resistance, Threshold Bonus, Barrier HP. Caster chooses which currency when composing the rote. NEVER reaches absolute — that's Immunity's domain.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical barriers and reinforcement. Stone walls (Earth), ice shields (Water/Air), wind barriers (Air), heat shields (Fire). B-03 Slag self-seals its own fractures. B-09 Bedrock = raw physical mass as defense. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological armor. Bark skin (Flora), chitin plating (Fauna), regenerative tissue response, immune system bolstering. Living armor that repairs itself. |
| **Synod** | Warding against entropy. Preservation fields. Soul shielding. S-07 Reliquary = protective containment. The Synod's protection feels like stasis — the ward doesn't deflect, it prevents change within its boundary. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Engineered defenses. Force fields, circuit-based wards, armored plating, Deepguard ward systems. F-13 Spire = crystalline lattice barriers. F-08 Cyberpunk = integrated defensive augmentation. Deepguard Academy's PRIMARY domain. |
| **Palimpsest** | Probability shields. Attacks "miss" through improbable deflection. Bad outcomes for the target are statistically suppressed. P-09 Casino = pure luck-based defense. P-06 Gambit = calculated risk manipulation. |

### Immunity (Base Cost 4)

**Absolute Negation of a Specific Expression**

Binary — you're immune or you're not. ESL determines breadth: single isotope expression (ESL 4) → isotope category (ESL 6) → planar family (ESL 8) → conceptual immunity (ESL 10). Narrow by design. The Wildpath Wardens teach that Transform is usually a better answer than stacking Immunities.

Natural immunity through biological adaptation (lava fleas, phase hounds) is the same mechanism expressed through evolution rather than spellcraft — generations of exposure to an isotope causing the organism's genome to incorporate the expression as a survival trait.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Elemental immunity. Immune to fire, cold, acid, electricity, or specific physical isotope expressions. Each purchase protects against ONE expression. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological immunity. Disease immunity, poison immunity, immunity to specific creature types or biological attack vectors. |
| **Synod** | Entropic immunity. Immune to aging, immune to specific undead abilities, immune to soul-targeting effects. The most difficult to achieve because you're becoming immune to entropy itself. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological immunity. Immune to specific energy types, immune to system disruption, EMP immunity. |
| **Palimpsest** | Conceptual immunity. Immune to probability manipulation, immune to mind effects, immune to illusion. The rarest and most expensive because you're becoming immune to narrative itself. |

### Immortality (Base Cost 5)

**Permanent Soul-Body Binding Preventing Death from Completing**

Not resurrection (that's high-rank Panacea reversing the death condition). Immortality = permanent soul-binding ensuring the character returns. Dot 1 = single vessel phylactery. Dot 2 = distributed binding (Warden state).

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural to the Borderlands. Physical elements don't address soul mechanics. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological immortality. Cellular renewal loops, telomere restoration, the biological fountain of youth. The body simply never stops repairing. V-15 Titan = hyper-regenerative biological immortality. |
| **Synod** | The Synod's most paradoxical expression. The plane of endings producing non-ending. Lichdom, undeath, soul binding to ossuary vessels. The bureaucracy of entropy granting an exemption. S-04 Necropolis = institutional undeath. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological immortality. Brain-in-jar. Consciousness upload to crystal matrix. Mechanical replacement of failing biology. F-15 Data = digital consciousness preservation. F-08 Cyberpunk = full-body replacement. |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative immortality. "The story needs you." The character persists because the narrative cannot function without them. The Maestro IS this — a being that exists because collective expectation collapsed a permanent superposition state. |

---

## Information Umbrella

Effects dealing with the acquisition, processing, and weaponization of information.

### Senses (Base Cost 2)

**Unified Information Management**

Acquisition, processing, AND dissemination. Absorbs Communication, Comprehend, Remote Sensing. The ESL determines not just range or clarity but the CATEGORY of information gathering: enhanced perception → analytical overlay → remote collection → historical reconstruction → temporal perception → comprehensive intelligence.

The Chronosynclastic Institute achieves ESL 5–7 results through mundane training (Ekman micro-expressions, game theory, statistical modeling) at 0 ÆP. Magic kicks in when mundane analysis isn't enough.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical senses. Darkvision (Earth/Shadow), tremorsense (Earth), echolocation (Air/Sonic), enhanced hearing (Air), thermal vision (Fire). |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological senses. Scent tracking (Fauna), danger sense (predator awareness), pheromone reading, biological threat assessment. |
| **Synod** | Historical/archival senses. Psychometry — reading the Record embedded in objects and locations. Communing with archived souls. S-05 Record = accessing the Synod's archive directly. S-06 Whispers = hearing the past. S-11 Echo = perceiving temporal afterimages. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological senses. Scanner arrays, analytical instruments, data processing, threat detection systems, diagnostic readouts. Deepguard's ward-detection networks. F-15 Data = information processing through crystalline matrices. |
| **Palimpsest** | Probability senses. Precognition, divination, reading fate currents, probability assessment. P-09 Casino = reading the odds. P-10 Prophecy = temporal probability perception. P-03 Nostalgia = perceiving echoes of what was. Chronosynclastic Institute's PRIMARY domain. |

### Mind Link (Base Cost 3)

**Bidirectional Deep Mental Access**

Categorically different from Senses — this is invasion of another mind, not information gathering from the world. Low rank: surface thoughts. Mid rank: implant suggestions, charm. High rank: dominate, memory rewrite. Unity Academy's signature effect.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural to Borderlands. Physical elements don't interface with consciousness. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological telepathy. Pheromone-based communication, hive mind connection, swarm consciousness. V-11 The Hive = distributed consciousness through biological network. |
| **Synod** | Communing with the dead. Accessing souls in transit through the Synod. S-06 Whispers = archived-mind contact. S-12 Possession = spectral entity entering a living mind. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological mind interface. Neural jacks, digital telepathy, encrypted mental channels. F-15 Data = crystal-matrix mind bridging. F-08 Cyberpunk = cybernetic neural link. |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative mind connection. Deep empathic resonance through shared story. P-05 Dream = shared dreamspace. P-08 Horror = forced emotional sharing. Unity Academy uses Palimpsest-adjacent techniques for their Thrallherd network. |

---

## Enhancement Umbrella

Effects that improve or degrade a target's existing capabilities. Mirrored pairs.

### Enhanced Trait (Base Cost 2/3/4) & Weaken (Base Cost 2/3/4)

**Attribute Modification — Mirrored Effects**

Single attribute (2), Pillar (3), Full Suite/negative levels (4). Enhanced Trait and Weaken use the same table with opposite signs.

| Plane | Enhanced Trait Expression | Weaken Expression |
|---|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Elemental empowerment. Stone-hard skin (+Might), wind-fast reflexes (+Finesse), enduring as earth (+Vigor). | Elemental sapping. Waterlogged sluggishness (−Finesse), cold-numbed muscles (−Might), heat exhaustion (−Vigor). |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological optimization. Muscle density increase (+Might), neural acceleration (+Wits), metabolic efficiency (+Vigor). PRIMARY for biological buffs. | Biological degradation. Toxin-induced weakness, disease-depleted stamina, parasitic drain on mental function. |
| **Synod** | "What doesn't end you strengthens you." Empowerment through proximity to entropy. | Soul-level attribute drain. The Synod strips capabilities the way entropy strips heat. Full Suite Weaken (negative levels) is the Synod's natural expression. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Kan-tech augmentation. Cybernetic boosting, exoskeleton support, chemical stimulant injection. | Precision degradation. Equipment malfunction, augmentation failure, system desynchronization. |
| **Palimpsest** | "The hero gets stronger when the stakes are higher." Narrative-driven enhancement. | Probability-weighted penalties. Bad luck accumulation. "Nothing goes right for you." |

---

## Temporal Umbrella

Effects involving the manipulation of time and temporal flow.

### Chronos (Base Cost 2)

**Temporal Flow Manipulation**

Local time dilation/compression. Both perceptual AND actual temporal acceleration can be simultaneously true (Sandevistan model). Haste, slow, aging, preservation. Buff and debuff in one effect. Chronosynclastic Institute's signature.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Minimal natural expression. Physics-based time effects are Synod or Palimpsest territory. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological time. Aging/de-aging, maturation acceleration, hibernation induction, metabolic rate manipulation. V-15 Titan = hyper-accelerated biological processes. |
| **Synod** | Entropy IS time's arrow. The Synod's relationship with Chronos is intimate. Preservation (halt decay), stasis fields, accelerated entropy as "fast-forward." PRIMARY alongside Palimpsest. S-08 Censor = temporal redaction. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Mechanical time. Clockwork precision, synchronized systems, time-delayed activation. F-06 Clockwork = precise temporal measurement and manipulation. |
| **Palimpsest** | Probability-adjusted temporal flow. Temporal perception manipulation. PRIMARY alongside Synod. P-01 Loop = time loops. P-10 Prophecy = future-reading through temporal probability. The Chronosynclastic Institute operates at the Palimpsest-Synod interface. |

### Time Travel (Base Cost 5)

**Temporal Displacement Through Deep Palimpsest**

Three modes: Loop (round trip, fixed endpoints), Rethread (one-way, adding to the tapestry), Loose Thread (serial travel, origin must be anchored). Temporal wall at the Calamity — cannot navigate pre-Calamity.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural. |
| **Verdant Font** | Not natural. |
| **Synod** | PARTIAL. The Synod IS the museum of Setting C history. Accessing past through temporal Synod navigation. Read-only — you can observe but the Synod preserves, it doesn't change. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Not natural. |
| **Palimpsest** | PRIMARY and ONLY home for actual temporal displacement. All Time Travel navigates through the Palimpsest using Synod archives as reference points. |

---

## Spatial Umbrella

Effects involving translocation and spatial manipulation.

### Teleport (Base Cost 3)

**Same-Plane Translocation by Skimming the Palimpsest Surface**

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural — physical elements don't fold space. |
| **Verdant Font** | Not natural. Mycelial network traversal (V-09) is Movement through a biological medium, not true teleportation. |
| **Synod** | Partial — spectral shortcuts through the Synod's geography. S-09 Wraith = phase-skip through spectral space. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Partial — engineered teleportation grids, Kan-tech beaming technology. F-05 Grid = network-routed instant transit. |
| **Palimpsest** | PRIMARY home. Teleportation IS Palimpsest surface navigation. Translocation Geometry. Every teleport skims the Palimpsest briefly. |

### Dimensional Travel (Base Cost 4)

**Interplanar Transit Through Deep Palimpsest**

Dot 1: controlled aperture, Material ↔ Palimpsest + Translocation Geometry shortcuts. Dot 2: full interplanar transit. Planar incursions = Palimpsest stretching thin (the inverse of Create's Palimpsest pinching).

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural. |
| **Verdant Font** | Not natural. |
| **Synod** | Partial — accessing the Synod directly as a destination. S-12 Possession = crossing the spectral boundary. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Partial — Forge-built portal infrastructure. Dimensional engineering. |
| **Palimpsest** | PRIMARY home. All interplanar transit navigates through the Palimpsest. The Palimpsest IS the medium between planes. |

---

## Creation Umbrella

Effects that produce something that didn't exist before.

### Create (Base Cost 2)

**Form Objects or Spaces from Prima Æther**

Physical objects and Palimpsest folds (extradimensional spaces) from the same effect. ESL determines volume/complexity for spaces, complexity/durability for objects.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical object creation. Stone walls, ice structures, metal objects, earthworks. PRIMARY for physical objects. The isotope determines the material. B-09 Bedrock = create stone. B-15 Frost = create ice. B-05 Abyss = create water. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological creation. Grow plants, create organic structures, produce biological compounds. V-01 World Tree = grow wooden structures. V-04 Overgrowth = rapid vegetation creation. |
| **Synod** | Paradoxical creation. Creating a vessel for entropy — undead constructs, necromantic structures, preserved spaces. S-04 Necropolis = create structures of interred material. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological fabrication. Manufacture Kan-tech, build constructs, engineer solutions. Daedalon's domain. F-13 Spire = crystal-lattice construction. |
| **Palimpsest** | Spatial creation. Palimpsest folds — extradimensional spaces, demiplanes. PRIMARY for spaces. Every Bag of Holding, Rope Trick, and demiplane is a Palimpsest fold. The fold has a LOCATION in the Palimpsest accessible from the Palimpsest side. |

### Move Object (Base Cost 2)

**Telekinesis**

Move, lift, and manipulate objects at distance. ESL determines weight capacity.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical telekinesis. Air currents lifting objects (B-13), magnetic force (B-14 Current), gravitational manipulation (B-09). B-02 Steam = pressure as motive force (the steam engine principle). |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological telekinesis. Vine grasping, prehensile appendage extension, symbiotic organism manipulation. |
| **Synod** | Spectral telekinesis. Poltergeist activity. S-09 Wraith = ghostly manipulation. Objects moved by invisible spectral force. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Mechanical telekinesis. Tractor beams, magnetic cranes, force projectors, remote-operated manipulators. |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative telekinesis. Things move because the probability of their current position becomes zero. "The book was always going to fall off the shelf." |

---

## Perception Manipulation Umbrella

### Illusion (Base Cost 2 Single Sense / 3 Multi-Sensory)

**Palimpsest Superposition Collapse**

Pull potential states from the Palimpsest and partially collapse them into material reality. ESL determines collapse percentage: 5% (ghostly) → 50% (quasi-real) → 100% (fully real) → >100% (hyper-real, MORE anchored than baseline reality). Succeeding on save against >100% causes Metaphysical track damage. Chiaroscuro Institute's signature effect.

Absorbs Concealment — invisibility is an illusion that replaces visual presence with empty space.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Minimal natural expression — physical elements are maximally real. Exceptions: B-04 Scorch = light/heat shimmer distortion. B-15 Frost = frost shimmer, mirage effects. B-10 Shadow (Synod crossover) = darkness as visual denial. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological illusion. Camouflage, mimicry, bioluminescent deception, pheromone-based false signals. Natural creatures use biological illusion constantly — the Verdant Font invented deception before sapient beings existed. |
| **Synod** | Shadow/spectral illusion. Ghostly images, death's visage, afterimages of the dead. S-10 Shadow = darkness-based concealment. S-11 Echo = temporal afterimage displayed as present-tense illusion. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Holographic illusion. Projected images, technological deception, SimStim entertainment. F-14 Lens = focused light projection. F-15 Data = digital illusion, virtual reality. |
| **Palimpsest** | PRIMARY home. Illusion IS Palimpsest superposition collapse. Every illusion is pulling a potential state from the Palimpsest and partially collapsing it. P-05 Dream = dream-stuff made quasi-real. P-06 Gambit = bluff made real. P-07 Fey Logic = internally consistent illogic that functions because you accept the premise. P-16 Simulation = full-scale reality simulation. |

---

# III. ADVANCED ARTS — The Specialist's Domain

These effects require deep understanding of specific magical principles. They're not just harder to cast — they require a fundamentally different relationship with the prima æther.

---

## Counter-Magic

### Nullify (Base Cost 3)

**Magical Neutralization Reaction**

Introduce an incompatible magical expression to the target spell's concordance structure. The two react (acid-base chemistry model). The structured lattice of the target and the disruptive expression cancel each other out, producing inert residue and released energy. The released energy scales with the target's ESL — dispelling a cantrip produces a puff of warmth; breaking a Deepguard ward releases a pressurized burst.

Against Structured Current (Architect) spells: attack the concordance lattice, introduce turbulence, collapse the rigid structure.
Against Primal Current (Improviser) spells: impose unwanted structure on the chaotic flow, crystallize until it shatters.
Against natural phenomena: significantly harder — no caster maintaining it, the flow IS the environment.

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Counter elemental expressions with opposing elements. Fire nullifies with water-aspected disruption (or vice versa). Earth-based grounding dissipates electrical expressions. Classical elemental opposition. |
| **Verdant Font** | Counter biological/organic magic. Antivenom, antibiotic, immune response. The body's own Nullify mechanism IS the immune system. V-12 Plague (ironic) = weaponized immune response against foreign magical biology. |
| **Synod** | Counter necromantic/entropic expressions. Turn Undead is a Nullify expression through the Synod. S-08 Censor = archival redaction of magical Records. Inherently good at stripping duration because entropy is the Synod's native tool. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Counter Kan-tech. Deepguard Academy's specialty. Disabling wards, disrupting engineered magical systems, EMP-equivalent effects against magical technology. F-07 Galvanism = electrical disruption of magical circuits. |
| **Palimpsest** | Counter illusions, probability manipulation, and narrative effects. Restore baseline reality by collapsing artificial superposition states back to default. P-14 Paradox = self-contradicting logic that unravels constructed narratives. P-13 Error = induce reality errors in the target expression. |

---

## Summoning

### Summon (Base Cost 3)

**Condense Prima Æther Into a Temporary Entity**

The caster shapes raw prima æther through an isotope expression into a temporary being. The summoned creature is a magical expression given a body — the same grammar that Create uses for objects, applied to a living template. Its character sheet uses the same engine as a player's, powered by the caster's ÆP and maintained by their Mastery Capacity Pool.

ESL caps the Creature Mastery Level (CML) of the summoned entity. The creature's abilities ARE spell sentence expressions baked into its temporary character sheet. A summoned fire elemental's burn attack IS a Damage rote. Its fire immunity IS an Immunity expression. Its flight IS Movement. The creature is a walking, temporary bundle of linked rotes.

This is fundamentally different from Binding (Path of Contracts), where you establish a magical agreement with an EXISTING creature that has its own independent character sheet, history, and will.

| Plane | What Condenses |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Elemental constructs. Beings of pure physical element — fire elementals, earth elementals, water elementals, air elementals, and compound-isotope hybrids (steam mephits, magma elementals). The most common and mechanically straightforward summons. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological constructs. Magical beasts, nature spirits, animate plants, swarm entities. These feel more "alive" than Borderlands summons because the Verdant Font's nature is biological process. V-05 Hunt = predatory beast summon. V-01 World Tree = animate plant guardian. V-11 Hive = swarm summon. |
| **Synod** | Spectral/undead entities. Animate dead, call spectral servitors, manifest archived souls temporarily. The Synod doesn't "create" undead — it calls patterns from the archive and gives them temporary expression. S-09 Wraith = spectral entity. S-01 Grave = skeletal construct. S-04 Necropolis = mass undead animation. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Kan-tech constructs. Activate dormant constructs, assemble drones from ambient Forge energy, fabricate temporary golems. F-06 Clockwork = precision mechanical servant. F-08 Cyberpunk = advanced synthetic entity. The Sōmaturge's Forge Construct lives here. |
| **Palimpsest** | Probability entities. Beings that COULD exist, collapsed from the Palimpsest's superposition into temporary reality. These are the strangest summons — creatures from probability space that have no natural history, no species, no ecology. They exist because the math says they should. P-07 Fey Logic = entities operating on dream-logic. P-13 Error = things that shouldn't exist but do because of reality malfunction. The Maestro was, in a sense, a permanent Palimpsest Summon. |

---

## Translocation (Advanced)

### Teleport (Base Cost 3)

See Spatial Umbrella above. Palimpsest-primary.

### Dimensional Travel (Base Cost 4)

See Spatial Umbrella above. Palimpsest-primary.

### Time Travel (Base Cost 5)

See Temporal Umbrella above. Palimpsest-primary via Synod navigation.

---

# IV. MASTER ARTS — The Pinnacle of Mortal Magic

These effects represent the absolute ceiling of what mortal practitioners can achieve. They're not just expensive — they touch cosmic mechanisms.

---

### Immortality (Base Cost 5)

See Defense Umbrella above.

### Variable (Rank × 2)

**Bounded Replicator — The Ultimate Flexibility**

Variable produces any known effect on demand. BER = Rank × 2 (the only effect where BER scales). Output identical to the replicated effect at that ESL. Constraint: can only replicate effects the caster has ≥1 dot in. Variable bypasses isotope requirement — the ultimate generalist tool.

Praxis Collegium's signature. Overt (Scholar's Gambit) = flexibility as philosophy. Covert (Fulcrum) = learns effects specifically to deconstruct them.

Variable has no isotope home — it IS the home for all isotopes. A Variable user channels whatever isotope the replicated effect requires, drawn from their knowledge base. This is why the Praxis Collegium is the spy agency with the gadget bag — they can produce any tool for any situation because Variable lets them access any effect they've studied.

---

# V. UNIVERSAL NOTES

## Effects With No Isotope Home
- **Variable** — explicitly bypasses isotope requirements. No planar home.
- **Feature** — expressed through the character's deepest affinity. Every isotope produces Feature.

## Effects With a Single Primary Home
- **Teleport, Dimensional Travel, Time Travel** — Palimpsest primary. Other planes provide partial support at best.
- **Illusion** — Palimpsest primary (superposition collapse). Other planes produce limited illusion through their native mechanisms.

## Effects Natural to ALL Planes
- **Damage** — every plane can produce harm through its native expressions.
- **Affliction** — every plane can impose conditions through its native mechanisms.
- **Protection** — every plane offers defensive expressions.
- **Senses** — every plane offers information through its native channels.
- **Transform** — every plane can alter what things are through its native processes.
- **Movement** — every plane offers mobility through its native media.

## The Smoothing Effect Note

All magic in Tessera operates within the prima æther — the ongoing byproduct of the Calamity's neutralization reaction between the Primal Current (Anima's Law) and the Structured Current (Kanon's Law). No spell, no matter how powerful, operates outside this framework. The Smoothing Effect maintains the coherence of Setting C as a unified reality. Effects that could theoretically expose the seams (high-ESL Nullify suppressing the Smoothing Effect locally, >100% Illusion exceeding baseline reality's fidelity) are monitored by the Generalist College and, at cosmic scale, by Aíon directly.

# Ætherium Codex — Effect & Isotope Integration Reference (Unified)
## Complete Effect Mapping: Planar Homes, Scaling, and Application

**Date:** April 9, 2026
**Version:** 2.0 — Reorganized by frequency of use, tiered by mastery
**Prerequisites:** AC_SpellSentence_Revision_Session2.md, all five plane isotope SRD documents

---

# HOW TO READ THIS DOCUMENT

**Effect ordering:** Most-to-least frequently used in play. Combat effects first, meta-effects last.

**Category structure:** Each effect entry contains:
1. Core definition and base cost
2. Scaling table (where applicable)
3. Planar expression map (all five planes, consistent order)
4. Tier breakdown within each plane: Novice (ESL 1–4), Journeyman (ESL 5–7), Master (ESL 8–10)

**Plane order (consistent throughout):**
1. Borderlands — The Physical Register (inorganic chemistry)
2. Verdant Font — The Biological Register (organic chemistry)
3. Synod of Endings — The Entropic Register (thermodynamics)
4. Hidden Forge — The Technological Register (catalysis/process chemistry)
5. Palimpsest — The Narrative Register (statistical mechanics/equilibrium)

**Phase order within each plane (consistent throughout):** Solid → Liquid → Gas → Plasma

| Phase | Borderlands | Verdant Font | Synod | Hidden Forge | Palimpsest |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Solid** | Earth (B-09) | Flora (V-01) | Osseous (S-01) | Crystal (F-13) | Echo (P-01) |
| **Liquid** | Water (B-05) | Fauna (V-05) | Archival (S-05) | Chem (F-09) | Veil (P-05) |
| **Gas** | Air (B-13) | Myco (V-09) | Spectral (S-09) | Pneuma (F-01) | Probability (P-09) |
| **Plasma** | Fire (B-01) | Viral (V-13) | Void (S-13) | Volt (F-05) | Glitch (P-13) |

**Terminology:**
- **Soak** (not DR) — flat damage reduction per hit
- **TN** (Target Number, not DC) — the threshold for saves and checks
- **Synod of Endings** — the bureaucracy of entropy and the archive of everything that has ended. NOT "the plane of death."
- **Verdant Font** — the engine of biological process: growth, decay, adaptation, mutation. Cancer is as much Verdant Font as healing. NOT "the plane of life."
- **Palimpsest** — the connective tissue between all planes, with its own terrain, ecology, and native inhabitants. NOT a featureless void.
- **Prima Æther** — the ambient magical field; the ongoing byproduct of the Calamity's cosmic neutralization reaction between the Primal Current (Anima's Law) and the Structured Current (Kanon's Law).
- **BER** — Base Effect Rank. Static cost representing pool footprint.
- **ESL** — Effective Spell Level. Final Rank = BER + Additives − Subtractives, capped by min(Effect Tier, Isotope Tier, Mastery).
- **ÆP** — Æther Points. Casting cost = (ESL × 2) − 1.

---

# I. COMBAT CORE

The effects every practitioner encounters first and uses most. Combat is where magic meets consequence — these are the verbs of harm, condition, defense, and restoration that define every encounter.

---

## Damage (Base Cost 1)

**Direct harm across any pillar. The most immediate magical expression.**

Channel prima æther through an isotope to produce destructive output. The isotope determines damage type and die size band. The ESL determines dice count. Damage targets the Physical pillar by default; targeting other pillars (Mental, Social, Metaphysical) requires specific isotope selection.

### Scaling Table

| ESL | d4 (floor) | d6 (standard) | d8 (ceiling) | d10 (specialized) | d12 (legendary) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1d4 | 1d6 | 1d8 | 1d10 | 1d12 |
| 2 | 2d4 | 2d6 | 2d8 | 2d10 | 2d12 |
| 3 | 3d4 | 3d6 | 3d8 | 3d10 | 3d12 |
| 4 | 5d4 | 5d6 | 5d8 | 5d10 | 5d12 |
| 5 | 7d4 | 7d6 | 7d8 | 7d10 | 7d12 |
| 6 | 9d4 | 9d6 | 9d8 | 9d10 | 9d12 |
| 7 | 11d4 | 11d6 | 11d8 | 11d10 | 11d12 |
| 8 | 13d4 | 13d6 | 13d8 | 13d10 | 13d12 |
| 9 | 15d4 | 15d6 | 15d8 | 15d10 | 15d12 |
| 10 | 18d4 | 18d6 | 18d8 | 18d10 | 18d12 |

Die size determined by isotope material properties (Tensile Strength). Floor (d4) through ceiling (d12) = the band. Each isotope sits at a specific position within its band.

### Planar Expressions

#### Borderlands — Physical Destruction (PRIMARY HOME)

Every Borderlands isotope can produce Damage as a natural expression. This is the physical register — energy converted directly into destructive force.

**Novice (ESL 1–4):**
- B-01 Inferno (Plasma): Combustion on contact. Consuming fire. Self-sustaining as long as fuel exists.
- B-04 Scorch (Plasma+Gas): Ranged radiant heat without combustion. Flash-burn at distance. Bypasses cover.
- B-05 The Abyss (Liquid): Crushing pressure and hypothermia. Drowning.
- B-08 Hail (Liquid+Gas): Blunt cold projectiles. Impact + cold simultaneously.
- B-09 The Bedrock (Solid): Raw mass impact. Bludgeoning, crushing, burial.
- B-11 Acid (Solid+Liquid): Corrosive dissolution. Persistent chemical damage.
- B-13 The Void Sky (Gas): Vacuum, depressurization, suffocation. Concussive sonic.
- B-14 Current (Gas+Plasma): Electrical discharge through atmosphere. Lightning.

**Journeyman (ESL 5–7):**
- B-02 Steam (Plasma+Liquid): Scalding penetrating burns. Pressure damage that permeates sealed protection.
- B-03 Slag (Plasma+Solid): Adhesive molten mass. Both thermal and bludgeoning simultaneously. Sticks and keeps burning.
- B-07 Mire (Liquid+Solid): Entangling suffocation. Viscous crushing.
- B-10 Magma (Solid+Plasma): Superheated earth. Adhesive weight with thermal component.
- B-15 Frost (Gas+Liquid): Flash-freezing cold snap. Crystalline shattering.
- B-16 Erosion (Gas+Solid): Abrasive particulate stripping. Sustained surface destruction.

**Master (ESL 8–10):**
- Any Borderlands isotope at maximum intensity. B-01 at Stage 8–10 = self-sustaining consumption field that melts stone. B-09 at maximum = tectonic-scale mass impact. B-13 at maximum = total atmospheric removal.
- Compound isotopes at extreme stages produce effects that blur elemental boundaries — a Stage 10 B-03 Slag is simultaneously fire, earth, and the concept of "molten destruction" itself.

#### Verdant Font — Biological Harm

Biological damage works through the target's own systems — poison, disease, parasitic invasion, allergic cascade. Often slower than Borderlands damage but harder to treat because the damage vector is INSIDE the target.

**Novice (ESL 1–4):**
- V-01 World Tree (Solid): Crushing mass of ancient wood. Piercing thorns. Strangling constriction.
- V-02 Carnivorous (Solid+Liquid): Predatory plant attack. Digestive acid. Snapping jaws.
- V-05 The Hunt (Liquid): Predatory physical damage. Bite, claw, venom injection.
- V-06 Sylvan (Liquid+Solid): Nature-red-in-tooth-and-claw. Combined predator/plant attack vectors.

**Journeyman (ESL 5–7):**
- V-03 The Spore (Solid+Gas): Respiratory invasion. Spore clouds that damage from inside.
- V-07 Cordyceps (Liquid+Gas): Parasitic invasion through biological vector. The damage IS the infection.
- V-09 The Mycelium (Gas): Network-based decomposition. Breaking down living tissue through distributed fungal action.
- V-11 The Hive (Gas+Liquid): Swarm damage. Death by a thousand small organisms.
- V-12 Plague (Gas+Plasma): Weaponized pathogen. Mass biological harm.

**Master (ESL 8–10):**
- V-04 Overgrowth (Solid+Plasma): Destructive uncontrolled growth THROUGH the target. Plants growing inside a body.
- V-08 Chimera (Liquid+Plasma): Hybrid biological attack using multiple vectors simultaneously.
- V-13 The Cancer (Plasma): Uncontrolled cellular reproduction destroying tissue from within. The target's own biology becomes the weapon.
- V-15 Titan (Plasma+Liquid): Hyper-growth biological overload. The body cannot contain the energy being channeled.
- V-16 Mutagen (Plasma+Gas): Forced mutation as damage vector. The target is harmed by being changed.

#### Synod of Endings — Entropic Damage

The Synod does not deal "death damage." It deals ENTROPY. Things touched by Synod damage don't die — they END. Death is a transition in the soul cycle. Ending is the cessation of a process. Synod damage degrades the target's structural coherence — physical, mental, social, or metaphysical.

**Novice (ESL 1–4):**
- S-01 The Grave (Solid): Structural collapse into final rest. Dessication, calcification. Brittle destruction.
- S-05 The Record (Liquid): Overwriting existing patterns with archived ones. The target's current state is replaced by an "ended" version from the archive.
- S-09 The Wraith (Gas): Spectral contact draining vitality. Soul-touching damage that bypasses physical defenses.
- S-10 Shadow (Gas+Solid): Damage through darkness contact. Cold absence of energy.

**Journeyman (ESL 5–7):**
- S-02 The Ossuary (Solid+Liquid): Archived remains weaponized. Bone shards carrying entropic charge.
- S-03 The Husk (Solid+Gas): Hollowing out. Leaving empty shell. The target's substance is removed, leaving form without content.
- S-06 Whispers (Liquid+Gas): Information as corrosive agent. Secrets that damage the listener's pattern.
- S-11 The Echo (Gas+Liquid): Hit by something that already happened. Temporal afterimage dealing damage.
- S-14 Decay (Plasma+Solid): Accelerated decomposition. Entropy fast-forwarded.

**Master (ESL 8–10):**
- S-04 Necropolis (Solid+Plasma): Mass structural termination. Everything in the area ENDS.
- S-08 The Censor (Liquid+Plasma): Selective deletion of the target's properties. Surgical entropic removal.
- S-12 Possession (Gas+Plasma): Damage from internal spiritual invasion. A spectral entity entering and destroying from within.
- S-13 Oblivion (Plasma): Pure entropic negation. Damage through existence-denial. The target is harmed by being told it doesn't exist.
- S-15 Hollow (Plasma+Gas): Consuming internal substance. The target retains its form but loses everything inside.
- S-16 Amnesia (Plasma+Liquid): Destroying the target's connection to their own pattern. Metaphysical HP (Pattern) damage.

#### Hidden Forge — Technological Weaponry

Engineered damage. Precise, efficient, often requiring specific infrastructure or materials. The Forge produces designed weapon effects, not raw elemental destruction.

**Novice (ESL 1–4):**
- F-01 The Engine (Gas): Mechanical force projection. Pneumatic impact. Concussive pressure.
- F-05 The Grid (Plasma): Network-routed electrical damage. Lightning channeled through infrastructure.
- F-09 The Vat (Liquid): Chemical burns. Alchemical weapon effects. Corrosive compounds.
- F-13 The Spire (Solid): Crystalline cutting edges. Focused resonant shattering.

**Journeyman (ESL 5–7):**
- F-02 Steampunk (Gas+Plasma): Pressure-based weaponry enhanced by electrical charge.
- F-03 Diesel (Gas+Liquid): Combustion-engine driven impact. Chemical-propelled projectiles.
- F-07 Galvanism (Plasma+Liquid): Electrically animated projectiles. Chemical-electrical hybrid weapons.
- F-10 Hydraulic (Liquid+Gas): Pressure-based destruction. Water-cutter equivalent.
- F-14 Lens (Solid+Gas): Focused energy amplification. Crystal lens concentrating force into a beam.

**Master (ESL 8–10):**
- F-04 Æther-Tech (Gas+Solid): Magical-mechanical hybrid weapons. Kan-tech at its most advanced.
- F-08 Cyberpunk (Plasma+Solid): Integrated weapon systems. Full technological augmentation weaponized.
- F-11 Battery (Liquid+Plasma): Stored energy discharge. Massive accumulated charge released at once.
- F-12 Mana-Punk (Liquid+Solid): Magical-chemical explosive reactions. Alchemical WMD territory.
- F-15 Data (Solid+Plasma): Information overload as damage vector. Neural disruption through crystalline-electrical interface.
- F-16 Forge Mutagen (Solid+Liquid): Chemical-crystal hybrid weaponization. The Forge's equivalent of biological mutation.

#### Palimpsest — Probability Damage

The Palimpsest doesn't produce physical energy. It produces improbable outcomes weaponized. Damage occurs through events that SHOULDN'T have happened but did. At high ESL this becomes reality editing — the target is damaged because local reality was briefly rewritten to include their injury.

**Novice (ESL 1–4):**
- P-01 The Loop (Solid): Trapped in a damage cycle. The same injury echoing.
- P-05 Dream (Liquid): Nightmare made manifest. Dream-damage that persists into waking.
- P-09 Casino (Gas): Pure probability manipulation. The target's luck turns lethal — they trip into a blade, their shield cracks at the wrong moment.
- P-13 Error (Plasma): Fundamental reality malfunction. The target's HP was always lower than recorded.

**Journeyman (ESL 5–7):**
- P-02 The Haunt (Solid+Liquid): Damage from persistent echoes of past violence at a location.
- P-06 Gambit (Liquid+Gas): Calculated risk weaponized. The target's own decisions become the attack vector.
- P-08 Horror (Liquid+Solid): Fear made physically destructive. Social/Mental pillar damage through pure dread.
- P-10 Prophecy (Gas+Solid): Foreknowledge weaponized. The attack hits because you knew it would.
- P-11 Karma (Gas+Liquid): Accumulated consequence delivered all at once.

**Master (ESL 8–10):**
- P-03 Nostalgia (Solid+Gas): Longing made physically harmful. Metaphysical damage through unresolvable desire.
- P-04 Corrupt (Solid+Plasma): Echo degradation. The target's pattern corrupts from accumulated temporal stress.
- P-07 Fey Logic (Liquid+Plasma): Damage from internally consistent illogic. Hurt by a paradox.
- P-12 Chaos (Gas+Plasma): Random destructive events clustered impossibly on the target.
- P-14 Paradox (Plasma+Solid): Self-contradicting damage that can't be logically resolved. The wound contradicts itself.
- P-15 Hazard (Plasma+Gas): Accumulated small errors cascading into catastrophic failure.
- P-16 Simulation (Plasma+Liquid): Damage from the revelation that local reality is less stable than assumed. Metaphysical track damage through Cognitive Dissonance.

---

## Affliction (Base Cost 1–5 by Subcategory)

**Impose discrete conditions or cause condition track movement.**

Subcategorized by severity — the BER IS the condition's severity price. No lookup table needed. The spell sentence names the specific condition being imposed. Recovery conditions are Additives.

### Severity Tiers

| Subcategory | BER | Example Conditions |
|---|---|---|
| Trivial | 1 | Off-Balance, Hampered |
| Minor | 2 | Bleeding, Dazed, Fatigued, Flat-Footed |
| Moderate | 3 | Blinded, Deafened, Nauseated, Snared, Staggered |
| Severe | 4 | Confused, Restrained, Stunned |
| Critical | 5 | Helpless, Paralyzed, Petrified |

### Track Movement

| Steps Imposed | BER |
|---|---|
| 1 step on specified pillar | 2 |
| 2 steps | 3 |
| 3 steps | 4 |
| 4 steps | 5 |

### Recovery Additives

| Recovery | Cost | Method |
|---|---|---|
| Default | +0 | Duration expires or rest |
| Tenacious Recovery | +1 | Requires skill check or treatment |
| Rooted Recovery | +2 | Requires magical intervention |
| Bound Recovery | +3 | Requires specific narrative condition |

### Planar Affliction Profiles

Every plane can produce Afflictions. The isotope determines the condition's FLAVOR and often carries inherent recovery characteristics.

| Plane | Common Afflictions | Inherent Recovery Tendency |
|---|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Burning (Fire), Frozen (Cold/Water), Blinded (Light/Darkness), Deafened (Sonic/Air), Entangled (Earth/Water) | Default — duration expires or rest |
| **Verdant Font** | Bleeding (Fauna), Diseased (Myco/Viral), Fatigued (biological exhaustion), Nauseated (toxin), Poisoned (Flora/Fauna) | Tenacious — biological processes continue after casting ends |
| **Synod** | Exhausted (soul-fatigue), Frightened (confronting mortality), Weakened (entropic drain) | Rooted or Bound — entropy resists reversal. Synod Afflictions are inherently hard to remove. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Blinded (flash), Dazed (electrical shock), Deafened (sonic overload), Stunned (system overload) | Default or Tenacious depending on whether physical systems were damaged |
| **Palimpsest** | Charmed (narrative compulsion), Confused (Cognitive Dissonance), Fascinated (superposition fixation), Frightened (existential horror) | Variable — some self-resolve when the narrative shifts, some require specific narrative resolution |

---

## Protection (Base Cost 2)

**Numeric defense increases. Never reaches absolute — that's Immunity's domain.**

Five currencies: Defense Bonus (Dodge/Deflect increases), Soak, Energy Resistance, Threshold Bonus, Barrier HP. Caster chooses which currency when composing the rote. The ESL determines the magnitude.

### Scaling Table

| ESL | Defense Bonus | Soak | Energy Resistance | Threshold Bonus | Barrier HP |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | +1 | — | — | +1 | — |
| 2 | +2 | — | 5 | +1 | — |
| 3 | +3 | 5 | 10 | +2 | 10 |
| 4 | +4 | 5 | 15 | +2 | 20 |
| 5 | +5 | 10 | 20 | +3 | 40 |
| 6 | +6 | 10 | 25 | +4 | 60 |
| 7 | +7 | 15 | 30 | +5 | 80 |
| 8 | +8 | 15 | 100 | +6 | 100 |
| 9 | +9 | 20 | 200 | +7 | 200 |
| 10 | +10 | 20 | 300 | +8 | 300 |

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Protection Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical barriers. Stone walls (Earth), ice shields (Water/Air), wind barriers (Air), heat shields (Fire). B-03 Slag self-seals fractures. B-09 Bedrock = raw physical mass. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological armor. Bark skin (Flora), chitin plating (Fauna), regenerative tissue response, immune bolstering. Living protection that repairs itself. |
| **Synod** | Entropy warding. Preservation fields that prevent change within their boundary. Soul shielding. S-07 Reliquary = protective containment. The Synod's protection doesn't deflect — it prevents change. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Engineered defenses. Force fields, circuit-based wards, armored plating, Deepguard ward systems. Deepguard Academy's PRIMARY domain. F-08 Cyberpunk = integrated augmented defense. F-13 Spire = crystalline lattice barriers. |
| **Palimpsest** | Probability shields. Attacks "miss" through improbable deflection. Bad outcomes statistically suppressed. P-06 Gambit = calculated risk manipulation. P-09 Casino = pure luck defense. |

---

## Healing (Base Cost 2)

**HP restoration across all four pillars. Strictly HP — not conditions, not transformation reversal.**

Duration from Construction Pool creates healing-over-time effects. Regeneration is merged here — HoTs are Healing + duration, not a separate effect.

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Healing Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Minimal. Water isotopes (B-05 Abyss, B-06 Vapor) can purify and cleanse — the only Borderlands healing. Elements break things; they don't fix them. |
| **Verdant Font** | PRIMARY HOME. Cellular repair, tissue regeneration, immune boost, biological knitting. Every Verdant Font isotope can express Healing. Flora = herbal medicine. Fauna = biological repair signals. Myco = symbiotic healing fungi. Even Viral (V-13 Cancer) can heal — hyper-cellular reproduction is what wound healing IS at the cellular level. |
| **Synod** | Anti-healing is the Synod's default. Healing with a Synod isotope means REVERSING entropy — telling time's arrow "not yet." Incredibly difficult, profoundly expensive, and among the most impressive expressions of mastery in the setting. The chirurgeon who heals using entropy magic is the Synod's rarest practitioner. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Repair, not biological healing. Restoring Kan-tech systems, mending constructs, structural restoration. F-16 Forge Mutagen = chemical healing compounds. The Forge fixes machines; the Verdant Font fixes bodies. |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative healing. "The story needs you whole." Probability-adjusted recovery. Wounds that should have been fatal weren't because the outcome was statistically adjusted. |

---

# II. ENHANCEMENT & TACTICAL

Effects that modify existing capabilities or the flow of combat. Less immediately dramatic than Combat Core but equally essential to every encounter.

---

## Movement (Base Cost 1)

**Grant or enhance ANY movement mode. One effect, all media.**

The isotope determines the movement type (land, swim, burrow, climb, flight). The ESL determines speed. Magic is the engine. Low ESL air movement requires forward momentum (ekranoplan effect). Mid ESL unlocks hover. High ESL = full three-dimensional authority at any speed including zero.

### Scaling Table

| ESL | Speed | Real-World Equivalent |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | +10 ft | Fast walk |
| 2 | 30 ft / ~20 mph | Sprint |
| 3 | 60 ft / ~40 mph | Horse gallop |
| 4 | 120 ft / ~80 mph | Highway speed |
| 5 | 250 ft / ~170 mph | Hurricane |
| 6 | 500 ft / ~340 mph | Subsonic aircraft |
| 7 | 750 ft / ~510 mph | Commercial jet |
| 8 | 2000+ ft / ~1300+ mph | Hypersonic |
| 9 | Physics stops applying | Magic sustaining structural integrity |
| 10 | Speed Force | Moving outside normal reference frames |

### Planar Movement Modes

| Plane | Solid (Burrow) | Liquid (Swim) | Gas (Flight/Air Speed) | Plasma (Propulsion) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Earth tunneling, crystal boring | Deep-pressure swimming, surface running | Wind-riding, aerial movement | Rocket thrust, thermal lift, explosive acceleration |
| **Verdant Font** | Root-network traversal | Animal-speed pursuit, aquatic predator forms | Spore-drift, mycelial network hop | Hyper-accelerated cellular burst speed |
| **Synod** | Grave-walk through interred ground | Following the Record's archived routes | Ghostly passage, shadow-step | Distance-negation through void |
| **Hidden Forge** | Rail systems, crystalline resonance paths | Chemical propulsion, hydraulic systems | Pneumatic/jet propulsion, compressed-air systems | Electromagnetic propulsion, maglev, lightning-rail |
| **Palimpsest** | Walk paths that used to exist | Dream-walk through subjective space | Be where you're statistically most needed | Spatial error — suddenly somewhere else |

---

## Enhanced Trait (Base Cost 2/3/4) & Weaken (Base Cost 2/3/4)

**Attribute modification — mirrored effects.**

Single attribute (BER 2), Pillar (BER 3), Full Suite / negative levels (BER 4). Same table, opposite signs.

### Scaling Table

| ESL | Single (BER 2) | Pillar (BER 3) | Full Suite (BER 4) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | ±1 | — | — |
| 3 | ±1 | ±1 | — |
| 4 | ±2 | ±1 | — |
| 5 | ±2 | ±1 | ±1 |
| 6 | ±3 | ±2 | ±1 |
| 7 | ±3 | ±2 | ±1 |
| 8 | ±4 | ±2 | ±2 |
| 9 | ±4 | ±3 | ±2 |
| 10 | ±5 | ±3 | ±2 |

Magnitude only. Duration, persistence, targeting, progressive application handled separately through Construction Pool and Additives.

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Enhancement | Weakening |
|---|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Elemental empowerment — stone-hard skin (+Might), wind-fast reflexes (+Finesse), enduring as earth (+Vigor) | Elemental sapping — cold-numbed muscles (−Might), waterlogged sluggishness (−Finesse), heat exhaustion (−Vigor) |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological optimization — muscle density (+Might), neural acceleration (+Wits), metabolic efficiency (+Vigor). PRIMARY for biological buffs. | Biological degradation — toxin weakness, disease depletion, parasitic drain |
| **Synod** | "What doesn't end you strengthens you." Empowerment through proximity to entropy. Rare but thematically profound. | Soul-level attribute drain. Full Suite Weaken (negative levels) is the Synod's natural expression. Entropy strips capability the way it strips heat. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Kan-tech augmentation — cybernetic boosting, exoskeleton support, chemical stimulants | Precision degradation — equipment malfunction, augmentation failure, system desynchronization |
| **Palimpsest** | "The hero gets stronger when the stakes are higher." Narrative-driven enhancement. | Probability-weighted penalties. Bad luck accumulation. "Nothing goes right." |

---

## Chronos (Base Cost 2)

**Temporal flow manipulation — local time dilation and compression.**

Both perceptual AND actual temporal acceleration can be simultaneously true (Sandevistan model). Haste, slow, aging, preservation. Buff and debuff in one effect. Chronosynclastic Institute's signature.

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Minimal natural expression. Physics-based time effects are Synod or Palimpsest territory. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological time. Aging/de-aging, maturation acceleration, hibernation induction, metabolic rate manipulation. V-15 Titan = hyper-accelerated biological processes. |
| **Synod** | Entropy IS time's arrow. PRIMARY alongside Palimpsest. Preservation (halt decay), stasis fields, accelerated entropy as fast-forward. S-08 Censor = temporal redaction. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Mechanical time. Clockwork precision, synchronized systems, time-delayed activation. F-06 Clockwork = precise temporal manipulation. |
| **Palimpsest** | Probability-adjusted temporal flow. PRIMARY alongside Synod. P-01 Loop = time loops. P-10 Prophecy = future-reading through temporal probability. The Chronosynclastic Institute operates at the Palimpsest-Synod interface. |

---

*Document continues in Part 2: Information & Perception, Creation & Shaping (including expanded Summon), Restoration & Counter-Magic, Translocation, Absolute Effects, and Meta-Effects.*

---

---

# III. INFORMATION & PERCEPTION

Effects dealing with the acquisition, processing, weaponization, and manipulation of information. Information is the currency of power in Tessera — these effects determine who knows what and who controls what others perceive.

---

## Illusion (Base Cost 2 Single Sense / 3 Multi-Sensory)

**Palimpsest superposition collapse applied to perception.**

Pull potential states from the Palimpsest and partially collapse them into material reality. ESL determines collapse percentage. Absorbs Concealment — invisibility is an illusion replacing visual presence with empty space. Chiaroscuro Institute's signature effect.

### Scaling Table (Superposition Collapse)

| ESL | Collapse % | Quality |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | ~5% | Obviously false — shimmer, distortion, ghostly outline |
| 2 | ~10% | Convincing single-sense, holds until physically interacted with |
| 3 | ~20% | Convincing multi-sense OR perfect single-sense |
| 4 | ~30% | Rich multi-sensory, withstands casual physical scrutiny |
| 5 | ~50% | Quasi-real — deals partial damage, produces partial real effects |
| 6 | ~60% | Substantially real — significant physical presence |
| 7 | ~80% | Near-real — most observers cannot distinguish from genuine |
| 8 | ~90% | Near-indistinguishable from reality under any normal scrutiny |
| 9 | ~100% | Fully collapsed. IS real. No distinction from genuine expression. |
| 10 | >100% | Hyper-real. MORE metaphysically anchored than baseline reality. |

**>100% collapse:** Succeeding on the save to identify it as illusion causes METAPHYSICAL TRACK DAMAGE. The observer sees that reality has a fidelity setting that can be exceeded. Chiaroscuro masters who push past 100% are demonstrating that the Great Lie is a technique, not truth. The Maestro (Chiaroscuro Headmaster) IS a permanently >100% collapsed superposition state — a living meta-narrative about fictional characters existing through collective belief.

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Minimal. Physical elements are maximally real. Exceptions: B-04 Scorch = heat shimmer/mirage. B-15 Frost = frost shimmer distortion. Light and Shadow isotope crossovers. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological illusion. Camouflage, mimicry, bioluminescent deception, pheromone-based false signals. The Verdant Font invented deception before sapient beings existed — every mimic, every camouflaged predator, every lure is biological illusion. |
| **Synod** | Shadow/spectral illusion. Ghostly images, afterimages of the dead, phantom sounds from archived events. S-10 Shadow = darkness-based concealment. S-11 Echo = temporal afterimage displayed as present-tense. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Holographic illusion. Projected images, SimStim entertainment, technological deception. F-14 Lens = focused light projection. F-15 Data = digital illusion, virtual reality constructs. |
| **Palimpsest** | PRIMARY HOME. Illusion IS Palimpsest superposition collapse. P-05 Dream = dream-stuff made quasi-real. P-06 Gambit = bluff made real. P-07 Fey Logic = internally consistent illogic. P-16 Simulation = full-scale reality simulation. |

---

## Senses (Base Cost 2)

**Unified information management — acquisition, processing, and dissemination.**

Absorbs Communication, Comprehend, and Remote Sensing. The ESL determines the CATEGORY of information capability. The isotope determines the information channel. Construction Pool targeting determines input vs output vs bidirectional.

### Scaling Table (Information Management)

| ESL | Capability | Intelligence Discipline |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Sharpen existing sense — notice details others miss | Collection (enhanced) |
| 2 | Grant new mode — darkvision, detect magic, micro-expression reading | Collection (new channels) |
| 3 | Analytical overlay — assess threats, identify weaknesses, read emotions | Processing/Analysis |
| 4 | Remote collection — displace senses to distant point. Bidirectional information transmission. | Collection (remote) / Dissemination |
| 5 | Pattern recognition — Hypercognition, deduce from minimal data, scene reconstruction | Analysis (advanced) |
| 6 | Full remote sensory suite — comprehensive scrying | Collection (comprehensive) |
| 7 | Historical reconstruction — read past from location/object, Synod archive access | Collection (temporal, past) |
| 8 | True Seeing — penetrate all deception, all layers simultaneously | Analysis (penetrating) |
| 9 | Temporal perception — glimpse actual futures, true precognition | Prediction (temporal) |
| 10 | Metafaculty — near-universal awareness of a single target | Comprehensive intelligence |

**Chronosynclastic efficiency:** The Institute achieves ESL 5–7 results through mundane training (micro-expression analysis, game theory, statistical modeling, behavioral prediction) at 0 ÆP. Their students learn to read rooms the way Sherlock reads crime scenes — no magic, just trained observation and superhuman analytical processing. Magic is reserved for things math genuinely can't solve.

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical senses. Darkvision (Earth), echolocation (Air), enhanced hearing (Air), thermal vision (Fire), tremorsense (Earth). |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological senses. Danger sense (predator awareness), pheromone reading (Fauna/Myco), scent tracking (Fauna). |
| **Synod** | Historical/archival senses. Communing with archived souls (Archival/Spectral), perceiving temporal afterimages (Echo), psychometry — reading the Record (Archival). S-05 Record = direct Synod archive access. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological senses. Analytical instruments, data processing, diagnostic readouts, scanner arrays, ward-detection networks. F-15 Data = information processing through crystalline matrices. |
| **Palimpsest** | Probability senses. Divination, fate current reading, precognition, probability assessment. P-03 Nostalgia = perceiving echoes. P-09 Casino = reading the odds. P-10 Prophecy = temporal probability perception. Chronosynclastic Institute's PRIMARY domain. |

---

## Mind Link (Base Cost 3)

**Bidirectional deep mental access. Categorically separate from Senses.**

The invasion of another mind is fundamentally different from information gathering. Low rank: surface thoughts. Mid rank: implant suggestions, charm. High rank: dominate, memory rewrite. Unity Academy's signature effect.

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural. Physical elements don't interface with consciousness. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological telepathy. Hive mind connection (V-11 Hive), pheromone communication, swarm consciousness. |
| **Synod** | Communing with the dead. Accessing souls in transit. S-06 Whispers = archived-mind contact. S-12 Possession = spectral entity entering a living mind. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological mind interface. Cybernetic neural links (F-08 Cyberpunk), digital telepathy, encrypted mental channels, neural jacks. F-15 Data = crystal-matrix mind bridging. |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative mind connection. Deep empathic resonance through shared story. P-05 Dream = shared dreamspace. P-08 Horror = forced emotional sharing. Unity Academy's Thrallherd network operates at the Palimpsest boundary. |

---

# IV. CREATION & SHAPING

Effects that produce, move, alter, or condense things that didn't exist before or change things that already do. This category spans from practical utility (make a wall, move a boulder) to the most conceptually dense effect in the system (Summon as universal shaping verb).

---

## Create (Base Cost 2)

**Form solid non-living objects or extradimensional spaces from prima æther.**

Physical objects use material isotopes. Extradimensional spaces are Palimpsest folds — pinches in the fabric creating accessible pockets. ESL determines volume/complexity.

### Scaling Table (Extradimensional Spaces)

| ESL | Volume | Equivalent |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | ~1 cu ft | Familiar Pocket, hidden compartment |
| 2 | ~30 cu ft | Bag of Holding, haversack |
| 3 | ~250 cu ft | Portable Hole, large container |
| 4 | ~1,000 cu ft | Rope Trick, room for a party |
| 5 | ~5,000 cu ft | Suite of rooms |
| 6 | ~25,000 cu ft | Small building |
| 7 | ~100,000 cu ft | Large building, Mansion |
| 8 | ~500,000 cu ft | Lesser Demiplane |
| 9 | ~2,000,000 cu ft | Demiplane |
| 10 | ~10,000,000+ cu ft | Greater Demiplane / Genesis |

**Palimpsest fold architecture:** Extradimensional spaces have LOCATIONS accessible from the Palimpsest side. The Palimpsest's fabric is effectively infinite — mortal-scale folding is scooping a cup from the ocean. Nesting works. Ancient abandoned folds persist as explorable spaces. The Palimpsest IS the mega-dungeon.

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical object creation. PRIMARY for objects. B-05 Abyss = create water. B-09 Bedrock = create stone. B-15 Frost = create ice. Isotope determines material. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological creation. Grow plants, create organic structures, produce biological compounds. V-01 World Tree = wooden construction. V-04 Overgrowth = rapid vegetation. |
| **Synod** | Paradoxical creation — creating vessels for entropy. Undead constructs, necromantic structures, preserved spaces. S-04 Necropolis = structures of interred material. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological fabrication. Manufacture Kan-tech, build constructs, engineer solutions. Daedalon's domain. F-13 Spire = crystal-lattice construction. |
| **Palimpsest** | Spatial creation. PRIMARY for spaces. Every Bag of Holding, Rope Trick, and demiplane is a Palimpsest fold. Demiplanes = construction projects using multiple linked rotes over multiple sessions. |

---

## Move Object (Base Cost 2)

**Telekinesis — move, lift, manipulate objects at distance.**

### Scaling Table

| ESL | Weight |
|---|---|
| 1 | 5 lbs |
| 2 | 25 lbs |
| 3 | 100 lbs |
| 4 | 250 lbs |
| 5 | 500 lbs |
| 6 | 1,000 lbs |
| 7 | 2,500 lbs |
| 8 | 5,000 lbs |
| 9 | 10,000 lbs |
| 10 | 25,000+ lbs |

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical telekinesis. Air currents (B-13), gravitational manipulation (B-09), magnetic force (B-14 Current), steam pressure as motive force (B-02). |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological telekinesis. Prehensile appendage extension, symbiotic organism manipulation, vine grasping. |
| **Synod** | Spectral telekinesis. Poltergeist activity — objects moved by invisible spectral force. S-09 Wraith = ghostly manipulation. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Mechanical telekinesis. Force projectors, magnetic cranes, remote-operated manipulators, tractor beams. |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative telekinesis. Things move because the probability of their current position becomes zero. "The book was always going to fall." |

---

## Transform (Base Cost 1 Minor / 2 Significant)

**Alter fundamental properties of any target — object OR living tissue.**

The unified alteration effect. Minor (BER 1): cosmetic changes, elongation, extra appendages. Significant (BER 2): size change, full biological rewrite, phase state changes, hostile forced transformation, species alteration.

Absorbs: Elongation, Extra Limbs, Growth, Insubstantial, Morph, Shrinking.

Three-leg reversion for significant transformations: Believing (Integrity/self-concept) + Knowing (Metaphysics/theory) + Doing (Praxis/execution). Wildpath Wardens train all three. Forest "monsters" = failed students whose psyche collapsed.

**Phase state self-transformation:** Gaseous Form = Transform (flesh→gas). Ethereal Jaunt = Transform (material→Palimpsest-adjacent). Blink = Transform flickering between states. Ghosts are NOT using Transform — they're souls without bodies. A living creature "going incorporeal" is Transform; an actual ghost is a different phenomenon entirely.

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical transformation. Phase state changes — stone to mud, water to ice, metal to rust. Smelting, calcination. B-01 Inferno = fire as processing agent. B-09 Bedrock = reshape stone. B-11 Acid = dissolve and reform. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological transformation. PRIMARY for living targets. Wildpath Wardens' domain. Beast shape, chimeric hybridization, plant-body modification, polymorph. V-05 Hunt = predator form. V-08 Chimera = multi-species hybrid. V-16 Mutagen = controlled mutation. |
| **Synod** | Entropic transformation. Petrification as "ending mobility." Aging as accelerated temporal transformation. Disintegration. S-01 Grave = flesh to burial material. S-09 Wraith = phase shift toward spectral. S-13 Oblivion = transformation into nothing. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological transformation. Material processing, refining, alloy creation, Kan-tech modification. F-09 Vat = reaction-vessel processing. F-13 Spire = lattice optimization. |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative transformation. Become what the story needs. P-01 Loop = revert to previous form. P-05 Dream = fluid identity. P-13 Error = retroactive form change ("you were always this way"). |

---

## Summon (Base Cost 3)

**Condense prima æther into a shaped magical expression.**

The most conceptually expansive effect in the system. Summon is NOT limited to "call a creature." It is the verb for shaping ACTIVE magic into any persistent form. The expression dissolves when will or ÆP ceases — nothing was physically created (that's Create) or biologically altered (that's Transform). The magic IS the product.

### The Shaping Spectrum

| Application | What You're Shaping | Form | Legacy Reference |
|---|---|---|---|
| Creature Summon | Temporary entity from condensed æther | Independent being | Summon Monster, Astral Construct |
| Soul Weapon | Weapon-shaped magical expression | Hand-held equipment | Soul Knife, Spiritual Weapon, Flame Blade |
| Soul Armor | Protective equipment of shaped æther | Worn equipment | Incarnum soulmelds, Akashic veils |
| Totem Aspect | Biological augmentation worn as magical overlay | Body overlay | Totemist beast aspects, Akashic shaped veils |
| Ætheric Tool | Functional tool that exists only while maintained | Held/deployed object | Unseen Servant (shaped force), Mage Hand construct |
| Synthesist Fusion | Creature worn as an exosuit | Merged entity | PF1e Synthesist Summoner, Arcane Synthesis talent |
| Construct Shell | Autonomous construct body | Independent construct | Astral Construct (EPH), animated objects |

### The Critical Distinction: Summon vs Create vs Transform

| Verb | What Changes | Persistence | Reversion |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Create** | Physical matter is produced | Persists independently (Released = permanent object) | Object remains after magic ends unless duration expires |
| **Transform** | Existing matter/biology is altered | Target's actual properties change | Three-leg reversion required for significant changes |
| **Summon** | Active magic takes form | Dissolves without will/ÆP | No reversion needed — nothing was changed, the magic just stops |

**The Wildpath boundary:** A novice uses Summon to wear bear-aspect æther as a temporary overlay (safe, removable, no reversion needed). A master uses Transform to actually BECOME bear-aspected (dangerous, psyche collapse risk, three-leg reversion). Same visual result, completely different risk profile.

### The Build Menu

ESL caps the Creature Mastery Level (CML) of summoned entities. For non-creature shapes (weapons, armor, tools), ESL determines the power level of the expression. The summoned entity/object's capabilities ARE spell sentence expressions at miniature scale:

| Capability Category | Corresponding Effect |
|---|---|
| Natural attacks / weapon damage | Damage |
| Natural armor / shield value | Protection |
| Movement modes / speed | Movement |
| Energy resistance / immunity | Protection / Immunity |
| Special attacks (breath weapon, gaze) | Damage + Affliction (linked rotes within the construct) |
| Sensory modes (darkvision, scent) | Senses |
| Stealth / invisibility | Illusion |
| Size change | Transform |
| Attribute bonuses | Enhanced Trait |

The Feature Point budget (to be formally built) scales with ESL. Higher ESL = more points = more capable expression. The tiered menu system (Menu A/B/C/D from astral constructs) maps to the ESL tiers:

| Menu Tier | ESL Range | Capability Scale |
|---|---|---|
