| Menu A (basic) | 1–3 | Simple abilities: bonus damage, claws, dodge, basic movement |
| Menu B (intermediate) | 4–5 | Significant abilities: energy attacks, flight, DR, reach, fast healing |
| Menu C (advanced) | 6–7 | Powerful abilities: incorporeal form, power resistance, rend, spring attack |
| Menu D (expert) | 8–10 | Extraordinary abilities: massive DR, energy auras, amplified invisibility |

### Planar Expressions

The isotope determines WHAT condenses:

#### Borderlands — Elemental Constructs

Beings of pure physical element. The most common and mechanically straightforward summons.

- **Solid (Earth):** Stone constructs, earth elementals, crystal golems. Durable, slow, heavy hitters.
- **Liquid (Water):** Water elementals, ice constructs, pressure-based entities. Fluid, adaptive, engulfing.
- **Gas (Air):** Air elementals, wind constructs, lightning sprites. Fast, evasive, reach-focused.
- **Plasma (Fire):** Fire elementals, magma constructs, radiant beings. High damage, fragile, energy aura.

Soul weapons from Borderlands isotopes: flaming sword (Fire), ice blade (Water/Air), stone hammer (Earth), lightning javelin (Air/Fire).

#### Verdant Font — Biological Constructs

Magical beasts, nature spirits, animate plants, swarm entities. Feel more "alive" than Borderlands summons because the Verdant Font's nature is biological process.

- **Solid (Flora):** Animate plant guardians, treant-like sentinels, thorn constructs. V-01 World Tree = wooden guardian.
- **Liquid (Fauna):** Predatory beast summons, nature spirit companions, animal guardians. V-05 Hunt = predator summon.
- **Gas (Myco):** Swarm summons, spore clouds with agency, distributed fungal intelligence. V-11 Hive = swarm entity.
- **Plasma (Viral):** Chimeric horrors, hyper-mutated beasts, unstable biological constructs. Powerful but unpredictable.

Totem aspects from Verdant Font: bear claws (Fauna), bark skin (Flora), predator senses (Fauna), poison spines (Myco/Flora). The totemist wears Verdant Font æther shaped by biological templates.

#### Synod of Endings — Spectral/Undead Entities

The Synod doesn't "create" undead — it calls patterns from the archive and gives them temporary expression. The summoned entity is an archived pattern temporarily expressed in the material plane.

- **Solid (Osseous):** Skeletal constructs, bone golems, calcified servitors. S-01 Grave = animate skeletal remains.
- **Liquid (Archival):** Archived soul fragments given temporary voice. Not truly sentient — echoes of the Record performing their last function.
- **Gas (Spectral):** Spectral entities, wraith servitors, shadow constructs. S-09 Wraith = ghostly combatant.
- **Plasma (Void):** Void-constructs, entropy-aspected entities that destroy by proximity. S-13 Oblivion = negation entity.

Soul weapons from Synod: spectral blade (Spectral), bone weapon (Osseous), void-edge that damages by REMOVING rather than cutting (Void).

#### Hidden Forge — Kan-tech Constructs

Activated dormant constructs, assembled drones, fabricated temporary golems. The Sōmaturge's Forge Construct lives here.

- **Solid (Crystal):** Crystal constructs, gemstone guardians, lattice-based entities. F-13 Spire = crystalline sentinel.
- **Liquid (Chem):** Chemical constructs, alchemical golems, acid-based entities. F-09 Vat = chemical servitor.
- **Gas (Pneuma):** Mechanical drones, steam-powered servitors, pneumatic constructs. F-01 Engine = mechanical companion. F-02 Steampunk = steam-powered construct.
- **Plasma (Volt):** Electrical constructs, lightning-animated golems, magnetic entities. F-06 Clockwork = precision mechanical servant. F-08 Cyberpunk = advanced synthetic entity.

Soul weapons from Forge: force blade (Crystal), lightning whip (Volt), pneumatic hammer (Pneuma). Kan-tech weapon constructs shaped from Forge æther.

#### Palimpsest — Probability Entities

Beings that COULD exist, collapsed from superposition into temporary reality. The strangest summons — creatures from probability space with no natural history, no species, no ecology. They exist because the math says they should.

- **Solid (Echo):** Echoes of beings that existed in prior timeline configurations. Temporal afterimage given form.
- **Liquid (Veil):** Dream entities. Things from the Dream isotope given quasi-real form. P-05 Dream = nightmare made manifest.
- **Gas (Probability):** Pure probability constructs. Statistical aggregates given form. P-09 Casino = luck entity.
- **Plasma (Glitch):** Things that shouldn't exist but do because of reality malfunction. P-13 Error = error entity. P-07 Fey Logic = paradox creature.

Soul weapons from Palimpsest: probability blade (hits because it was statistically inevitable), echo weapon (deals damage from attacks that already happened), dream weapon (hurts because the target believes it should).

The Maestro was, in a sense, a permanent Palimpsest Summon — collective belief condensed into a self-sustaining entity that exceeded 100% collapse and became permanently real.

---

# V. RESTORATION & COUNTER-MAGIC

Situational but critical effects that reverse damage, undo conditions, and disrupt magical expressions.

---

## Nullify (Base Cost 3)

**Magical neutralization reaction.**

Introduce an incompatible magical expression to the target spell's concordance structure. The reaction follows acid-base chemistry: the two cancel, producing inert residue and released energy. Energy release scales with target's ESL.

### Scaling Table

| ESL | Capability | Energy Release |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Suppress cantrip/Feature-level effect | Negligible |
| 2 | Counter single low-ESL effect | Minor |
| 3 | Targeted dispel — strip one specific effect | Noticeable, manageable |
| 4 | Area dispel — suppress effects below your ESL | Significant area release |
| 5 | Counter higher-ESL with check | Dangerous if successful |
| 6 | Suppress isotope category in area | Major energy management |
| 7 | Suppress ALL magic in area (Antimagic Field) | Massive containment load |
| 8 | Permanently strip magical properties | Irreversible neutralization |
| 9 | Suppress planar traits | Reality may hiccup |
| 10 | Sever connection to Prima Æther | Paper logic only |

**Neutralization mechanics:** Against Structured Current (Architect) spells — attack concordance lattice, introduce turbulence. Against Primal Current (Improviser) spells — impose unwanted structure, crystallize until it shatters. Against natural phenomena — significantly harder, no caster maintaining it.

**Deepguard wards** are pressurized containers of concordance-structured magic. Breaking one releases stored energy as uncontrolled burst — the ward was HOLDING that energy stable. Warden Breaker talent exists because breaking static wards is fundamentally different from countering active spells.

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Counter with opposing elements. Fire nullifies water. Earth grounds electricity. Classical elemental opposition. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological counter-magic. Antivenom, antibiotic, immune response as Nullify mechanism. The body's own immune system IS Nullify. |
| **Synod** | Counter necromantic/entropic expressions. Turn Undead = Synod Nullify. S-08 Censor = archival redaction. Inherently good at stripping duration — entropy is the Synod's native tool. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Counter Kan-tech. Deepguard Academy's specialty. Disabling wards, disrupting engineered systems, EMP-equivalent effects. |
| **Palimpsest** | Counter illusions, probability manipulation, narrative effects. Restore baseline reality by collapsing artificial superposition states. P-13 Error = induce reality errors in target expression. P-14 Paradox = self-contradicting logic unraveling constructs. |

---

## Panacea (Base Cost 3)

**Condition reversal — the inverse of Affliction.**

Whatever Affliction can impose at a severity, Panacea can reverse. Disease cure. Poison neutralization. Limb regrowth (reversing Crippled). Resurrection = high-rank Panacea (reversing the death condition).

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Physical condition clearing. Unfreeze, extinguish, restore senses dulled by elemental exposure. |
| **Verdant Font** | PRIMARY HOME. Biological condition reversal. Cure disease, neutralize poison, purge parasites, restore function. The Verdant Font produces the problem AND the solution. |
| **Synod** | Reverse entropy-based conditions. Extremely difficult — fighting the plane's nature. The most profound Panacea expression: undoing what entropy says should be permanent. |
| **Hidden Forge** | System reset. Clear malfunction conditions, reboot crashed Kan-tech, debug corrupted programming. |
| **Palimpsest** | Retroactive condition reversal. "That condition never happened." Probability editing the event that caused it. |

---

# VI. TRANSLOCATION

Effects governing movement between locations, planes, and time periods. The Palimpsest is the primary medium for all translocation — these effects navigate its topology.

---

## Teleport (Base Cost 3)

**Same-plane translocation by skimming the Palimpsest surface.**

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural — physical elements don't fold space. |
| **Verdant Font** | Not natural. Mycelial network traversal is Movement through a biological medium, not teleportation. |
| **Synod** | Partial — spectral shortcuts through the Synod's geography. S-09 Wraith = phase-skip. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Partial — engineered teleportation grids. F-05 Grid = network-routed instant transit. |
| **Palimpsest** | PRIMARY. Teleportation IS Palimpsest surface navigation. Translocation Geometry. Every teleport skims the Palimpsest briefly. |

---

## Dimensional Travel (Base Cost 4)

**Interplanar transit through deep Palimpsest.**

Dot 1: controlled aperture, Material ↔ Palimpsest + Translocation Geometry shortcuts. Dot 2: full interplanar transit. Planar incursions = Palimpsest stretching thin (inverse of Create's pinching).

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural. |
| **Verdant Font** | Not natural. |
| **Synod** | Partial — accessing the Synod directly as destination. S-12 Possession = crossing spectral boundary. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Partial — Forge-built portal infrastructure, dimensional engineering. |
| **Palimpsest** | PRIMARY. All interplanar transit navigates through the Palimpsest. The Palimpsest IS the medium between planes. |

---

## Time Travel (Base Cost 5)

**Temporal displacement through deep Palimpsest via Synod navigation.**

Three modes: Loop (round trip, fixed endpoints), Rethread (one-way, adding to tapestry), Loose Thread (serial travel, origin must be anchored). Temporal wall at the Calamity — cannot navigate pre-Calamity. Settings A and B were raw thread stock woven INTO Setting C (never existed as separate fabrics).

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural. |
| **Verdant Font** | Not natural. |
| **Synod** | PARTIAL. The Synod IS the museum of Setting C history. Read-only temporal observation — you can observe but the Synod preserves, it doesn't change. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Not natural. |
| **Palimpsest** | PRIMARY and ONLY home for actual temporal displacement. All Time Travel navigates through the Palimpsest using Synod archives as reference points. |

---

# VII. ABSOLUTE EFFECTS

The rarest and most powerful effects. They don't scale incrementally — they represent categorical changes in a target's relationship with reality.

---

## Immunity (Base Cost 4)

**Absolute negation of a specific expression. Binary — immune or not.**

ESL determines breadth, not intensity. Narrow by design. The Wildpath Wardens teach that Transform is usually a better answer than stacking Immunities: "Why resist lava when you can become something that lives in lava?"

Natural immunity through biological adaptation (lava fleas, phase hounds) = same mechanism expressed through evolution rather than spellcraft.

### Scaling Table (Breadth)

| ESL | Scope |
|---|---|
| 4 | Single specific isotope expression (B-01 Inferno specifically) |
| 6 | Isotope category (all Borderlands fire expressions) |
| 8 | Planar family (all Borderlands expressions) |
| 10 | Conceptual immunity (immune to the CONCEPT, not just the damage type) |

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Elemental immunity. Fire, cold, acid, electricity, or specific physical isotope. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological immunity. Disease, poison, specific creature types or biological vectors. |
| **Synod** | Entropic immunity. Aging, soul-targeting effects, specific undead abilities. Most difficult to achieve — becoming immune to entropy itself. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological immunity. Specific energy types, system disruption, EMP. |
| **Palimpsest** | Conceptual immunity. Probability manipulation, mind effects, illusion. Rarest and most expensive — becoming immune to narrative itself. |

---

## Immortality (Base Cost 5)

**Permanent soul-body binding preventing death from completing.**

Not resurrection (that's high-rank Panacea). Dot 1 = single vessel phylactery. Dot 2 = distributed binding (Warden state).

### Planar Expressions

| Plane | Expression |
|---|---|
| **Borderlands** | Not natural. Physical elements don't address soul mechanics. |
| **Verdant Font** | Biological immortality. Cellular renewal loops, telomere restoration, fountain of youth. V-15 Titan = hyper-regenerative biological immortality. |
| **Synod** | The paradoxical expression — the bureaucracy of entropy granting an exemption. Lichdom, undeath, soul binding to ossuary vessels. S-04 Necropolis = institutional undeath. |
| **Hidden Forge** | Technological immortality. Brain-in-jar, consciousness upload, mechanical body replacement. F-08 Cyberpunk = full-body replacement. F-15 Data = digital consciousness preservation. |
| **Palimpsest** | Narrative immortality. "The story needs you." The Maestro IS this. P-07 Fey Logic = existing because the logic of the narrative demands it. |

---

# VIII. META-EFFECTS

Effects that operate outside or above the normal effect framework.

---

## Feature (Base Cost 0)

**The universal magical tell. Cosmetic only.**

Everyone in Tessera has a Feature. The prima æther acknowledging you exist and you acknowledging it back. The candle a fire-aspected child can't stop flickering. The static charge trailing an electricity-aspected person. Trained observers read Features diagnostically to map magical aptitude — the easiest way to determine affinity.

Not learned. Expressed. A character's Feature reflects their deepest isotope affinity across any of the five planes.

---

## Variable (Rank × 2)

**Bounded replicator — the ultimate flexibility.**

Produce any known effect on demand. BER = Rank × 2 (the only effect where BER scales). Output identical to the replicated effect at that ESL. Constraint: can only replicate effects the caster has ≥1 dot in. Bypasses isotope requirement.

Praxis Collegium's signature. Overt (Scholar's Gambit) = flexibility as philosophy. Covert (Fulcrum) = learns effects specifically to deconstruct them.

Variable has no isotope home. A Variable user channels whatever isotope the replicated effect requires, drawn from their knowledge base. This is why the Praxis Collegium is the spy agency with the gadget bag — any tool for any situation.

---

# IX. COLLEGE SIGNATURE EFFECTS

| College | Signature Effect | Planar Home |
|---|---|---|
| Chiaroscuro Institute | Illusion (superposition collapse) | Palimpsest |
| Chronosynclastic Institute | Chronos + Senses (temporal flow + information management) | Palimpsest / Synod interface |
| Deepguard Academy | Protection + Nullify (wards + counter-magic) | Hidden Forge |
| Mortis Ævum Collegium | Affliction + Weaken + Healing (entropic manipulation) | Synod |
| Praxis Collegium | Variable (bounded replicator) | None (isotope-independent) |
| Sovereign Exchange | Create + Enhanced Trait (commerce + enhancement) | Borderlands / Hidden Forge |
| Stormpeak Academy | Damage (direct offensive magic) | Borderlands |
| Unity Academy | Mind Link (bidirectional mental access) | Palimpsest boundary |
| Wildpath Wardens | Transform (adaptive form change) | Verdant Font |

---

# X. CROSS-REFERENCE NOTES

## Effects Natural to ALL Five Planes
- Affliction, Damage, Movement, Protection, Senses, Transform

## Effects With a Single Primary Home
- Dimensional Travel → Palimpsest
- Illusion → Palimpsest
- Teleport → Palimpsest
- Time Travel → Palimpsest

## Effects With Dual Primary Homes
- Chronos → Palimpsest + Synod
- Healing → Verdant Font (biological) + Hidden Forge (mechanical repair)
- Immortality → Synod (undeath) + Verdant Font (biological)

## Effects Not Tied to Any Plane
- Feature (universal expression)
- Variable (bypasses isotope requirements)

## The Smoothing Effect Constraint
All magic operates within the prima æther — the Calamity's ongoing neutralization reaction between the Primal Current and Structured Current. Effects that could expose the seams (high-ESL Nullify suppressing the Smoothing Effect locally, >100% Illusion exceeding baseline reality's fidelity) are monitored by the Generalist College and by Aíon directly.

# ÆTHERIUM CODEX — Isotope Effect Reference
## The Borderlands: The Physical Register

*Profile derivation: Solid=Static, Liquid=Cycling, Gas=Projecting, Plasma=Absorbing*
*Compound isotopes: dominant axis sets primary profile, recessive adds behavioral character*
*Formation Stage Bands: 1-4 (Minor / NFPA 0-1), 5-7 (Moderate / NFPA 2-3), 8-10 (Intense / NFPA 3-4)*

---

### B-01 — Inferno (Plasma Pure / Fire)

**Phase:** Plasma | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Absorbing
*Consuming fire. Draws fuel inward and converts it to heat. Self-sustaining as long as fuel exists.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Damage** — Direct thermal destruction. The most immediate expression. Combustion on contact.
- **Environment** — Area denial through sustained heat. Temperature alteration. Atmospheric change.
- **Affliction** — Burning condition (ongoing). Ignition of flammable materials. Thermal shock.
- **Weaken** — Prolonged exposure degrades material integrity. Melting, warping, structural softening.
- **Transform** — Fire as processing agent at controlled application. Smelting, calcination, charcoal production. The basis of metallurgical processing.
- **Senses** — Light production. Fire illuminates. At low Stage this is the most common practical application — a torch.
- **Feature** — Persistent warmth. Heat source. Comfort in cold. Cooking.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing, Protection (physical), Concealment (fire is maximally visible), Luck Control, Flight, Teleport, Communication, Mind Reading.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Warmth. Ignites tinder. Light source. Comfort heat. The campfire.
- Stage 5–7: Active combustion. Sustained burning. Area fire damage. Structural threat.
- Stage 8–10: Self-sustaining consumption field. Consumes all available fuel without cessation. Melts stone. Plasma threshold.

**Median Expression:** Damage (fire). A B-01 compound installed without further specification produces fire damage on contact or activation.

---

### B-02 — Steam (Plasma + Liquid / Fire + Water)

**Phase:** Plasma dominant, Liquid recessive | **Type:** ⚙ Catalyzed Diagonal | **Profile:** Absorbing primary, Cycling character
*Scalding high-pressure vapor. Expansion under containment. The diagonal means this state requires Forge mediation to hold — fire and water naturally annihilate toward one or the other.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Damage** — Scalding thermal damage. Pressure damage. Burns that penetrate sealed protection because vapor permeates.
- **Environment** — Fog/vapor creation. Pressure alteration. Humidity. Atmospheric saturation.
- **Affliction** — Scalding burns (ongoing, penetrating). Disorientation from steam blindness. Respiratory distress from superheated air.
- **Concealment** — Steam as visual obscurant. Reduces both physical and magical line of sight.
- **Move Object** — Pressure as motive force. The steam engine principle — contained pressure pushing mass. The historical basis of Setting B industrial power.
- **Weaken** — Pressure degradation of sealed containers, joints, and armor. Steam finds and exploits every gap.
- **Feature** — Persistent heat and moisture. Anti-freeze. Humidity maintenance.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing, Luck Control, Illusion, Teleport, Mind Reading, Growth, Create (physical — steam disperses rather than builds).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Warmth and dampness. Prevents freezing. Mild obscurement. Comfortable humidity.
- Stage 5–7: High-pressure emission. Scalding damage on contact. Active obscurement. Armor joint degradation begins.
- Stage 8–10: Violent decompression. Explosive pressure release. Structural destruction of containment. Boiler-burst equivalent.

**Median Expression:** Damage (scalding) + Environment (obscurement). Steam naturally does both simultaneously — it hurts and it hides.

---

### B-03 — Slag (Plasma + Solid / Fire + Earth)

**Phase:** Plasma dominant, Solid recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Absorbing primary, Static character
*Superheated semi-liquid heavy matter. Adhesive, slow-moving, retains heat long after source withdraws. The Solid recessive gives the fire mass and persistence — it doesn't flash and fade, it sticks and burns.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Damage** — Fire damage with mass behind it. Persistent adhesive burn. Both thermal and bludgeoning simultaneously.
- **Affliction** — Adhesion condition. Molten material sticks to target, ongoing burn that can't be brushed off. Requires active removal.
- **Environment** — Terrain hazard creation. Molten pools as difficult terrain with ongoing fire damage. Persistent zone control.
- **Protection** — Self-sealing property. Fills its own fractures with molten material. Resists condition track degradation downward. Slag heals itself.
- **Create** — Crude structural material from cooled flow. Volcanic stone, basalt. Rough but effective.
- **Weaken** — Thermal degradation through sustained contact. Melts through lower-tier materials over time.
- **Feature** — Persistent heat long after removal from source. Thermal mass. Cold resistance.

**Hard Exclusions:** Concealment (magma glows), Healing, Flight, Speed, Luck Control, Teleport, Communication.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Retains heat for hours. Minor cold resistance. Warm to touch.
- Stage 5–7: Active adhesive burn on contact. Ongoing damage. Self-sealing begins.
- Stage 8–10: Flowing destruction. Self-reshaping under its own heat. Area terrain creation on knockback. Cannot be destroyed by fire or cold extremes.

**Median Expression:** Damage (adhesive fire). Slag hits, sticks, and keeps burning with weight behind it.

---

### B-04 — Scorch (Plasma + Gas / Fire + Air)

**Phase:** Plasma dominant, Gas recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Absorbing primary, Projecting character
*Solar wind. Radiant heat delivered at distance without combustion. No fuel consumed, no smoke, no residue. The Gas recessive gives the fire range and penetration — it reaches across distance rather than burning locally.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Damage** — Ranged fire damage without combustion. Flash-burn at distance. Radiant energy. Bypasses cover that would stop flame because it doesn't need a fuel path.
- **Senses** — Light production at range. Radiance. Illumination as a ranged field rather than a point source. The basis of all "light" spells that aren't biological.
- **Affliction** — Charring without ignition. Desiccation. Sunburn at intensity. Dehydration through radiant exposure.
- **Environment** — Radiant heat field. Temperature increase in an area without physical fire. Heat shimmer affecting visibility.
- **Weaken** — Material degradation through sustained radiant exposure. UV-equivalent damage. Bleaching. Brittling of organic materials.
- **Feature** — Persistent radiance. Warmth at distance. The "ever-warm" property at low Stage.

**Hard Exclusions:** Concealment (Scorch is the opposite — maximum illumination and visibility), Protection (physical), Healing, Communication, Teleport, Luck Control.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Radiant warmth from a distance. Melts soft materials on extended contact. Light source.
- Stage 5–7: Flash-burn delivery. Ranged fire damage without combustion. Chars rather than ignites.
- Stage 8–10: Solar intensity field. Area radiant aura dealing ongoing fire damage. Melts stone. Sustained exposure destroys organic material.

**Median Expression:** Damage (radiant fire at range). Scorch is the sniper rifle of fire — it reaches out and burns without needing to be there.

---

### B-05 — The Abyss (Liquid Pure / Water)

**Phase:** Liquid | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Cycling
*Endless crushing ocean. Pressure without limit. Sensory deprivation. Weight of infinite depth made tangible. Cycling because water transforms what passes through it — dissolves, carries, redistributes, converts.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Protection** — Hydrostatic pressure as defense. Compression resistance. The deeper the Abyss expression, the harder it is to penetrate.
- **Environment** — Deep water conditions. Pressure increase. Temperature drop. Darkness. Atmospheric replacement with liquid medium.
- **Affliction** — Crushing pressure. Suffocation. Disorientation. Sensory deprivation. The Abyss imposes isolation.
- **Move Object** — Hydraulic force. Current and flow. Water as motive medium carrying or dragging mass.
- **Concealment** — Depth obscurement. Sound dampening. Light absorption. The deep ocean hides everything.
- **Speed (Swimming)** — Enhanced movement through liquid medium specifically. The Abyss facilitates travel through water.
- **Weaken** — Pressure fatigue. Sustained compression weakening structural integrity. Water seepage undermining foundations.
- **Feature** — Weight increase. Density enhancement. Pressure resistance for the wielder.
- **Damage** — At high Stage, crushing/bludgeoning through overwhelming hydraulic pressure. The ocean floor expression.

**Hard Exclusions:** Flight (the Abyss pulls down, not up), Fire damage (water extinguishes), Illusion, Luck Control, Growth.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Heavy. Item feels denser than expected. Minor resistance to displacement. Pressure comfort.
- Stage 5–7: Pressure field. Area movement impeded. Sensory suppression begins. Sound and light dampened.
- Stage 8–10: Abyssal depth. Total sensory deprivation zone. Crushing force. Area control through overwhelming pressure.

**Median Expression:** Protection (pressure). The Abyss makes things harder to reach, harder to move, harder to affect.

---

### B-06 — Vapor (Liquid + Plasma / Water + Fire)

**Phase:** Liquid dominant, Plasma recessive | **Type:** ⚙ Catalyzed Diagonal | **Profile:** Cycling primary, Absorbing character
*Choking white fog. Humidity that drowns on land. Distinct from Steam — Vapor is the fog state, not the pressure state. Requires Forge mediation to hold the intermediate state between boiling off and condensing.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Concealment** — The primary expression. Obscurement of both physical sight and magical detection simultaneously. The fog jams scrying as effectively as it blinds eyes.
- **Environment** — Fog creation. Humidity saturation. Atmospheric alteration. Visibility reduction.
- **Affliction** — Choking. Respiratory distress. Disorientation in dense fog. Spatial confusion.
- **Nullify** — Magical detection interference. Scrambles targeting systems and divination. The "jamming" expression — Vapor doesn't hide the target, it corrupts the detection method.
- **Senses (negative)** — Suppresses existing senses and magical detection. Anti-perception field.
- **Feature** — Persistent dampness. Fire resistance through moisture saturation. Humidity maintenance.

**Hard Exclusions:** Damage (direct — Vapor diffuses energy rather than concentrating it), Healing, Luck Control, Teleport, Growth, Create, Protection (physical — Vapor has no structural integrity).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Persistent dampness. Fire resistance. Mild obscurement in enclosed spaces.
- Stage 5–7: Dense fog field. Significant visual and magical obscurement. Ranged attacks penalized.
- Stage 8–10: Blinding fog. Total obscurement in radius. Ranged attacks cannot acquire targets. Magical detection severely penalized. Items within become difficult to locate magically.

**Median Expression:** Concealment. Vapor hides things from both eyes and magic.

---

### B-07 — Mire (Liquid + Solid / Water + Earth)

**Phase:** Liquid dominant, Solid recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Cycling primary, Static character
*The bog. Mud, quicksand, sucking earth. Neither fully liquid nor solid. The Solid recessive gives the water drag, mass, and cohesion — it doesn't flow freely, it pulls.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Affliction** — Restrain, entangle, slow. The cohesive pull that holds targets. The primary combat expression — Mire grabs and doesn't let go.
- **Protection** — Cohesive resistance. Surfaces coated in Mire resist penetration. The mud absorbs impact by deforming around it.
- **Environment** — Terrain alteration. Difficult terrain creation. Movement impediment over area.
- **Feature** — Grip. Non-slip surfaces. Enhanced grapple/hold maintenance. The craftsman's use.
- **Create** — Mud and clay constructs. Physical objects formed from the cohesive medium. Adobe, brick, ceramic when dried/fired.
- **Concealment** — Submersion. Targets pulled beneath Mire surface are hidden from sight and most detection.
- **Move Object** — Slow inexorable pull. Drag. Patient cohesive force. Mire doesn't throw — it sinks.
- **Weaken** — Structural undermining. Foundation loosening. Material saturation weakening structural integrity over time.
- **Enhanced Trait** — Grapple/grip enhancement at specialized application. The wrestler's mud.

**Hard Exclusions:** Flight, Teleport, Illusion, Speed (Mire is definitionally anti-speed), Communication, Luck Control, Damage (direct — Mire doesn't strike, it holds).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Tacky surface. Enhanced grip. Non-slip. Minor adhesion.
- Stage 5–7: Active cohesive pull. Movement halved for opponents. Restrained on failed Physical defense check.
- Stage 8–10: Immobilizing field. Automatic difficult terrain in radius. Foundations undermined. Structures sink.

**Median Expression:** Affliction (restrain/slow). Mire grabs things and holds them.

---

### B-08 — Hail (Liquid + Gas / Water + Air)

**Phase:** Liquid dominant, Gas recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Cycling primary, Projecting character
*Hardened water. Glaciers. Persistent cold that endures. The Gas recessive gives the cold penetration and reach — it spreads through the air rather than requiring contact.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Damage** — Cold damage. Thermal drain on contact. Flash-freeze. Ice impact.
- **Affliction** — Slowed (joint crystallization). Restrained (full freeze, especially on wet/water-adjacent targets). Numbness. Frostbite.
- **Environment** — Temperature reduction in area. Ice formation on surfaces. Slippery terrain. Frost conditions.
- **Protection** — Ice as ablative barrier. Shield that shatters on impact but absorbs the hit. Temporary structural defense.
- **Create** — Ice constructs. Walls, bridges, barriers, weapons from crystallized moisture. Temporary but immediately available.
- **Weaken** — Material embrittlement through thermal stress. Cold makes ductile materials brittle (direct Brittle Flag interaction — cold can temporarily IMPOSE the Brittle condition on materials that don't normally carry it).
- **Feature** — Preservation through cold. Organic material doesn't decay at low temperature. The earliest food preservation technology.
- **Concealment** — Ice refraction/reflection creating visual distortion at specialized application. Glare.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing (cold arrests biological processes rather than accelerating them — that's Synod territory), Fire damage, Growth, Luck Control, Teleport.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Persistent chill. Cold immunity for wielder. Liquids freeze on contact. Organic preservation.
- Stage 5–7: Flash-freeze field. Slowed condition on hit. Wet targets Restrained. Area temperature drop.
- Stage 8–10: Cryogenic. Shatters most metals through thermal shock. Area ice field. Sustained exposure Restrains automatically — the cold overwhelms Physical defense. Most metals become temporarily Brittle.

**Median Expression:** Damage (cold) + Affliction (Slowed). Hail freezes what it hits and impedes what it doesn't fully freeze.

---

### B-09 — The Bedrock (Solid Pure / Earth)

**Phase:** Solid | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Static
*Infinite stone. Geology without time limit. Maximum structural density. THE baseline structural expression of the Borderlands — everything else is modifications of this foundation.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Protection** — Physical defense. Structural reinforcement. Soak increase. The single most direct physical defense expression in the entire system.
- **Create** — Physical objects. Stone, earth, structural elements. Walls, barriers, foundations, buildings. The basis of all construction.
- **Environment** — Terrain creation and alteration. Stable ground. Structural modification of space. Earthworks.
- **Feature** — Weight increase. Density. Knockback resistance. Structural permanence. The "immovable" property.
- **Enhanced Trait (Might)** — Density enhancing physical force at specialized application. Stone-fist. Earth's weight behind a blow.
- **Affliction** — Entombment at high Stage. Burial. Encasement in stone. The earth swallowing targets.
- **Move Object** — Earthquake. Ground displacement. Shifting earth masses. Landslide. Not telekinesis — the earth itself moving.

**Hard Exclusions:** Flight (Bedrock is weight — the opposite of lift), Speed (mass resists acceleration), Concealment (stone is maximally present and detectable), Healing, Illusion, Luck Control, Teleport, Communication.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Dense material. +1 Soak contribution. Minor knockback resistance. Heavy.
- Stage 5–7: Structural permanence. Condition track resists downward movement. Full knockback immunity for wielder.
- Stage 8–10: Immovable object. Resists all displacement, magical and physical. Only Master-tier Sunder with focused effort can threaten condition track.

**Median Expression:** Protection. Bedrock makes things harder to break and harder to move.

---

### B-10 — Magma (Solid + Plasma / Earth + Fire)

**Phase:** Solid dominant, Plasma recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Static primary, Absorbing character
*The volcano. Flowing destructive rock. Both the mass of stone and the consuming nature of fire. The Static primary means it persists — the Absorbing character means it consumes what it persists against. Slow but nothing stops it.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Damage** — Fire damage with mass. Both thermal and bludgeoning simultaneously. Slow but devastating.
- **Environment** — Terrain hazard creation. Molten pools. Persistent fire zones as difficult terrain with ongoing damage. Area denial that lasts.
- **Affliction** — Ongoing burn with mass. Can't be brushed off easily. Adhesive + thermal condition simultaneously. Armor becomes a heat trap.
- **Protection** — Self-sealing property. Fills fractures with molten material. Resists condition track degradation. Magma items repair their own structural damage.
- **Create** — Volcanic stone from cooled flow. Basalt, pumice, obsidian (rapid cooling). Raw building material.
- **Weaken** — Thermal degradation through sustained contact. Melts through lower-tier structural materials over extended exposure.
- **Feature** — Persistent heat. Cold immunity. Thermal mass that sustains temperature for days.

**Hard Exclusions:** Concealment (magma glows — maximum visibility and thermal signature), Healing, Flight, Speed, Luck Control, Teleport, Communication.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Hot and heavy. Supplemental fire damage on contact. Cold suppression.
- Stage 5–7: Flowing destruction. Self-sealing activated. Area fire damage on successful hits.
- Stage 8–10: Terrain-creating devastation. Cannot be destroyed by fire or cold extremes (they neutralize). Knockback creates hazard zones behind displaced targets.

**Median Expression:** Damage (fire with mass). Magma is slow, heavy, devastating fire that persists and creates lasting terrain hazards.

---

### B-11 — Acid (Solid + Liquid / Earth + Water)

**Phase:** Solid dominant, Liquid recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Static primary, Cycling character
*Solvents and sludge. Rock dissolved into corrosive fluid. The Static primary means the dissolution is persistent and structural — it doesn't splash and fade, it eats through. The Cycling character means it converts what it contacts into dissolved product.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Damage** — Corrosive damage. Dissolution on contact. Penetrates by eating through barriers rather than overpowering them.
- **Weaken** — Structural degradation. Item condition track damage on struck items. Soak reduction through material dissolution. THE anti-equipment expression.
- **Affliction** — Ongoing corrosion condition. Continues dissolving after initial contact until neutralized or expended. Tissue damage that worsens over rounds.
- **Environment** — Corrosive area. Pools of dissolving material. Hazardous terrain that damages anything resting in it.
- **Transform** — Dissolution as processing step. Acid as tool for breaking compounds into constituent parts. The basis of refining and separation chemistry.
- **Feature** — Enhanced Sunder effectiveness against organic and low-tier materials. Corrosive surface treatment.
- **Nullify** — At specialized application: dissolving the physical substrate of magical constructs or enchantments. If the item carrying the enchantment dissolves, the enchantment has nothing to inhabit.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing (acid destroys tissue, doesn't repair it), Protection (acid weakens structural integrity), Concealment, Luck Control, Flight, Teleport, Communication.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Mildly corrosive surface. Slow degradation on prolonged contact. Enhanced Sunder effectiveness vs organic materials.
- Stage 5–7: Active dissolution. Struck items move down condition track. Armor degradation over sustained combat. Ongoing damage condition.
- Stage 8–10: Deep corrosion. Bypasses Soak. Tissue degradation on critical hits. Structural failure of contacted materials.

**Median Expression:** Damage (corrosive) + Weaken (structural). Acid damages the target AND makes the target more vulnerable to subsequent damage.

---

### B-12 — Dust (Solid + Gas / Earth + Air)

**Phase:** Solid dominant, Gas recessive | **Type:** ⚙ Catalyzed Diagonal | **Profile:** Static primary, Projecting character
*Erosion made material. Sandstorms, floating islands, dunes. The Solid being dispersed by the Gas — Earth in the process of being scattered into Air. Requires Forge mediation to hold this tension — without it, the material either settles as inert solid or disperses entirely as gas.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Weaken** — Erosion. Ablative stripping. Gradual structural degradation over sustained exposure. The primary expression — Dust wears things down.
- **Damage** — Abrasive damage. Particulate impact at velocity. Sandblasting. Grinding.
- **Affliction** — Blindness through abrasive particulate. Respiratory distress. Desiccation. Skin abrasion. Eye damage.
- **Concealment** — Sandstorm obscurement. Particulate cloud reducing visibility. Dust as visual screen.
- **Environment** — Dust storm conditions. Reduced visibility. Abrasive atmosphere. Terrain alteration through sand/particulate deposition.
- **Senses (negative)** — Suppresses visual and auditory perception through particulate density. Interferes with tracking and observation.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing, Protection (Dust erodes rather than protects), Create (Dust disperses structure rather than building it), Luck Control, Flight, Teleport, Growth.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Abrasive coating. Eye and skin protection required. Provides camouflage in arid environments. Minor Sunder enhancement on unprotected targets.
- Stage 5–7: Erosion field. Objects near the source lose effective Soak over time. Armor degrades during extended encounters.
- Stage 8–10: Sandstorm aura. Blinding abrasion in radius. Item condition tracks of opponents degrade per round of exposure.

**Median Expression:** Weaken (erosion). Dust wears things down gradually and reliably.

---

### B-13 — The Void Sky (Gas Pure / Air)

**Phase:** Gas | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Projecting
*Endless sky. No gravity, no ground. Movement in every direction simultaneously possible. The Projecting profile means the Void Sky pushes outward — it disperses, spreads, expands, broadcasts.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Speed** — Enhanced movement through reduced effective weight. Faster ground travel. The simplest expression.
- **Move Object** — Wind force. Air pressure as motive power. Pushing, dragging, lifting through atmospheric manipulation.
- **Environment** — Atmospheric alteration. Wind creation. Pressure change. Weather manipulation. The sky as controllable medium.
- **Feature** — Weight reduction. Lightness. Encumbrance reduction. The "featherweight" property.
- **Leaping** — Enhanced jump distance through reduced effective gravity. Assisted by upward air current.
- **Flight** — At Stage 5+, the Gas expression fully overcomes gravity. The natural progression of the Void Sky's upward push.
- **Affliction** — Suffocation (air removed or replaced with unbreathable composition). Deafening (wind noise overwhelming). Disorientation (weightlessness, vertigo).
- **Concealment** — Sound masking through wind. At high Stage, light dispersal through atmospheric distortion.
- **Damage** — At high Stage: vacuum damage, pressure differential damage, or high-velocity wind impact. The hurricane.

**Hard Exclusions:** Protection (physical — Gas disperses rather than resists), Create (physical solids — Gas cannot build solid structures), Healing, Luck Control, Illusion.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Lighter. Encumbrance reduced. Minor speed increase. Wind whisper.
- Stage 5–7: Gravitational independence. Hover capability. Enhanced prone resistance. Jump distance multiplied.
- Stage 8–10: Weightless field in radius. Full flight. Opponents' footing unreliable — forced movement costs doubled for those in the field.

**Median Expression:** Speed + Feature (lightness). The Void Sky makes things lighter and faster.

---

### B-14 — Current (Gas + Plasma / Air + Fire)

**Phase:** Gas dominant, Plasma recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Projecting primary, Absorbing character
*Lightning and plasma arcing through atmosphere. Storm without rain. Pure electrical-kinetic force. The Projecting primary means it reaches across distance — the Absorbing character means it consumes conductors it reaches.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Damage** — Electrical damage. Arc discharge. The most mobile destructive force in the Borderlands — reaches everywhere the atmosphere reaches.
- **Feature** — Chain effect. Electrical arc jumps to secondary targets through conductive proximity. Metal armor becomes a liability near Current expression.
- **Affliction** — Electrical stun. Muscle seizure. Nervous system disruption. At high Stage: paralysis, cardiac disruption. At specialized application against constructs: system overload.
- **Senses** — Electrical detection. Sense metal and conductors in range. Lightning-flash illumination. At specialized application: electromagnetic field reading.
- **Environment** — Storm conditions. Electrified atmosphere. All conductors in area become secondary arc targets. Chain hazard zone.
- **Weaken** — Electrical overload disrupting mechanical and magical systems. Circuit burning. Fuse-blowing. Kan-tech disruption.
- **Move Object** — Electromagnetic force at specialized application. Magnetism as electrical phenomenon — attracting or repelling metallic objects.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing, Concealment (lightning is maximally visible — flash, thunder, ozone), Protection (physical), Luck Control, Teleport.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Static charge. Mild supplemental electrical damage. Metal-wearing targets take minor shock on contact.
- Stage 5–7: Arc discharge. Chain to nearest conductor within range on critical hit. Electrical knockback immunity for wielder. System disruption begins.
- Stage 8–10: Storm channel. Absorbs and redirects incoming electrical damage. Full chain-lightning discharge. All electrical effects in radius drawn through the item.

**Median Expression:** Damage (electrical) + Feature (chain). Current hits the primary target and arcs to the nearest conductor.

---

### B-15 — Frost (Gas + Liquid / Air + Water)

**Phase:** Gas dominant, Liquid recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Projecting primary, Cycling character
*Flash-freezing wind. Cold carried at range. Rapid crystallization on contact with moisture. The Projecting primary gives it range — the Cycling character means it transforms what it reaches, converting liquid water to solid ice on contact.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Damage** — Cold damage delivered at range. Flash-freeze. Wind-carried thermal drain. Doesn't require contact — the cold arrives on the wind.
- **Affliction** — Slowed (joint crystallization from moisture in tissue freezing). Restrained (full freeze on wet or water-adjacent targets). Numbness reducing manual dexterity. Frostbite.
- **Environment** — Temperature drop across area. Ice formation on surfaces creating slippery terrain. Frost conditions. Moisture crystallization.
- **Weaken** — Material embrittlement through rapid thermal contraction. Cold makes ductile materials temporarily Brittle. Direct Brittle Flag interaction — Frost can IMPOSE the Brittle condition on materials that normally don't carry it.
- **Feature** — Cold immunity for wielder. Preservation through cold (organic material doesn't decay). Liquid freeze on contact.
- **Create** — Ice formation at high Stage. Frost can build ice structures from ambient moisture — walls, bridges, terrain features. Temporary but immediate.
- **Concealment** — Ice refraction creating visual distortion. Glare. Frost-covered surfaces reflecting and scattering light unpredictably.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing, Fire damage (diametrically opposed), Growth (cold arrests biological acceleration), Luck Control, Teleport, Protection (Frost makes things brittle, not durable — it weakens rather than strengthens).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Persistent chill radiating from item. Liquids freeze on contact. Cold immunity for wielder. Organic preservation.
- Stage 5–7: Flash-freeze on hit. Slowed condition. Wet/water-adjacent targets Restrained. Area temperature drop begins.
- Stage 8–10: Ice field aura. Continuous difficult terrain in radius. Sustained exposure Restrains automatically — the cold overwhelms Physical defense. Most metals become temporarily Brittle.

**Median Expression:** Damage (cold at range) + Affliction (Slowed). Frost reaches out, freezes what it touches, and impedes movement.

---

### B-16 — Erosion (Gas + Solid / Air + Earth)

**Phase:** Gas dominant, Solid recessive | **Type:** ⚙ Catalyzed Diagonal | **Profile:** Projecting primary, Static character
*Abrasive dispersal. Stone broken and carried at velocity by wind. The diagonal tension between Air's dispersal and Earth's persistence requires Forge mediation — without it, the stone settles or the wind carries it away entirely. The Static character from the Solid recessive gives the abrasion weight and persistence that pure wind lacks.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Weaken** — Structural degradation at range. Ablative stripping of protection. The primary expression — everything Erosion touches loses structural integrity over time.
- **Damage** — Abrasive damage. Particulate impact at velocity. Sandblasting. Not as immediately devastating as fire or lightning, but relentless.
- **Affliction** — Blindness from abrasive particulate. Skin abrasion. Respiratory distress from inhaled grit. Dehydration through moisture stripping.
- **Environment** — Erosion field as persistent area effect. Long-term structural degradation of buildings, walls, terrain features. Desert creation.
- **Concealment** — Particulate cloud reducing visibility. Sand-screen. Dust veil.
- **Senses (negative)** — Sensory interference through particulate density. Tracking disrupted. Observation impaired. Acoustic masking from constant abrasive hiss.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing, Protection (Erosion is definitionally anti-protection — it strips defenses), Create (Erosion destroys structure, doesn't build it), Luck Control, Teleport, Flight, Growth.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Abrasive surface. Minor structural wear on prolonged contact. Camouflage benefit in arid environments.
- Stage 5–7: Active erosion field. Soak loss on objects in sustained proximity. Armor degradation over extended encounters.
- Stage 8–10: Violent dispersal. Rapid structural failure of exposed materials. Area ablation stripping protection from everything in radius.

**Median Expression:** Weaken (erosion). Erosion strips away protection and structure, patiently and at range.

---

## BORDERLANDS SUMMARY — Effect Coverage

**Effects the Borderlands EXCELS at:** Damage (physical/elemental — the widest damage type coverage of any plane), Protection (physical — B-09 Bedrock is THE structural defense), Environment (terrain and atmospheric — the Borderlands IS the physical environment), Affliction (physical conditions — burning, freezing, restraining, blinding), Create (physical objects), Move Object (physical force), Speed/Movement (physical enhancement), Weaken (structural degradation)

**Effects the Borderlands CAN produce with effort:** Concealment (through Vapor, Dust, Frost — physical obscurement), Senses (through Scorch light, Current detection — physical perception), Transform (through Acid dissolution, fire processing — physical conversion), Flight (through Void Sky at high Stage), Nullify (through Vapor jamming, Acid substrate dissolution — indirect)

**Effects the Borderlands CANNOT produce:** Healing (biological repair is Font territory), Regeneration, Panacea, Illusion (false perception is Palimpsest), Luck Control (probability is Palimpsest), Mind Reading (cognitive access crosses planes), Communication (information transmission is Forge), Teleport (location selection is Palimpsest), Dimensional Travel, Time Travel, Immortality (soul management is Synod), Summon (calling through infrastructure is Forge), Comprehend (pattern recognition is Forge)

---

*End of Borderlands Isotope Reference. Next: The Palimpsest.*

# ÆTHERIUM CODEX — Isotope Effect Reference
## The Palimpsest: The Arbiter

*Phase Wheel: Echo/Resolved (Solid) / Veil/Handed (Liquid) / Probability/Contested (Gas) / Glitch/Suspended (Plasma)*
*Profile derivation: Echo=Static, Veil=Cycling, Probability=Projecting, Glitch=Absorbing*
*Diagonal pairs: Echo↔Probability (Resolved↔Contested), Veil↔Glitch (Handed↔Suspended)*
*Formation Stage Bands: 1-4 (Minor / NFPA 0-1), 5-7 (Moderate / NFPA 2-3), 8-10 (Intense / NFPA 3-4)*

*The Palimpsest produces no physical effects on its own. Every physically impactful Palimpsest expression requires a secondary plane to give the probability a body. What the Palimpsest does is determine WHICH of the possible outcomes actually occurs.*

---

### P-01 — The Loop (Solid Pure / Echo)

**Phase:** Solid | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Static
*A moment determined to repeat. The fixed outcome that cycles back to itself. Not a time loop — a probability state that has Resolved to "this outcome will occur again." Static because the Loop doesn't adapt, respond, or negotiate. It simply IS and will be again. The most absolute expression of Resolved fate.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Immunity** — THE fate-lock expression. A specified outcome is guaranteed for the next triggering event. No variance roll. The result is predetermined. At low Stage, a single guaranteed hit or defense check. At high Stage, an entire event sequence fixed in advance.
- **Affliction** — Forced repetition. The target is compelled to repeat an action, relive an experience, return to a location. The curse of the Loop. The Groundhog Day expressed as a condition.
- **Nullify** — Counter-probability manipulation. The outcome is already Resolved — attempts to shift probability bounce off because there's nothing left to shift. Anti-Luck Control.
- **Feature** — Absolute reliability. The item performs exactly as specified every time. No variance. No critical success, no critical failure. Take average on all dice. Perfectly predictable output.
- **Protection** — At specialized application, fated defense. The Loop has Resolved that the target is not harmed by the next triggering event — so they aren't. Not physical resistance; narrative immunity.
- **Senses** — At specialized application, reading a Resolved outcome. The Loop already knows what happens next because it's already happened. Precognition through repetition rather than prediction.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing (the Loop doesn't repair — it repeats), Damage (the Loop doesn't cause harm — it fixes outcomes), Growth, Speed, Teleport, Create, Move Object, Transform, Concealment (the Loop asserts itself — it demands to be recognized).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Minor fate-fixing. A single die roll predetermined. Reliability enhancement on one specific function.
- Stage 5–7: Active outcome guarantee. A full action guaranteed to succeed or fail as specified. Curse-level repetition compulsion on targets.
- Stage 8–10: Sequence-level fate lock. An entire chain of events fixed. The target lives the same moment over and over. Local reality repeating.

**Median Expression:** Immunity (fate-lock) + Feature (reliability). The Loop guarantees specific outcomes.

---

### P-02 — The Haunt (Solid + Liquid / Echo + Veil)

**Phase:** Solid dominant, Liquid recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Static primary, Cycling character
*The historical overlay. The past visible in the present. Two descriptions of the same space — what it is now and what it was then — simultaneously true and perceivable. NOT the same as S-09 Wraith (Synod Spectral persistence). The Palimpsest Haunt is about perceiving a fixed past through the lens of the present. Static (the historical record is Resolved, permanent, unchangeable) with Cycling (the Veil converting between the two descriptions, allowing the observer to perceive either or both).*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Illusion** — Historical overlay. The space shows what it was alongside what it is. Not a false image — a TRUE image from a different time visible in the current moment. THE "see the past" expression.
- **Senses** — Perceiving historical events in a location. Psychic archaeology. Reading what happened here by witnessing it replayed in spectral overlay. Investigation through temporal perception.
- **Affliction** — Temporal disorientation. The target cannot distinguish past from present. They interact with furniture that no longer exists. They dodge attacks from wars that ended centuries ago. They respond to people who died generations back.
- **Feature** — Dual-timeline perception. The ability to see both the current and historical version of a space simultaneously. Invaluable for investigation, archaeology, and navigation through changed terrain.
- **Communication** — At specialized application, the historical figures in the overlay respond to contextual prompts from their original event. Not conscious — they're recordings. But the recording responds as if the original event were still occurring.
- **Environment** — At high Stage, the historical overlay becomes partially tangible. The old building's walls provide actual cover. The historical fire produces actual heat. The past becomes locally real alongside the present.

**Hard Exclusions:** Damage (direct), Healing, Growth, Speed, Teleport, Luck Control, Protection (physical), Create (new objects — the Haunt only shows what was), Move Object.

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Faint impressions. Emotional residue of past events. Vague visual shimmer of historical conditions. "Something happened here."
- Stage 5–7: Full historical overlay. Past events visible in detail. Temporal disorientation becomes a combat hazard. Investigation-grade historical perception.
- Stage 8–10: The past reasserts physically. Historical objects are tangible. Historical conditions (temperature, weather, light) manifest locally. Two timelines coexist as equally real.

**Median Expression:** Illusion (historical overlay) + Senses (past-reading). The Haunt shows you what happened here.

---

### P-03 — Nostalgia (Solid + Gas / Echo + Probability)

**Phase:** Solid dominant, Gas recessive | **Type:** ⚙ Catalyzed Diagonal | **Profile:** Static primary, Projecting character
*The past as the primary experience. Not an overlay (that's Haunt) — a replacement. The Golden Age made locally real. The historical version of reality asserted as MORE REAL than the present. Requires Forge mediation because Resolved (the past is fixed) and Contested (current perception is open) are antithetical — Nostalgia holds both by making the fixed past dominate the open present.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Illusion** — The past as dominant perception. Current reality becomes the secondary experience — still accessible but requiring effort to perceive. The world looks, sounds, and feels like it did in better times. The primary expression.
- **Affliction** — Temporal fixation. The target becomes so immersed in the historical version that they lose track of present conditions. They make decisions based on conditions that no longer exist. Strategic blindness through beautiful memory.
- **Senses** — Historical perception given priority over current perception. Useful for research but dangerous in combat — you see terrain, weather, and conditions as they were, not as they are.
- **Environment** — At high Stage, historical environmental conditions partially reassert. Temperature, light, atmosphere, and seasonal conditions shift toward historical norms. Not fully tangible (that's Haunt at high Stage) but environmental context changes.
- **Feature** — Memorial preservation. Places and objects under Nostalgia expression resist change because the probability space favors the historical version. Monuments that never weather. Ruins that look freshly built.
- **Weaken** — Degrading the target's engagement with current reality. They become less effective in the present because their awareness is anchored in the past. Reduced initiative. Delayed reactions. The world has moved on but they haven't.

**Hard Exclusions:** Damage, Healing, Growth, Speed, Teleport, Create, Protection (physical), Move Object, Enhanced Trait, Communication (Nostalgia isolates the target in the past — it doesn't connect them to others).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Mild nostalgic impression. The area feels like a better time. Minor emotional warmth. Faint historical aesthetic.
- Stage 5–7: Active temporal displacement. Present conditions require effort to perceive. Historical version dominant. Tactical impairment from outdated perception.
- Stage 8–10: The past overwhelms the present. Targets in the area operate entirely within historical conditions. Only deliberate resistance allows present-awareness. The Golden Age made local and dominant.

**Median Expression:** Illusion (past as primary). Nostalgia makes the past more real than the present.

---

### P-04 — Corrupt (Solid + Plasma / Echo + Glitch)

**Phase:** Solid dominant, Plasma recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Static primary, Absorbing character
*A fixed record losing resolution. The degrading memory. The photograph fading. The outcome is still Resolved but the CONTENT of that resolution is becoming uncertain — the signal is being eaten by noise. Static (the record exists permanently) with Absorbing (the Glitch consuming the clarity and fidelity of that record). Not erasure — degradation. The result is unreliable information that the reader doesn't know is unreliable.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Nullify** — Record degradation. THE anti-divination expression through signal corruption. Fate-reading targeting the Corrupt field receives degraded, unreliable information. Not blocked (that's Concealment) — corrupted. Worse than blocked because the reader doesn't know which parts are wrong.
- **Weaken** — Fidelity loss. Reliability degradation. Things that were certain become approximate. Precise measurements become estimates. Detailed plans become vague impressions. Competence erodes as the knowledge supporting it degrades.
- **Affliction** — Memory degradation in targets. Not deletion (that's S-16 Amnesia) — loss of clarity. The target remembers something happened but can't recall specifics. The emotion remains; the details dissolve.
- **Concealment** — Hiding through degraded signal. The target is still present in the probability space but their signal has too much noise to locate precisely. Palimpsest Dust's anti-divination property IS this expression — dead alternatives flooding the read with noise.
- **Feature** — Signal noise generation. The area passively produces corrupted probability reads. Divination in the area is unreliable not because it's blocked but because results are degraded. The passive anti-scrying field.
- **Senses (negative)** — Degraded perception of fate, probability, and magical signatures. The reader gets information but can't trust its accuracy. False confidence in corrupted data.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing, Damage (direct), Growth, Speed, Teleport, Create, Protection, Enhanced Trait, Communication (corrupted information can't be reliably communicated).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Minor signal degradation. Divination results slightly fuzzy. Historical records seem vaguely unreliable. Background noise.
- Stage 5–7: Active corruption. Divination targeting the area is meaningfully degraded. Memory clarity erodes on exposure. Information from the area cannot be fully trusted.
- Stage 8–10: Total signal corruption. Divination returns plausible but fundamentally unreliable results. Extended exposure degrades the target's own memory fidelity. Palimpsest Dust at concentration produces this effect as environmental hazard.

**Median Expression:** Nullify (signal corruption) + Concealment (noise-hiding). Corrupt makes information unreliable.

---

### P-05 — Dream (Liquid Pure / Veil)

**Phase:** Liquid | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Cycling
*Surrealism. Logic is fluid and narrative. The selected version of reality operates on different rules than objective physics. Not an illusion (implying falsehood) — a genuine alternative description where narrative logic replaces physical logic. Cycling because the Veil continuously converts between descriptions, processing input and producing output according to story rules rather than physics rules.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Illusion** — Narrative-logic reality. Items and environments function according to what they SHOULD do rather than what physics says they CAN do. THE most coherent expression of narrative override without the Fey's conviction requirement.
- **Feature** — Dramatic appropriateness. The item or effect responds to narrative context. Things work when they should work and fail when failure would be meaningful. Not random — story-shaped. Not player-controlled — GM-interpreted.
- **Senses** — Perception through narrative lens. The Dream reveals what matters to the story rather than what physically exists. Clues become visible. Red herrings fade. The important door is the one that glows.
- **Concealment** — Narrative irrelevance as invisibility. Things that don't matter to the current narrative become imperceptible. Background characters fade. Unimportant details become impossible to notice.
- **Affliction** — Reality dissociation. The target cannot distinguish Dream logic from physical logic. They try to fly because it makes narrative sense and fall because physics disagrees. They trust the friendly stranger because the story says to.
- **Communication** — Narrative-level information exchange. The Dream communicates through symbols, omens, thematic resonance, and dramatic irony rather than language. You understand because the story requires you to understand.
- **Environment** — At high Stage, the Dream reshapes the physical environment to match narrative expectations. The haunted house BECOMES haunted. The dark forest BECOMES dangerous. The safe haven BECOMES safe. Reality follows the script.

**Hard Exclusions:** Damage (direct physical — the Dream doesn't strike), Protection (physical), Create (physical objects from nothing), Move Object (physical force), Growth (biological), Speed (physical acceleration), Enhanced Trait (physical capability).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Mild narrative influence. Things feel meaningful. Coincidences align slightly. Dreams are vivid and symbolically coherent.
- Stage 5–7: Active narrative logic. Items begin behaving according to what they should do. Environment responds to dramatic expectations. NPCs act in character.
- Stage 8–10: Full narrative override. Physical law becomes secondary to story logic within the Dream field. The environment, conditions, and events follow dramatic structure rather than physics. The GM is literally writing reality within the field.

**Median Expression:** Illusion (narrative logic) + Feature (dramatic appropriateness). Dream makes reality follow story rules.

---

### P-06 — Gambit (Liquid + Gas / Veil + Probability)

**Phase:** Liquid dominant, Gas recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Cycling primary, Projecting character
*Illusion as weapon. Deception where the truth of which version is real remains genuinely Contested. The heist movie. The con. The bluff that works because the Palimpsest itself hasn't decided which version is true yet. Cycling (the Veil converting between presented versions) with Projecting (the Probability broadcasting multiple possible truths outward). The mark cannot detect the lie because it might not BE a lie yet.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Illusion** — Active deception. THE "which one is real?" expression. Not a simple false image — a presented version where the truth remains genuinely undetermined until the Gambit resolves.
- **Concealment** — Uncertain identity. The target's true nature hidden behind a Gambit that keeps "is this real?" genuinely Contested. Not invisible — indeterminate.
- **Affliction** — Decision paralysis. The target cannot determine which version to respond to. Action delayed by genuine uncertainty. Every option might be the wrong one because the truth hasn't been selected yet.
- **Senses (negative)** — Truth-detection suppressed. Divination, lie detection, insight checks — all return "genuinely uncertain" because the Palimpsest hasn't Resolved which description is primary. You can't detect a lie that might still become truth.
- **Feature** — Perfect poker face. The user's true intent is hidden behind a Gambit field. Enhanced social combat through genuine indeterminacy rather than skilled deception.
- **Communication** — Misleading information that the target cannot distinguish from truth because it might still BE truth. The difference between lying and gambling.
- **Luck Control** — At specialized application, the Gambit can be Resolved at the moment of the user's choosing. The con pays off when the con artist decides which version the Palimpsest selects as primary.

**Hard Exclusions:** Damage (direct), Healing, Protection (physical), Create, Growth, Speed, Teleport, Move Object, Enhanced Trait (physical).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Minor misdirection. The presented version is plausible but detectable with effort. Basic social deception enhancement.
- Stage 5–7: Active Gambit. Truth-detection fails because the truth is genuinely undetermined. Multiple versions sustained simultaneously. The con is running.
- Stage 8–10: Total indeterminacy. Reality itself hasn't decided which version is true. The Gambit encompasses entire scenarios — not just "is this the real me" but "is this the real situation." Ocean's Eleven at cosmic scale.

**Median Expression:** Illusion (genuine deception). Gambit presents versions of reality that might all be true.

---

### P-07 — Fey Logic (Liquid + Plasma / Veil + Glitch)

**Phase:** Liquid dominant, Plasma recessive | **Type:** ⚙ Catalyzed Diagonal | **Profile:** Cycling primary, Absorbing character
*Rules work if they are fun. Conviction as a genuine variable in probability. Narrative logic overriding physical law where belief provides the missing force. Requires Forge mediation because Handed (two valid descriptions coexist) and Suspended (the current state cannot sustain itself) are antithetical — Fey Logic is a state where BOTH descriptions are valid AND NEITHER can sustain itself, which is a paradox requiring catalysis to hold open.*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Luck Control** — THE conviction-as-variable expression. Genuine belief shifts genuine probability. A character who truly believes their action will succeed faces mathematically better odds — not because the universe has preferences but because conviction is a real thermodynamic input into the Palimpsest's probability calculation. The Tessera formulation.
- **Feature** — Dramatic logic. Rules operate on narrative appropriateness rather than consistent application. The improbable succeeds when the story demands it. The certain fails when failure would be more interesting. Not random (that's Casino) — dramatically structured.
- **Illusion** — At specialized application, narrative-logic constructs that function because they SHOULD be real. The Fey bridge holds because everyone believes it will. Disbelief collapses it — but so does engineering analysis, which is why Fey construction and Forge construction are incompatible.
- **Affliction** — The Fey bargain. Agreements made under Fey Logic are binding through narrative inevitability, not magical compulsion. Breaking a Fey promise doesn't trigger a curse — it triggers narrative consequence. The universe writes the punishment into the story.
- **Senses** — Intuitive perception. Knowing things without evidence because narrative logic provides the information. "I just know" as a legitimate epistemology within the Fey Logic field.
- **Enhanced Trait** — At specialized application, conviction-enhanced performance. The character succeeds not through physical capability but through narrative weight. The hero's moment. The mother lifting the cart off her child.
- **Nullify** — At specialized application, breaking rigid rules and constraints. If a law or limitation doesn't serve the narrative, Fey Logic erodes it. Locks open for protagonists. Cages fail to contain the righteous. The rules were always going to break for this character at this moment.

**Hard Exclusions:** Create (physical — Fey Logic reshapes perception and probability, not matter), Protection (physical), Healing (biological repair follows biochemistry, not narrative), Damage (direct physical force).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Mild narrative influence. Lucky breaks. Convenient coincidences. Things going slightly better than statistics would predict for sympathetic actors.
- Stage 5–7: Active Fey Logic. Conviction measurably affects outcomes. Dramatic timing becomes reliable. Rules bend for narratively appropriate reasons. The hero's luck is real.
- Stage 8–10: Full narrative override. Physics is secondary to story within the Fey field. Impossible actions succeed if dramatically appropriate. But the inverse is equally true — dramatically inappropriate actions fail regardless of physical capability. The story writes itself.

**Median Expression:** Luck Control (conviction-driven). Fey Logic makes belief a genuine force.

---

### P-08 — Horror (Liquid + Solid / Veil + Echo)

**Phase:** Liquid dominant, Solid recessive | **Type:** ◆ Natural | **Profile:** Cycling primary, Static character
*The mask slips. The comfortable version of reality is Suspended while the underlying truth breaks through. Eldritch revelation. Two descriptions exist: the safe version and the real version. The safe version is losing. The real version is asserting. Cycling (the Veil converting between descriptions) with Static (the Echo/Resolved truth is fixed and permanent — it was ALWAYS true, and the comfortable version was always temporary).*

**Natural Effects:**
- **Affliction** — The revelation. THE "you learn something you cannot unlearn" expression. The comfortable description of reality is replaced by the true one, and the true one is worse. Sanity damage. Existential dread. The Horror is not the monster — the Horror is discovering the monster was always there.
- **Senses** — Forced truth perception. The Horror strips comfortable interpretations and shows what IS. Not illusion-breaking (that's Simulation P-16) — truth-FORCING. The difference: Simulation reveals construction. Horror reveals the thing the construction was hiding.
- **Weaken** — Psychological defense erosion. The certainties that sustain normal function dissolve. Assumptions fail. Worldviews crack. The target's mental model of reality is degraded toward the true model, and the true model is less functional for daily life.
- **Illusion (inverse)** — The comfortable illusion FAILING. Horror doesn't create a false image — it destroys one. The wallpaper peels. The mask cracks. The pretense collapses. The smile was always hiding teeth.
- **Environment** — At high Stage, the physical environment reflects the underlying truth. The walls show what's behind them. The ground reveals what's buried. The sky shows what's watching. The Smoothing Effect's failure mode — the Great Lie showing its seams.
- **Nullify** — At specialized application, breaking morale effects, comfort enchantments, and false confidence. If a spell or effect maintains psychological stability through pleasant illusion, Horror strips it.
- **Feature** — Unflinching perception at willingly applied low Stage. Immunity to surprise — you've already seen the worst, so nothing shocks you. Professional tool for investigators, soldiers, and Chirurgeons who need to see clearly regardless of what they're looking at.

**Hard Exclusions:** Healing, Create, Growth, Speed, Teleport, Protection (Horror reveals how inadequate protection is), Enhanced Trait (positive — Horror diminishes confidence, not enhances capability).

**Formation Stage Shift:**
- Stage 1–4: Unease. Something is wrong but you can't identify what. Mild discomfort. The sense that the world is not quite what it presents.
- Stage 5–7: Active revelation. The comfortable version visibly failing. Cracks in the presentation. The truth partially visible. Mental defense checks required.
- Stage 8–10: Full eldritch exposure. The underlying truth is completely visible and completely inescapable. The comfortable version has collapsed entirely. All observers must make Mental defense checks or suffer lasting psychological conditions. The Smoothing Effect's narrative is locally visible as a narrative rather than experienced as reality.

**Median Expression:** Affliction (revelation) + Senses (forced truth). Horror shows you what was always behind the curtain.
